=== caption.js
// Trend-style caption: the lyric clause in heavy lowercase type, revealed word by word in
// sync, on rounded pills (one per row) or with an outline. The whole clause is laid out up
// front so rows never reflow as words appear. Options:
//   slam:    ['crying']           words that slam in (big -> 1x with overshoot)
//   sticker: { word: 'again', color, size, rot }   a word pulled out of the rows and slammed
//                                  on as its own sticker under the caption
//   backing: words in parentheses render as a smaller second line under the caption
//   drip:    0..1                  drips run down from the pills (bath)

import { clamp, ease, hash } from './util.js';
import { font, rrect } from './text.js';
import { rgba } from './theme.js';

export const CAPTION_DEFAULTS = {
  x: 540, y: 560, maxW: 900, size: 86, minSize: 54, lineH: 1.16,
  family: '"Inter", sans-serif', weight: 900, lowercase: true,
  style: 'pill',
  colors: { text: '#ffffff', pill: '#000000', outline: '#000000', accent: '#ff2a2a', backing: '#ffffff' },
  pillAlpha: 0.9, padX: 26, padY: 14, radius: 24,
  reveal: 'word', pop: 0.12, popTime: 0.09,
  slam: [], slamScale: 1.9, slamTime: 0.16,
  sticker: null,
  backingSize: 0.62,
  drip: 0,
  alpha: 1,
};

/** Display form of a lyric word: lowercase, parentheses dropped; `last` also drops a
 *  trailing , . ; (the caption's final full stop), keeping ellipses and quotes. */
export function captionWord(w, lowercase = true, last = false) {
  let s = String(w);
  if (last && !/\.\.\.["']?$|…$/.test(s)) s = s.replace(/[.,;]+(["']?)$/, '$1');
  s = s.replace(/^\(|\)$/g, '');
  return lowercase ? s.toLowerCase() : s;
}

const isMatch = (w, m) => captionWord(w.w).replace(/[^a-z0-9:']/g, '') === String(m).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9:']/g, '');

function layout(ctx, words, o) {
  let size = o.size;
  for (let pass = 0; pass < 6; pass++) {
    ctx.font = font(o.weight, size, o.family);
    const space = Math.max(ctx.measureText(' ').width, (o.wordGap ?? 0.3) * size);
    const ws = words.map((w) => ctx.measureText(w.text).width);
    // greedy wrap
    const rows = [];
    let cur = [], curW = 0;
    words.forEach((w, k) => {
      const add = (cur.length ? space : 0) + ws[k];
      if (cur.length && curW + add > o.maxW) { rows.push(cur); cur = [k]; curW = ws[k]; }
      else { cur.push(k); curW += add; }
    });
    if (cur.length) rows.push(cur);
    const widest = Math.max(...ws);
    if ((rows.length <= 3 && widest <= o.maxW) || size <= o.minSize) {
      // balance: move words down so the last row is not a lone orphan
      if (rows.length >= 2 && rows.at(-1).length === 1 && rows.at(-2).length > 2) rows.at(-1).unshift(rows.at(-2).pop());
      const rowW = rows.map((r) => r.reduce((a, k) => a + ws[k], 0) + space * (r.length - 1));
      return { size, space, ws, rows, rowW };
    }
    size = Math.max(o.minSize, size * 0.88);
  }
}

/**
 * Draw the caption for `words` (lyric words of one clause, with timings) at time t.
 * Returns the block's bounding box (design px) or null when nothing is revealed yet.
 */
export function drawCaption(ctx, t, words, opts = {}) {
  const o = { ...CAPTION_DEFAULTS, ...opts, colors: { ...CAPTION_DEFAULTS.colors, ...(opts.colors || {}) } };
  if (!words || !words.length || o.alpha <= 0) return null;
  const lastMain = words.reduce((acc, w, k) => (w.backing ? acc : k), -1);
  const all = words.map((w, k) => ({ ...w, text: captionWord(w.w, o.lowercase, k === lastMain || w.backing) })).filter((w) => w.text.length);
  let sticker = null;
  if (o.sticker) {
    const k = all.findIndex((w) => isMatch(w, o.sticker.word));
    if (k >= 0) sticker = all.splice(k, 1)[0];
  }
  const main = all.filter((w) => !w.backing);
  const backing = all.filter((w) => w.backing);
  if (!main.length) return null;
  const shown = (w) => (o.reveal === 'all' ? 1 : t >= w.start ? 1 : 0);
  if (!main.some((w) => shown(w))) return null;

  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= o.alpha;
  const L = layout(ctx, main, o);
  const rowH = L.size * o.lineH;
  const blockH = rowH * L.rows.length;
  const top = o.y - blockH / 2;
  const box = { x: o.x - Math.max(...L.rowW) / 2 - o.padX, y: top - o.padY, w: Math.max(...L.rowW) + o.padX * 2, h: blockH + o.padY * 2 };
  ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  const slamSet = new Set((o.slam || []).map((m) => String(m).toLowerCase()));

  L.rows.forEach((row, r) => {
    const x0 = o.x - L.rowW[r] / 2;
    const yTop = top + r * rowH;
    const base = yTop + rowH * 0.5 + L.size * 0.36;
    // positions
    const xs = [];
    let x = x0;
    for (const k of row) { xs.push(x); x += L.ws[k] + L.space; }
    const lastShown = row.reduce((acc, k, j) => (shown(main[k]) ? j : acc), -1);
    if (lastShown < 0) return;
    // pill
    if (o.style === 'pill') {
      const pw = xs[lastShown] + L.ws[row[lastShown]] - x0;
      ctx.fillStyle = rgba(o.colors.pill, o.pillAlpha);
      rrect(ctx, x0 - o.padX, yTop - o.padY * 0.35, pw + o.padX * 2, rowH + o.padY * 0.7, o.radius);
      ctx.fill();
      if (o.drip > 0) {
        for (let d = 0; d < 5; d++) {
          const hx = x0 + hash(d + r * 13, 91) * pw;
          const len = o.drip * (40 + 120 * hash(d + r * 13, 92)) * L.size / 86;
          const wd = (10 + 10 * hash(d + r * 13, 93)) * L.size / 86;
          const yb = yTop + rowH + o.padY * 0.3;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(hx - wd, yb - 4);
          ctx.lineTo(hx - wd * 0.6, yb + len - wd);
          ctx.arc(hx, yb + len - wd, wd * 0.6, Math.PI, 0, true);
          ctx.lineTo(hx + wd, yb - 4);
          ctx.closePath();
          ctx.fill();
        }
      }
    }
    row.forEach((k, j) => {
      const w = main[k];
      if (!shown(w)) return;
      const dt = t - w.start;
      const slam = slamSet.has(w.text.replace(/[^a-z0-9:']/g, ''));
      let sc = 1;
      if (o.reveal === 'word') {
        if (slam) sc = 1 + (o.slamScale - 1) * (1 - ease.outBack(clamp(dt / o.slamTime), 2.2));
        else sc = 1 + o.pop * Math.exp(-Math.max(0, dt) / o.popTime);
      }
      ctx.save();
      const cx = xs[j] + L.ws[k] / 2, cy = base - L.size * 0.33;
      if (sc !== 1) { ctx.translate(cx, cy); ctx.scale(sc, sc); ctx.translate(-cx, -cy); }
      ctx.font = font(o.weight, L.size, o.family);
      if (o.style === 'outline') {
        ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
        ctx.lineWidth = L.size * 0.16;
        ctx.strokeStyle = o.colors.outline;
        ctx.strokeText(w.text, xs[j], base);
      }
      ctx.fillStyle = slam && o.colors.slam ? o.colors.slam : o.colors.text;
      ctx.fillText(w.text, xs[j], base);
      ctx.restore();
    });
  });

  let yNext = top + blockH + o.padY;
  // backing vocals (stutter ad-libs) as a smaller line under the block
  const bShown = backing.filter((w) => shown(w));
  if (bShown.length) {
    const bs = L.size * o.backingSize;
    ctx.font = font(o.weight, bs, o.family, 'italic');
    const txt = bShown.map((w) => w.text).join(' ');
    const tw = ctx.measureText(txt).width;
    const dt = t - bShown[0].start;
    const sc = 1 + 0.25 * Math.exp(-Math.max(0, dt) / 0.08);
    const by = yNext + bs * 1.1;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(o.x, by - bs * 0.33); ctx.scale(sc, sc); ctx.translate(-o.x, -(by - bs * 0.33));
    if (o.style === 'pill') {
      ctx.fillStyle = rgba(o.colors.pill, o.pillAlpha * 0.75);
      rrect(ctx, o.x - tw / 2 - o.padX * 0.7, by - bs * 0.95, tw + o.padX * 1.4, bs * 1.3, o.radius * 0.7);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = o.colors.backing;
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText(txt, o.x, by);
    ctx.restore();
    yNext = by + bs * 0.5;
  }
  // sticker slam ("again")
  if (sticker && t >= sticker.start) {
    const s = o.sticker;
    const ss = (s.size || 1.6) * L.size;
    const dt = t - sticker.start;
    const k = clamp(dt / 0.14);
    const sc = 1 + 1.8 * (1 - ease.outBack(k, 2.6));
    const rot = (s.rot ?? -0.1) * (1 + 0.6 * (1 - k));
    ctx.font = font(400, ss, s.family || '"Archivo Black", sans-serif');
    const tw = ctx.measureText(sticker.text).width;
    const sy = yNext + ss * 0.85;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(o.x + (s.dx || 0), sy);
    ctx.rotate(rot);
    ctx.scale(sc, sc);
    ctx.fillStyle = s.pill || o.colors.accent;
    rrect(ctx, -tw / 2 - ss * 0.28, -ss * 0.72, tw + ss * 0.56, ss * 1.0, ss * 0.22);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = s.color || '#ffffff';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText(sticker.text, 0, ss * 0.1);
    ctx.restore();
    box.h = sy + ss * 0.4 - box.y;
  }
  ctx.restore();
  return box;
}
=== karaoke.js
// Karaoke band: the current lyric line at the bottom of frame, per-word highlight with a
// left-to-right wipe through the active word (per-syllable when `syl` is present).
// Title-safe by default (inside the central 80% of width, above the bottom 10%).

import { clamp } from './util.js';
import { font, rrect } from './text.js';
import { rgba } from './theme.js';

export const KARAOKE_DEFAULTS = {
  y: 930,            // baseline of the last row, design px (1080-tall frame)
  maxW: 1500,
  size: 46,
  minSize: 32,
  rowGap: 1.18,      // row height in em
  weight: 800,
  family: '"Inter", sans-serif',
  backingFamily: null, // defaults to family
  colors: { unsung: '#5a5e66', sung: '#c9ccd1', active: '#ffffff', backing: '#8a8e96', bg: '#0b0b0e' },
  bgAlpha: 0.62,
  padX: 34, padY: 18, radius: 18,
  lead: 0.28, hold: 0.5, fade: 0.14,
  pop: 0.035,
};

function wordProgress(w, t) {
  if (t <= w.start) return 0;
  if (t >= w.end) return 1;
  if (Array.isArray(w.syl) && w.syl.length > 1) {
    const n = w.syl.length;
    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      const [a, b] = w.syl[k];
      if (t < a) return k / n;
      if (t < b) return (k + (t - a) / Math.max(1e-3, b - a)) / n;
    }
    return 1;
  }
  return (t - w.start) / Math.max(1e-3, w.end - w.start);
}

/** Lay out a line's words into 1-2 centered rows. Returns { rows, size, width, height }. */
export function layoutLine(ctx, line, o) {
  const words = line.words;
  let size = o.size;
  const measureAll = (s) => {
    ctx.font = font(o.weight, s, o.family);
    const space = ctx.measureText(' ').width;
    const ws = words.map((w) => {
      ctx.font = w.backing ? font(o.weight - 200, s, o.backingFamily || o.family, 'italic') : font(o.weight, s, o.family);
      return ctx.measureText(w.w).width;
    });
    return { ws, space };
  };
  let m = measureAll(size);
  let total = m.ws.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) + m.space * (words.length - 1);
  if (total > o.maxW) {
    size = Math.max(o.minSize, (size * o.maxW) / total);
    m = measureAll(size);
    total = m.ws.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) + m.space * (words.length - 1);
  }
  let rows;
  if (total <= o.maxW) rows = [words.map((w, k) => k)];
  else {
    // balanced 2-row split
    let best = 1, bestDiff = Infinity, acc = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < words.length - 1; k++) {
      acc += m.ws[k] + (k ? m.space : 0);
      const diff = Math.abs(acc - (total - acc - m.space));
      if (diff < bestDiff) { bestDiff = diff; best = k + 1; }
    }
    rows = [words.slice(0, best).map((_, k) => k), words.slice(best).map((_, k) => k + best)];
  }
  const rowW = rows.map((r) => r.reduce((a, k) => a + m.ws[k], 0) + m.space * (r.length - 1));
  return { rows, size, ws: m.ws, space: m.space, rowW, width: Math.max(...rowW), height: rows.length * size * o.rowGap };
}

/**
 * Draw the band. `lyrics` is the makeLyrics() object. Returns the band box or null.
 * opts.mode: 'full' | 'dim' | 'off'; opts.alpha multiplies everything.
 */
export function drawKaraoke(ctx, t, lyrics, opts = {}) {
  const o = { ...KARAOKE_DEFAULTS, ...opts, colors: { ...KARAOKE_DEFAULTS.colors, ...(opts.colors || {}) } };
  if (o.mode === 'off') return null;
  const line = lyrics.displayLine(t, o.lead, o.hold);
  if (!line) return null;
  const W = o.W || 1920;
  const fadeIn = clamp((t - (line.start - o.lead)) / o.fade);
  const fadeOut = clamp((line.end + o.hold - t) / o.fade);
  const alpha = Math.min(fadeIn, fadeOut) * (o.alpha ?? 1) * (o.mode === 'dim' ? 0.55 : 1);
  if (alpha <= 0.001) return null;

  ctx.save();
  const L = layoutLine(ctx, line, o);
  const rowH = L.size * o.rowGap;
  const top = o.y - L.size * 0.78 - (L.rows.length - 1) * rowH;
  const box = { x: W / 2 - L.width / 2 - o.padX, y: top - o.padY, w: L.width + o.padX * 2, h: L.size * 0.78 + (L.rows.length - 1) * rowH + L.size * 0.24 + o.padY * 2 };
  if (o.bgAlpha > 0) {
    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(o.colors.bg, o.bgAlpha);
    rrect(ctx, box.x, box.y, box.w, box.h, o.radius);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  L.rows.forEach((row, r) => {
    let x = W / 2 - L.rowW[r] / 2;
    const y = o.y - (L.rows.length - 1 - r) * rowH;
    for (const k of row) {
      const w = line.words[k];
      const p = wordProgress(w, t);
      const ww = L.ws[k];
      const f = w.backing ? font(o.weight - 200, L.size, o.backingFamily || o.family, 'italic') : font(o.weight, L.size, o.family);
      ctx.font = f;
      const active = p > 0 && p < 1;
      const popK = active ? 1 + o.pop * Math.exp(-(t - w.start) / 0.12) : 1;
      ctx.save();
      if (popK !== 1) { ctx.translate(x + ww / 2, y); ctx.scale(popK, popK); ctx.translate(-(x + ww / 2), -y); }
      const unsung = w.backing ? o.colors.backing : o.colors.unsung;
      const sung = w.backing ? o.colors.backing : o.colors.sung;
      ctx.globalAlpha = alpha * (w.backing && p === 0 ? 0.6 : 1);
      ctx.fillStyle = p >= 1 ? sung : unsung;
      ctx.fillText(w.w, x, y);
      if (active) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.rect(x - 2, y - L.size, (ww + 4) * p, L.size * 1.4);
        ctx.clip();
        ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
        ctx.fillStyle = w.backing ? o.colors.sung : o.colors.active;
        ctx.fillText(w.w, x, y);
        ctx.restore();
      }
      ctx.restore();
      x += ww + L.space;
    }
  });
  ctx.restore();
  return box;
}
=== hud.js
// Generic HUD drawing helpers (song code composes them into its own HUD).

import { clamp } from './util.js';
import { rrect } from './text.js';
import { rgba } from './theme.js';

/** Minutes since midnight -> "02:00 AM" (12-hour) or "02:00" (24-hour). */
export function clockText(minutes, { h24 = false, ampm = true } = {}) {
  let m = Math.floor(((minutes % 1440) + 1440) % 1440);
  const hh = Math.floor(m / 60), mm = m % 60;
  if (h24) return `${String(hh).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(mm).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  const h12 = hh % 12 === 0 ? 12 : hh % 12;
  return `${String(h12).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(mm).padStart(2, '0')}${ampm ? (hh < 12 ? ' AM' : ' PM') : ''}`;
}

export function drawBattery(ctx, x, y, w, h, level, color, alpha = 1) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.fillStyle = color;
  ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1.5, h * 0.12);
  rrect(ctx, x, y, w, h, h * 0.18);
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillRect(x + w + h * 0.06, y + h * 0.3, h * 0.14, h * 0.4);
  const pad = h * 0.2;
  ctx.fillRect(x + pad, y + pad, (w - pad * 2) * clamp(level), h - pad * 2);
  ctx.restore();
}

export function drawSignal(ctx, x, y, h, level, color, dimColor, n = 4) {
  const bw = h * 0.22, gap = h * 0.12;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const bh = h * (0.3 + (0.7 * (i + 1)) / n);
    ctx.fillStyle = i < Math.round(level * n) ? color : dimColor;
    ctx.fillRect(x + i * (bw + gap), y + h - bh, bw, bh);
  }
}

export function drawDot(ctx, x, y, r, color, alpha = 1) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  ctx.fillStyle = color;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
}

/** Thin progress bar with a track. */
export function drawBar(ctx, x, y, w, h, frac, fg, track, alpha = 1) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  ctx.fillStyle = track;
  ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
  ctx.fillStyle = fg;
  ctx.fillRect(x, y, w * clamp(frac), h);
  ctx.restore();
}

/** Corner brackets (camera framing marks). */
export function drawBrackets(ctx, x, y, w, h, len, color, lw = 3, alpha = 1) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.lineWidth = lw;
  ctx.beginPath();
  for (const [cx, cy, sx, sy] of [[x, y, 1, 1], [x + w, y, -1, 1], [x, y + h, 1, -1], [x + w, y + h, -1, -1]]) {
    ctx.moveTo(cx + sx * len, cy);
    ctx.lineTo(cx, cy);
    ctx.lineTo(cx, cy + sy * len);
  }
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}

export { rgba };

// Preview page: scrub and play the video against the audio file.
//   Space play/pause · ←/→ one beat (shift: one bar) · ,/. one frame · [/] prev/next plate
//   ?t=23 start time · ?plate=hook&n=2 jump to a plate's hero · ?res=720 canvas height
//   ?layout=16x9 secondary layout (songs that declare layouts)
//   ?audio=<url> override the audio file

import { createEngine } from './core/engine.js';
import { fmtTime } from './core/util.js';

const PLATE_COLORS = ['#4b6584', '#8854d0', '#20bf6b', '#eb3b5a', '#fa8231', '#0fb9b1', '#f7b731', '#a5b1c2', '#3867d6', '#e056fd', '#26de81', '#fd9644'];

async function exists(url) {
  try { const r = await fetch(url, { method: 'HEAD', cache: 'no-store' }); return r.ok; } catch { return false; }
}

export async function startPreview(song, { lyricsJson, audioJson, q, base, missingFonts = [], sheet = null }) {
  const layout = q.get('layout') || song.defaultLayout || null;
  const d = (layout && song.layouts?.[layout]) || song.design || { w: 1920, h: 1080 };
  const portrait = d.h > d.w;
  const res = Number(q.get('res') || (portrait ? 1280 : 720));   // canvas height in px
  const resH = res, resW = Math.round((res * d.w) / d.h);
  const root = document.getElementById('app') || document.body;
  root.innerHTML = `
    <div class="pv-stage"><canvas id="pv-canvas" width="${resW}" height="${resH}"></canvas><pre id="pv-err"></pre></div>
    <div class="pv-bar">
      <button id="pv-play">▶</button>
      <span id="pv-time" class="pv-mono"></span>
      <span id="pv-plate" class="pv-mono"></span>
      <span id="pv-audio" class="pv-mono pv-dim"></span>
    </div>
    <canvas id="pv-scrub" height="44"></canvas>
    <div class="pv-help pv-dim">space play/pause · ←/→ beat (shift: bar) · ,/. frame · [ ] plate · click the strip to seek</div>`;
  const canvas = root.querySelector('#pv-canvas');
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });
  const err = root.querySelector('#pv-err');
  const engine = createEngine({ song, lyricsJson, audioJson, width: resW, height: resH, fps: 60, layout, sheet });
  const { env } = engine;
  const B = env.B;

  // Audio: ?audio=, else the song's candidates in order, first one that exists.
  const audio = new Audio();
  audio.preload = 'auto';
  let audioUrl = q.get('audio');
  if (!audioUrl) {
    for (const c of song.audioCandidates?.(audioJson) || []) {
      const u = new URL(c, base).href;
      if (await exists(u)) { audioUrl = u; break; }
    }
  }
  root.querySelector('#pv-audio').textContent = audioUrl ? `audio: ${decodeURIComponent(new URL(audioUrl, base).pathname.split('/').slice(-3).join('/'))}` : 'audio: none found';
  if (missingFonts.length) root.querySelector('#pv-audio').textContent += `  ·  MISSING FONTS: ${missingFonts.join(', ')}`;
  if (audioUrl) audio.src = audioUrl;

  let t = Number(q.get('t') || 0);
  if (q.get('plate')) {
    const n = q.get('n');
    const w = engine.windows.find((x) => x.plate === q.get('plate') && (n == null || String(x.params.n) === n));
    if (w) t = engine.env.T.heroTime(w);
  }
  let playing = false;
  let lastDrawn = -1;
  const frameDur = 1 / 30;

  const scrub = root.querySelector('#pv-scrub');
  const sctx = scrub.getContext('2d');
  function drawScrub() {
    const wpx = scrub.clientWidth * devicePixelRatio, hpx = 44 * devicePixelRatio;
    if (scrub.width !== wpx) { scrub.width = wpx; scrub.height = hpx; }
    const D = engine.duration;
    sctx.setTransform(devicePixelRatio, 0, 0, devicePixelRatio, 0, 0);
    const Wc = scrub.clientWidth;
    sctx.fillStyle = '#111'; sctx.fillRect(0, 0, Wc, 44);
    for (const s of B.sections) {
      const x0 = (s.start / D) * Wc, x1 = (s.end / D) * Wc;
      sctx.fillStyle = '#2a2a30'; sctx.fillRect(x0, 0, x1 - x0 - 1, 12);
      sctx.fillStyle = '#9a9aa2'; sctx.font = '10px "IBM Plex Mono", monospace'; sctx.fillText(s.name, x0 + 3, 10);
    }
    engine.windows.forEach((w, i) => {
      const x0 = (w.start / D) * Wc, x1 = (w.end / D) * Wc;
      sctx.fillStyle = PLATE_COLORS[i % PLATE_COLORS.length]; sctx.globalAlpha = 0.75;
      sctx.fillRect(x0, 14, x1 - x0 - 1, 30);
      sctx.globalAlpha = 1; sctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; sctx.font = '11px "IBM Plex Mono", monospace';
      if (x1 - x0 > 30) sctx.fillText(w.id, x0 + 3, 33);
    });
    const px = (t / D) * Wc;
    sctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; sctx.fillRect(px - 1, 0, 2, 44);
  }
  const seek = (nt) => {
    t = Math.max(0, Math.min(engine.duration - 1e-3, nt));
    if (audioUrl) audio.currentTime = t;
  };
  let dragging = false;
  const scrubTo = (e) => { const r = scrub.getBoundingClientRect(); seek(((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width) * engine.duration); };
  scrub.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => { dragging = true; scrub.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); scrubTo(e); });
  scrub.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => { if (dragging) scrubTo(e); });
  scrub.addEventListener('pointerup', () => { dragging = false; });

  const playBtn = root.querySelector('#pv-play');
  const setPlaying = (p) => {
    playing = p;
    playBtn.textContent = p ? '❚❚' : '▶';
    if (p) { if (audioUrl) { audio.currentTime = t; audio.play().catch(() => {}); } startWall = performance.now() - t * 1000; }
    else { audio.pause(); const u = new URL(location.href); u.searchParams.set('t', t.toFixed(3)); u.searchParams.delete('plate'); u.searchParams.delete('n'); history.replaceState(null, '', u); }
  };
  let startWall = 0;
  playBtn.addEventListener('click', () => setPlaying(!playing));
  window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.target.tagName === 'INPUT') return;
    const bp = B.beatPos(t);
    if (e.code === 'Space') { e.preventDefault(); setPlaying(!playing); }
    else if (e.code === 'ArrowRight') seek(B.beatTime(Math.floor(bp.beat + 1e-6) + (e.shiftKey ? B.bpb : 1)));
    else if (e.code === 'ArrowLeft') seek(B.beatTime(Math.ceil(bp.beat - 1e-6) - (e.shiftKey ? B.bpb : 1)));
    else if (e.key === '.') seek(t + frameDur);
    else if (e.key === ',') seek(t - frameDur);
    else if (e.key === ']' || e.key === '[') {
      const ws = engine.windows, cur = env.T.windowAt(t);
      const j = Math.max(0, Math.min(ws.length - 1, ws.indexOf(cur) + (e.key === ']' ? 1 : -1)));
      seek(e.key === '[' && t - cur.start > 0.5 ? cur.start : ws[j].start);
    }
  });

  const timeEl = root.querySelector('#pv-time'), plateEl = root.querySelector('#pv-plate');
  function loop() {
    if (playing) {
      t = audioUrl && !audio.paused ? audio.currentTime : (performance.now() - startWall) / 1000;
      if (t >= engine.duration) { t = engine.duration - 1e-3; setPlaying(false); }
    }
    if (t !== lastDrawn) {
      try { engine.render(ctx, t); err.textContent = ''; } catch (e) { err.textContent = String(e.stack || e); }
      lastDrawn = t;
      const bp = B.beatPos(t), w = env.T.windowAt(t), s = B.sectionAt(t);
      timeEl.textContent = `${fmtTime(t)}  bar ${bp.bar + 1}.${bp.beatInBar + 1}`;
      plateEl.textContent = `${w.id}  ·  ${s?.name ?? ''}`;
    }
    drawScrub();
    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  window.__preview = { engine, seek: (x) => seek(x), get t() { return t; } };
  return window.__preview;
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
// Render frames in headless Chrome and encode with ffmpeg.
//
//   node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video [options]
//
//   --layout 9x16|16x9      song layout (default: the song's default)
//   --w 540 --h 960         output size (default: half the layout's design size)
//   --fps 30                frame rate
//   --from 0 --to 10        time range in seconds (default: whole song)
//   --out file.mp4          encode an MP4 (libx264 CRF 16 + AAC from the song's audio)
//   --audio file.wav        audio override (default: picked from data/audio.json "source")
//   --data dir/             timing data dir, relative to the song dir (default data/)
//   --workers 8             parallel Chrome instances (frames are independent)
//   --crf 16 --preset medium
//   --sheet file.png        contact sheet: one cell per timeline window (+ song extras)
//   --sheet-mode clips|even --count 24 --cols 10 --cell-w 216
//   --stills 1.5,23,40      single PNG frames  (--stills-dir dir)
//   --check                 determinism check (ordered vs shuffled across workers) and,
//                           if the song loops (outro = intro), frame-exactness of the loop;
//                           a song that declares an outro loop but has none fails the check
//
// At start-up it prints where line sections came from and which section-derived plates
// (song.sectionPlates, e.g. the bridge long take) exist, with MISSING for absent ones.
//
// Chrome: /Applications/Google Chrome.app (override with CHROME=...). Nothing is downloaded.

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import os from 'node:os';
import { spawn, execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
import { createServer, listen, REPO } from './serve.mjs';

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
const opt = (k, d) => { const i = argv.indexOf('--' + k); return i >= 0 ? argv[i + 1] : d; };
const flag = (k) => argv.includes('--' + k);
const CHROME = process.env.CHROME || '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome';

// the song dir as a repo-relative URL path (the page is served from the repo root); absolute paths work too
const songDir = path.relative(REPO, path.resolve(REPO, opt('song', 'songs/2am-crying/video'))).split(path.sep).join('/');
const layout = opt('layout', null);
const fps = Number(opt('fps', 30));
const workersN = Number(opt('workers', Math.max(2, Math.min(10, Math.floor(os.cpus().length * 0.6)))));
const outMp4 = opt('out', null);
const sheetOut = opt('sheet', null);
const stills = opt('stills', null);
const check = flag('check');
const dataDir = opt('data', null);
let failures = 0;

const abs = (p) => (path.isAbsolute(p) ? p : path.join(REPO, p));
const log = (...a) => console.log('[render]', ...a);

function ffprobeDuration(file) {
  try { return Number(execFileSync('ffprobe', ['-v', 'error', '-show_entries', 'format=duration', '-of', 'csv=p=0', file]).toString().trim()); }
  catch { return NaN; }
}

function pickAudio(audioJson) {
  const given = opt('audio', null);
  const dur = audioJson.duration;
  const bar = (60 / audioJson.bpm) * (audioJson.beats_per_bar || 4);
  let file, why;
  if (given) { file = abs(given); why = '--audio'; }
  else if (String(audioJson.source || '').startsWith('synth')) {
    const dir = path.join(abs(songDir), '..', 'synth', 'out');
    const wavs = fs.existsSync(dir) ? fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.wav')).map((f) => path.join(dir, f)) : [];
    const scored = wavs.map((f) => ({ f, d: ffprobeDuration(f) })).filter((x) => isFinite(x.d)).sort((a, b) => Math.abs(a.d - dur) - Math.abs(b.d - dur));
    if (scored.length) { file = scored[0].f; why = 'synth (closest duration to audio.json)'; }
  }
  if (!file) { file = path.join(abs(songDir), 'audio', 'click.wav'); why = 'click track (audio.json source: ' + (audioJson.source || '?') + ')'; }
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) throw new Error(`audio ${file} not found (generate the click track: node ${songDir}/tools/make_click.mjs)`);
  const d = ffprobeDuration(file);
  if (Math.abs(d - dur) > bar && !flag('force')) {
    throw new Error(`audio ${path.relative(REPO, file)} is ${d.toFixed(2)}s but data/audio.json says ${dur.toFixed(2)}s (more than a bar apart): the timing and the audio do not match. Regenerate timing or pass --force.`);
  }
  return { file, why, d };
}

async function launchWorker(port, w, h, i) {
  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({
    executablePath: CHROME,
    headless: true,
    args: ['--hide-scrollbars', '--mute-audio', '--force-device-scale-factor=1', '--disable-background-timer-throttling',
      '--disable-renderer-backgrounding', '--disable-backgrounding-occluded-windows', '--disable-gpu', '--no-first-run'],
    defaultViewport: { width: Math.min(w, 1920), height: Math.min(h, 1920), deviceScaleFactor: 1 },
    protocolTimeout: 600000,
  });
  const page = await browser.newPage();
  const errors = [];
  page.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push(String(e)));
  page.on('console', (m) => { if ((m.type() === 'error' && !/status of 404/.test(m.text())) || m.type() === 'warn' || m.type() === 'warning') errors.push(m.text()); });
  page.on('response', (r) => { if (r.status() >= 400 && !/favicon/.test(r.url())) errors.push(`HTTP ${r.status()} ${r.url()}`); });
  const q = new URLSearchParams({ render: '1', w: String(w), h: String(h), fps: String(fps) });
  if (layout) q.set('layout', layout);
  if (dataDir) q.set('data', dataDir.replace(/\/?$/, '/'));
  try {
    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/${songDir}/index.html?${q}`, { waitUntil: 'load', timeout: 120000 });
    await page.waitForFunction('window.__render && (window.__render.ready || window.__render.error)', { timeout: 120000 });
  } catch (e) {
    await closeWorker({ browser });
    throw new Error(`worker ${i}: page not ready: ${e.message}${errors.length ? '\n  ' + errors.join('\n  ') : ''}`);
  }
  const info = await page.evaluate(() => (window.__render.error ? { error: window.__render.error } : {
    duration: window.__render.duration, windows: window.__render.windows, w: window.__render.w, h: window.__render.h, layout: window.__render.layout,
    sections: window.__render.sections,
  }));
  if (info.error) throw new Error(`worker ${i}: page failed: ${info.error}\n${errors.join('\n')}`);
  if (i === 0 && errors.length) log('page messages:\n  ' + errors.join('\n  '));
  return { browser, page, info, errors };
}

/** Close a worker's browser, but never hang on it: kill whatever has not exited after 5 s. */
async function closeWorker(x) {
  const proc = x.browser.process();
  await Promise.race([x.browser.close().catch(() => {}), new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5000))]);
  if (proc && proc.exitCode == null) proc.kill('SIGKILL');
}

async function main() {
  const audioJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(abs(songDir), dataDir || 'data', 'audio.json'), 'utf8'));
  const server = createServer(REPO);
  const port = await listen(server, 0);
  // output size from the layout's design size unless given
  let w = Number(opt('w', 0)), h = Number(opt('h', 0));
  if (!w || !h) {
    const portrait = (layout || '9x16') === '9x16';
    w = w || (portrait ? 540 : 960); h = h || (portrait ? 960 : 540);
  }
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const nW = outMp4 ? workersN : 1;
  // launch all workers; if any fails to start, close the ones that did (no orphaned Chromes)
  const launched = await Promise.allSettled(Array.from({ length: nW }, (_, i) => launchWorker(port, w, h, i)));
  const bad = launched.filter((x) => x.status === 'rejected');
  if (bad.length) {
    await Promise.all(launched.filter((x) => x.status === 'fulfilled').map((x) => closeWorker(x.value)));
    server.closeAllConnections?.(); server.close();
    throw bad[0].reason;
  }
  const workers = launched.map((x) => x.value);
  const info = workers[0].info;
  log(`${workers.length} worker(s) ready in ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s · ${info.w}x${info.h} @${fps} · layout ${info.layout} · song ${info.duration.toFixed(2)}s · ${info.windows.length} windows`);
  const P = workers[0].page;
  reportSections(info);

  try {
    if (stills) {
      const dir = abs(opt('stills-dir', path.join(songDir, 'renders', 'stills')));
      fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
      for (const s of stills.split(',').map(Number)) {
        const url = await P.evaluate((t) => window.__render.png(t), s);
        const f = path.join(dir, `still-${info.layout}-${s.toFixed(3)}.png`);
        fs.writeFileSync(f, Buffer.from(url.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
        log('still', path.relative(REPO, f));
      }
    }

    if (sheetOut) {
      const mode = opt('sheet-mode', 'clips');
      let cells;
      if (mode === 'even') {
        const n = Number(opt('count', 24));
        cells = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ t: (info.duration * (i + 0.5)) / n, label: `#${i + 1}` }));
      } else cells = await P.evaluate(() => window.__render.heroCells());
      const portrait = info.h > info.w;
      const cols = Number(opt('cols', portrait ? 10 : 5));
      const cellW = Number(opt('cell-w', portrait ? 216 : 384));
      const title = opt('title', `${songDir} · ${info.layout} · ${cells.length} cells · ${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 16)}`);
      const url = await P.evaluate((c, o) => window.__render.sheet(c, o), cells, { cols, cellW, title });
      const f = abs(sheetOut);
      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(f), { recursive: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(f, Buffer.from(url.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
      log(`sheet (${cells.length} cells) -> ${path.relative(REPO, f)}`);
    }

    if (check) await runChecks(workers, info);

    if (outMp4) await encode(workers, info, w, h, audioJson);
  } finally {
    await Promise.all(workers.map(closeWorker));
    server.closeAllConnections?.();
    server.close();
  }
}

/** Where line sections came from, and which section-derived plates exist (loud when missing). */
function reportSections(info) {
  const S = info.sections || {};
  const fmt = (t) => `${Math.floor(t / 60)}:${(t % 60).toFixed(2).padStart(5, '0')}`;
  log(`line sections from ${S.source || '?'}: ${(S.list || []).join(', ')}`);
  for (const sp of S.expect || []) {
    const ws = info.windows.filter((x) => x.fromSection === sp.label);
    if (ws.length) log(`section plate ok: ${sp.label} -> ${ws.map((x) => `${x.plate}${x.params.mode ? ':' + x.params.mode : ''} ${fmt(x.start)}-${fmt(x.end)} (${(x.end - x.start).toFixed(1)}s)`).join(', ')}`);
    else log(`section plate MISSING: ${sp.label} (no lyric line in section "${sp.section}")`);
  }
}

async function runChecks(workers, info) {
  const P = workers[0].page;
  const ts = [0.5, info.duration * 0.21, info.duration * 0.37, info.duration * 0.55, info.duration * 0.71, info.duration * 0.88].map((t) => Math.round(t * fps) / fps);
  const ordered = [];
  for (const t of ts) ordered.push(await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), t));
  const shuffled = [...ts].map((t, i) => ({ t, i })).sort((a, b) => ((a.i * 7919) % 13) - ((b.i * 7919) % 13));
  // on a fresh worker (or the same one, out of order) with unrelated frames in between
  const Q = workers[workers.length > 1 ? 1 : 0].page;
  const hashes = {};
  for (const { t, i } of shuffled) { await Q.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), info.duration - t); hashes[i] = await Q.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), t); }
  const bad = ts.filter((_, i) => hashes[i] !== ordered[i]);
  log(bad.length ? `DETERMINISM FAIL at ${bad.join(', ')}` : `determinism ok (${ts.length} frames, ordered vs shuffled)`);
  failures += bad.length;
  const intro = info.windows[0], outro = info.windows.find((x) => x.plate === intro.plate && x.params.loop);
  const wantsLoop = (info.sections?.expect || []).some((sp) => /loop/.test(sp.label));
  if (!outro && wantsLoop) { failures++; log('LOOP FAIL: the song declares an outro loop but the timeline has no loop window'); }
  if (outro) {
    const outroStartFrame = Math.ceil(outro.start * fps - 1e-6);
    let fails = 0;
    let shown = null;
    for (const j of [3, 17, 40, 60, 90, 150]) {
      const k = outroStartFrame + j;
      if (k / fps >= info.duration) continue;
      // the intro frame the loop maps onto: the song says (e.g. anchored on a lyric), else outro start -> intro start
      const src = await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.loopSource(x), k / fps);
      const jj = src == null ? Math.round(k - outro.start * fps) : Math.round(src * fps);
      if (src != null && (Math.abs(src * fps - jj) > 1e-6 || src >= outro.start)) { fails++; log(`LOOP FAIL: outro frame ${k} maps to t=${src}, not an intro frame`); continue; }
      const a = await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), jj / fps);
      const b = await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), k / fps);
      if (a !== b) { fails++; log(`LOOP MISMATCH: outro frame ${k} vs intro frame ${jj}`); }
      shown ??= { k, jj };
    }
    if (shown) log(`loop mapping: outro frame ${shown.k} (${(shown.k / fps).toFixed(3)}s) -> intro frame ${shown.jj} (${(shown.jj / fps).toFixed(3)}s), shift ${((shown.k - shown.jj) / fps).toFixed(3)}s`);
    failures += fails;
    log(fails ? `loop check: ${fails} mismatches` : `loop ok (outro frames are intro frames; outro window ${outro.start.toFixed(2)}s, intro ${intro.start.toFixed(2)}s)`);
  }
}

async function encode(workers, info, w, h, audioJson) {
  const from = Number(opt('from', 0));
  const to = Math.min(Number(opt('to', info.duration)), info.duration);
  const f0 = Math.round(from * fps), f1 = Math.round(to * fps);
  const N = f1 - f0;
  const audio = pickAudio(audioJson);
  log(`audio: ${path.relative(REPO, audio.file)} (${audio.why}, ${audio.d.toFixed(2)}s)`);
  const out = abs(outMp4);
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(out), { recursive: true });
  const ff = spawn('ffmpeg', [
    '-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-stats_period', '5',
    '-f', 'rawvideo', '-pix_fmt', 'rgba', '-s', `${w}x${h}`, '-r', String(fps), '-i', 'pipe:0',
    '-ss', String(from), '-t', String(N / fps), '-i', audio.file,
    '-map', '0:v', '-map', '1:a',
    '-vf', 'scale=out_color_matrix=bt709:out_range=tv,format=yuv420p,setparams=color_primaries=bt709:color_trc=bt709:colorspace=bt709:range=tv',
    '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', opt('preset', 'medium'), '-crf', String(opt('crf', 16)),
    '-colorspace', 'bt709', '-color_primaries', 'bt709', '-color_trc', 'bt709', '-color_range', 'tv',
    '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '192k', '-shortest', '-movflags', '+faststart', out,
  ], { stdio: ['pipe', 'inherit', 'inherit'] });
  const ffDone = new Promise((res, rej) => ff.on('close', (c) => (c === 0 ? res() : rej(new Error('ffmpeg exited ' + c)))));

  const buffered = new Map();
  let next = f0, dispatched = f0, written = 0;
  const t0 = Date.now();
  let paintMs = 0;
  const BATCH = 4;
  const maxAhead = workers.length * BATCH * 3;
  async function flush() {
    while (buffered.has(next)) {
      const buf = buffered.get(next);
      buffered.delete(next);
      next++; written++;
      if (!ff.stdin.write(buf)) await new Promise((r) => ff.stdin.once('drain', r));
    }
  }
  let lastLog = 0;
  async function work(wk) {
    while (dispatched < f1) {
      while (dispatched - next > maxAhead) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5));
      const a = dispatched;
      const b = Math.min(f1, a + BATCH);
      dispatched = b;
      const ts = [];
      for (let k = a; k < b; k++) ts.push(k / fps);
      const s0 = Date.now();
      const out64 = await wk.page.evaluate((x) => window.__render.frames(x), ts);
      paintMs += Date.now() - s0;
      out64.forEach((b64, i) => buffered.set(a + i, Buffer.from(b64, 'base64')));
      await flush();
      if (Date.now() - lastLog > 5000) {
        lastLog = Date.now();
        const el = (Date.now() - t0) / 1000;
        log(`${written}/${N} frames · ${(written / el).toFixed(1)} fps · eta ${((N - written) / Math.max(1e-3, written / el)).toFixed(0)}s`);
      }
    }
  }
  await Promise.all(workers.map(work));
  await flush();
  ff.stdin.end();
  await ffDone;
  const el = (Date.now() - t0) / 1000;
  log(`wrote ${path.relative(REPO, out)}: ${N} frames in ${el.toFixed(1)}s · wall ${((el * 1000) / N).toFixed(1)} ms/frame · per-worker paint+transfer ${(paintMs / N).toFixed(1)} ms/frame · ${workers.length} workers`);
}

main().then(() => {
  if (failures) { console.error(`[render] --check: ${failures} failure(s)`); process.exit(2); }
  process.exit(0);
}, (e) => { console.error('[render] FAILED:', e.message || e); process.exit(1); });
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Smoke test for the preview page: loads index.html in headless Chrome with each given
// query, waits for the preview to start, reports errors and saves a screenshot.
//   node shared/renderer/scripts/smoke.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --q "t=23" --q "layout=16x9&t=40" [--shots dir]
import path from 'node:path';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
import { createServer, listen, REPO } from './serve.mjs';

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
const song = argv.includes('--song') ? argv[argv.indexOf('--song') + 1] : 'songs/2am-crying/video';
const qs = argv.flatMap((a, i) => (a === '--q' ? [argv[i + 1]] : []));
const shots = argv.includes('--shots') ? argv[argv.indexOf('--shots') + 1] : null;
const srv = createServer(REPO);
const port = await listen(srv, 0);
const b = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: process.env.CHROME || '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome', headless: true, defaultViewport: { width: 1280, height: 1000 } });
let bad = 0;
for (const q of qs.length ? qs : ['t=0']) {
  const p = await b.newPage();
  const errs = [];
  p.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(String(e)));
  p.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error' && !/favicon|404/.test(m.text())) errs.push(m.text()); });
  await p.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/${song}/index.html?${q}`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
  await p.waitForFunction('window.__preview || (window.__render && window.__render.error)', { timeout: 30000 });
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600));
  const info = await p.evaluate(() => ({ err: document.getElementById('pv-err')?.textContent || '', bar: [...document.querySelectorAll('.pv-bar span')].map((s) => s.textContent).join(' | ') }));
  if (info.err || errs.length) bad++;
  console.log(`[smoke] ${q}: ${info.bar}${info.err ? '\n  RENDER ERROR: ' + info.err : ''}${errs.length ? '\n  ' + errs.join('\n  ') : ''}`);
  if (shots) { fs.mkdirSync(shots, { recursive: true }); await p.screenshot({ path: path.join(shots, `preview-${q.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '_')}.png`) }); }
  await p.close();
}
await b.close();
srv.close();
process.exit(bad ? 1 : 0);
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Static file server for the preview page (ES modules + fetch need http://, and <audio>
// seeking needs Range support). Serves the repo root.
//
//   node shared/renderer/scripts/serve.mjs [--port 8123]
//   open http://localhost:8123/songs/2am-crying/video/index.html?t=23

import http from 'node:http';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

export const REPO = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', '..');

const MIME = {
  '.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', '.js': 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8', '.mjs': 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8',
  '.json': 'application/json; charset=utf-8', '.css': 'text/css; charset=utf-8', '.png': 'image/png', '.jpg': 'image/jpeg',
  '.svg': 'image/svg+xml', '.wav': 'audio/wav', '.mp3': 'audio/mpeg', '.mp4': 'video/mp4', '.woff2': 'font/woff2',
  '.ttf': 'font/ttf', '.txt': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8', '.md': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8',
};

export function createServer(root = REPO) {
  return http.createServer((req, res) => {
    try {
      const url = new URL(req.url, 'http://x');
      let p = path.normalize(decodeURIComponent(url.pathname)).replace(/^([/\\])+/, '');
      const file = path.join(root, p);
      if (!file.startsWith(root)) { res.writeHead(403); return res.end(); }
      let st;
      try { st = fs.statSync(file); } catch { res.writeHead(404); return res.end('not found'); }
      if (st.isDirectory()) {
        const idx = path.join(file, 'index.html');
        if (fs.existsSync(idx)) { res.writeHead(302, { Location: url.pathname.replace(/\/?$/, '/') + 'index.html' + url.search }); return res.end(); }
        res.writeHead(404); return res.end();
      }
      const type = MIME[path.extname(file).toLowerCase()] || 'application/octet-stream';
      const headers = { 'Content-Type': type, 'Cache-Control': 'no-store', 'Accept-Ranges': 'bytes' };
      const range = req.headers.range && /bytes=(\d*)-(\d*)/.exec(req.headers.range);
      if (range) {
        const a = range[1] ? parseInt(range[1], 10) : 0;
        const b = range[2] ? Math.min(parseInt(range[2], 10), st.size - 1) : st.size - 1;
        if (a > b || a >= st.size) { res.writeHead(416, { 'Content-Range': `bytes */${st.size}` }); return res.end(); }
        res.writeHead(206, { ...headers, 'Content-Range': `bytes ${a}-${b}/${st.size}`, 'Content-Length': b - a + 1 });
        if (req.method === 'HEAD') return res.end();
        return fs.createReadStream(file, { start: a, end: b }).pipe(res);
      }
      res.writeHead(200, { ...headers, 'Content-Length': st.size });
      if (req.method === 'HEAD') return res.end();
      fs.createReadStream(file).pipe(res);
    } catch (e) {
      res.writeHead(500); res.end(String(e));
    }
  });
}

export function listen(server, port = 0, host = '127.0.0.1') {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    server.once('error', reject);
    server.listen(port, host, () => resolve(server.address().port));
  });
}

if (process.argv[1] && fileURLToPath(import.meta.url) === path.resolve(process.argv[1])) {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const port = args.includes('--port') ? Number(args[args.indexOf('--port') + 1]) : 8123;
  const srv = createServer();
  listen(srv, port, '0.0.0.0').then((p) => {
    console.log(`serving ${REPO}`);
    console.log(`  http://localhost:${p}/songs/2am-crying/video/index.html?t=0`);
  });
}

// "2AM (And I'm Crying)", v3 "the day": the baby's selfie video. Song module for the
// shared renderer. Every frame: map the outro onto the intro (the loop), build a clip
// descriptor, draw the camera frame (clip under the camera transform -> lens post ->
// lens overlays -> caption -> status bar), then lay it out: full frame (9:16) or inside a
// phone on a soft nursery backdrop (16:9).

import { drawCaption } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/caption.js';
import { rgba, mix } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';
import { rrect } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
import { clamp, TAU } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { timeline, SECTION_PLATES } from './clips.js';
import { makeStory } from './story.js';
import { grade, skyBody } from './tod.js';
import { drawStatusBar } from './statusbar.js';
import { cameraTransform, lensPost } from './camera.js';
import { CW, CH } from './props.js';
import hello from './scenes/hello.js';
import closeup from './scenes/closeup.js';
import crib from './scenes/crib.js';
import nap from './scenes/nap.js';
import pooping from './scenes/pooping.js';
import standing from './scenes/standing.js';
import highchair from './scenes/highchair.js';
import fan from './scenes/fan.js';
import bath from './scenes/bath.js';
import rear from './scenes/rear.js';
import grid from './scenes/grid.js';

export const FONTS = [
  { family: 'Archivo Black', weight: 400 },
  { family: 'IBM Plex Mono', weight: 500 }, { family: 'IBM Plex Mono', weight: 600 }, { family: 'IBM Plex Mono', weight: 700 },
  { family: 'Inter', weight: 700 }, { family: 'Inter', weight: 900 }, { family: 'Inter', weight: 900, style: 'italic' },
  { family: 'Nunito', weight: 900 },
];

export const scenes = {
  hello, closeup, crib, nap, pooping, standing, highchair, fan, bath, rear, grid,
};

export const CAPTION = { x: 540, y: 560, maxW: 900, size: 88, minSize: 56, family: '"Inter", sans-serif', weight: 900 };

/** Words the caption shows at t for this window. */
function captionWords(env, w, t) {
  const st = env.story;
  if (w.plate === 'hello') return w.lines.flatMap((l) => l.words);
  const c = st.clauseAt(t, w);
  return c ? c.words : [];
}

/** Clip descriptor: everything a clip type needs, nothing read from global state. */
export function makeClip(env, t, w, extra = {}) {
  const st = env.story;
  const min = extra.min ?? st.clock(t);
  const G = grade(min);
  return {
    type: w.plate, params: w.params, win: w, t, lt: t - w.start, dur: w.dur, start: w.start, end: w.end,
    min, G, night: G.light.night > 0.5, words: captionWords(env, w, t), clause: st.clauseAt(t, w),
    B: env.B, L: env.L, env, fps: env.fps, story: st, tile: false, ...extra,
  };
}

/** Draw one camera frame (1080x1920 camera space) for clip C. */
export function drawCameraFrame(ctx, C, { chrome = true } = {}) {
  const scene = scenes[C.type];
  const cam = scene.camera?.(C) || {};
  ctx.save();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(0, 0, CW, CH); ctx.clip();
  ctx.fillStyle = C.G.wall;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CW, CH);
  ctx.save();
  cameraTransform(ctx, C, C.tile ? { ...cam, still: true } : cam);
  scene.draw(ctx, C);
  ctx.restore();
  lensPost(ctx, C, cam);
  if (scene.overlay) { ctx.save(); scene.overlay(ctx, C); ctx.restore(); }
  if (chrome) {
    const capOpts = scene.caption ? scene.caption(C) : {};
    if (capOpts !== false) drawCaption(ctx, C.t, C.words, { ...CAPTION, ...capOpts });
    const st = C.story;
    drawStatusBar(ctx, { min: C.min, battery: st.battery(C.t), rec: st.rec(C.t) });
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

// ---- 16:9: the phone centred on a soft, out-of-focus nursery ---------------------------
const PHONE = { sx: 690, sy: 60, sw: 540, sh: 960, bezel: 16, r: 64 };

function drawBackdrop(ctx, C) {
  const G = C.G;
  const W = 1920, H = 1080;
  ctx.fillStyle = G.wall;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const night = G.light.night;
  // window light (left), soft
  const wx = 330, wy = 380;
  let g = ctx.createRadialGradient(wx, wy, 40, wx, wy, 520);
  g.addColorStop(0, rgba(mix(G.window, '#ffffff', 0.3), 0.85 * (1 - night) + 0.1));
  g.addColorStop(0.5, rgba(G.window, 0.35 * (1 - night) + 0.04));
  g.addColorStop(1, rgba(G.window, 0));
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  // crib slats (right), out of focus
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
    const x = 1360 + i * 62;
    const sg = ctx.createLinearGradient(x - 26, 0, x + 26, 0);
    sg.addColorStop(0, rgba(G.crib, 0));
    sg.addColorStop(0.5, rgba(G.crib, 0.55));
    sg.addColorStop(1, rgba(G.crib, 0));
    ctx.fillStyle = sg;
    ctx.fillRect(x - 26, 470, 52, 520);
  }
  ctx.fillStyle = rgba(G.crib, 0.4);
  ctx.fillRect(1310, 440, 600, 40);
  // floor
  g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 900, 0, H);
  g.addColorStop(0, rgba(G.floor, 0));
  g.addColorStop(1, rgba(G.floor, 0.9));
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 900, W, 180);
  // a lamp bokeh at night
  if (night > 0.3) {
    for (const [bx, by, br] of [[260, 760, 70], [330, 700, 40], [1700, 300, 55]]) {
      const bg = ctx.createRadialGradient(bx, by, 0, bx, by, br);
      bg.addColorStop(0, rgba('#ffcf8a', 0.35 * night));
      bg.addColorStop(1, rgba('#ffcf8a', 0));
      ctx.fillStyle = bg;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(bx, by, br, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    }
  }
  // the sun / moon arc (top right)
  const ax = 1660, ay = 230, ar = 150;
  ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(night > 0.5 ? '#c9d3ff' : mix(G.wall, '#000000', 0.45), 0.55);
  ctx.lineWidth = 3;
  ctx.setLineDash([4, 12]);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ax, ay, ar, Math.PI, TAU); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.setLineDash([]);
  const sb = skyBody(C.min);
  const a = Math.PI + sb.p * Math.PI;
  const px = ax + Math.cos(a) * ar, py = ay + Math.sin(a) * ar;
  ctx.fillStyle = sb.body === 'sun' ? '#ffd36b' : '#e8ecff';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, 18, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  if (sb.body === 'moon') { ctx.fillStyle = G.wall; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px + 8, py - 6, 15, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
  // soft vignette
  g = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, 300, W / 2, H / 2, 1150);
  g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
  g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(0,0,0,${0.35 + 0.3 * night})`);
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
}

function drawPhone(ctx, C) {
  const p = PHONE;
  // shadow
  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(p.sx + p.sw / 2, p.sy + p.sh * 0.55, p.sw * 0.3, p.sx + p.sw / 2, p.sy + p.sh * 0.55, p.sh * 0.75);
  g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)');
  g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(p.sx - 400, p.sy - 200, p.sw + 800, p.sh + 400);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0d10';
  rrect(ctx, p.sx - p.bezel, p.sy - p.bezel, p.sw + 2 * p.bezel, p.sh + 2 * p.bezel, p.r + p.bezel);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.save();
  rrect(ctx, p.sx, p.sy, p.sw, p.sh, p.r);
  ctx.clip();
  ctx.translate(p.sx, p.sy);
  ctx.scale(p.sw / CW, p.sh / CH);
  drawCameraFrame(ctx, C);
  ctx.restore();
}

export default {
  id: '2am-crying',
  layouts: { '9x16': { w: 1080, h: 1920 }, '16x9': { w: 1920, h: 1080 } },
  defaultLayout: '9x16',
  fonts: FONTS,
  timeline,
  sectionPlates: SECTION_PLATES,   // render.mjs reports these at start-up (MISSING if absent)
  scenes,
  audioCandidates(audio) {
    const synth = ['../synth/out/2am_crying.wav'];
    const click = ['audio/click.wav'];
    // a real take names its file in the source: "suno runE2 13e92be2 (../audio/runE2/<file>.mp3)"
    const named = /\(([^()]+\.(?:mp3|wav|m4a))\)/.exec(String(audio.source || ''));
    if (named) return [named[1], ...click];
    return String(audio.source || '').startsWith('synth') ? [...synth, ...click] : [...click];
  },
  setup(env) {
    env.story = makeStory(env);
    env.drawCameraFrame = drawCameraFrame;   // the grid draws other clips as tiles
  },
  /** The song time whose frame an outro frame repeats (render.mjs --check compares the two). */
  loopSource(env, t) { return env.story.loopTime(t); },
  /** Extra contact-sheet cells: stages of the long take and the grid filling in. */
  sheetCells(env) {
    const out = [];
    for (const w of env.T.windows) {
      if (w.plate === 'nap' && w.params.mode === 'long') for (const f of [0.3, 0.72, 0.9]) out.push({ t: w.start + w.dur * f, label: `long ${Math.round(f * 100)}%` });
      if (w.plate === 'nap' && w.params.mode === 'tip') out.push({ t: w.start + w.dur * 0.68, label: 'tip 68%' });
      if (w.plate === 'grid') for (const f of [0.2, 0.97]) out.push({ t: w.start + w.dur * f, label: `grid ${Math.round(f * 100)}%` });
    }
    return out;
  },
  frame(ctx, t, env) {
    const st = env.story;
    const tm = st.loopTime(t);
    const w = st.windowFor(tm);
    const C = makeClip(env, tm, w);
    if (env.layout === '16x9') {
      drawBackdrop(ctx, C);
      drawPhone(ctx, C);
    } else {
      drawCameraFrame(ctx, C);
    }
  },
};
// Clips from lyric content. Every "It's <time> and I'm <doing>" clause is one clip; the
// rule table maps the clause's words to a clip TYPE (scene module) and its params. The
// bridge is one long take, the final chorus one grid, the outro is the intro again.

import { norm } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/lyrics.js';

const HM = (h, m = 0) => h * 60 + m;

/** "2am" / "12pm" / "5:45" / "10:30" -> minutes since midnight. am/pm inferred for h:mm. */
export function minutesOf(text, prevMin = null) {
  const s = String(text).toLowerCase();
  let m = s.match(/(\d{1,2})\s*([ap])\.?\s*m\b/);
  if (m) { let h = +m[1] % 12; if (m[2] === 'p') h += 12; return HM(h); }
  m = s.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  if (m) {
    // h:mm without am/pm: take the reading closest after the previous clause's time
    const h = +m[1] % 12, mm = +m[2];
    const am = HM(h, mm), pm = HM(h + 12, mm);
    if (prevMin == null) return am;
    const fwd = (x) => (x - prevMin + 1440) % 1440;
    return fwd(am) <= fwd(pm) ? am : pm;
  }
  return null;
}

/** Split lyric lines into time clauses: a clause starts at "It's" followed by a word with a digit. */
export function clausesOf(L) {
  const out = [];
  let prevMin = null;
  for (const line of L.lines) {
    let cur = null;
    line.words.forEach((w, k) => {
      const next = line.words[k + 1];
      const starts = norm(w.w) === 'its' && next && /\d/.test(next.w) && !w.backing;
      if (starts) {
        cur = { words: [], line, section: line.section, start: w.start };
        out.push(cur);
      }
      if (cur) cur.words.push(w);
      else if (out.length && out.at(-1).section === line.section && !/^(intro|outro)$/.test(line.section)) out.at(-1).words.push(w);
    });
  }
  for (const c of out) {
    c.text = c.words.filter((w) => !w.backing).map((w) => w.w).join(' ');
    c.n = norm(c.text);
    c.end = c.words.at(-1).end;
    c.min = minutesOf(c.text, prevMin);
    prevMin = c.min;
    c.verb = c.n.replace(/^its \S+ (and )?/, '');
  }
  out.forEach((c, i) => { c.index = i; });
  return out;
}

// [pattern on the normalized clause, type, params]. First match wins.
export const RULES = [
  [/^its 2am and im crying/, 'crib', { stage: 0 }],
  [/^its 3am and im crying/, 'crib', { stage: 1 }],
  [/^its 4am and im crying/, 'crib', { stage: 2 }],
  [/^its 5am and im quiet/, 'crib', { mode: 'quiet' }],
  [/^its 7:45 and im sleeping/, 'crib', { mode: 'fake' }],
  [/^its (8pm|9pm) and im sleeping/, 'crib', { mode: 'sleep' }],
  [/^its 11pm and were sleeping/, 'crib', { mode: 'sleep', dark: 1 }],
  [/^its 12am and im sleeping/, 'crib', { mode: 'sleep', dark: 2 }],
  [/^its 1am and im/, 'crib', { mode: 'void' }],
  [/^its 10pm and youre sleeping/, 'rear', {}],
  [/^its 11am and im napping/, 'nap', { mode: 'tip' }],
  [/^its 3pm and im napping/, 'nap', { mode: 'carseat' }],
  [/^its 1pm and im pooping$/, 'pooping', {}],
  [/^its 2pm and im pooping again/, 'pooping', { again: true }],
  [/^its 12pm and im crying/, 'highchair', { mode: 'spoon' }],
  [/^its (8am|6pm) and im eating/, 'highchair', { mode: 'eat' }],
  [/^its 8:05 and im throwing it/, 'highchair', { mode: 'throw' }],
  [/^its 6:05 and the floor is eating/, 'highchair', { mode: 'floor' }],
  [/^its 5:45 and im up/, 'standing', { mode: 'up' }],
  [/^its 6am and im standing/, 'standing', { mode: 'stand' }],
  [/^its 6:15 and im still standing/, 'standing', { mode: 'stand', timer: true }],
  [/^its 7:30 and im wearing it/, 'standing', { mode: 'wearing' }],
  [/^its 7:15 and im naked and im running/, 'standing', { mode: 'run' }],
  [/^its 7pm and im splashing/, 'bath', {}],
  [/^its 10:30 and im pointing at the fan/, 'fan', { mode: 'point' }],
  [/^its 5:30 and im crying at the fan/, 'fan', { mode: 'cry' }],
  [/^its (7am|4pm) and im drinking/, 'closeup', { prop: 'bottle' }],
  [/^its 9am and im reading/, 'closeup', { prop: 'book' }],
  [/^its 9:01 and im eating it/, 'closeup', { prop: 'book', bite: true }],
  [/^its 7:30 and im reading/, 'closeup', { prop: 'book', crib: true }],
  [/^its 10am and im pointing/, 'closeup', { prop: 'point' }],
  [/^its 10:45 and im fighting it/, 'closeup', { mode: 'fight' }],
  [/^its 5pm and im crying/, 'closeup', { mode: 'cry' }],
];

export function ruleFor(clause) {
  for (const [re, type, params] of RULES) if (re.test(clause.n)) return { type, params: { ...params }, matched: String(re) };
  return { type: 'closeup', params: {}, matched: null };
}

/**
 * Plates that exist only because of a line's section id. If the sections are wrong (e.g.
 * audio.json's detected s1..s11 instead of the sheet's ids) they silently vanish, so
 * render.mjs lists them at start-up and flags any that are MISSING.
 */
export const SECTION_PLATES = [
  { label: 'bridge long take', section: 'bridge', plate: 'nap' },
  { label: 'final chorus grid', section: 'final', plate: 'grid' },
  { label: 'outro loop', section: 'outro', plate: 'hello' },
];

/** Timeline spec function: the engine calls it with the parsed lyrics and beat grid. */
export function timeline({ lyrics, beats }) {
  const clauses = clausesOf(lyrics);
  const entries = [{ plate: 'hello', at: { time: 0 }, params: { loop: false }, hero: (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.62 }];
  const hero80 = (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.8;
  const fallbacks = [];
  for (const c of clauses) {
    if (c.section === 'bridge') {
      if (!entries.some((e) => e.plate === 'nap' && e.params.mode === 'long')) {
        entries.push({ plate: 'nap', params: { mode: 'long', clauses: clauses.filter((x) => x.section === 'bridge').map((x) => x.index) }, at: { t: c.start, why: 'bridge long take' }, fromSection: 'bridge long take', hero: (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.5 });
      }
      continue;
    }
    if (c.section === 'final') {
      if (!entries.some((e) => e.plate === 'grid')) {
        entries.push({ plate: 'grid', params: { clauses: clauses.filter((x) => x.section === 'final').map((x) => x.index) }, at: { t: c.start, why: 'final chorus grid' }, fromSection: 'final chorus grid', hero: (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.55 });
      }
      continue;
    }
    const rule = ruleFor(c);
    if (!rule.matched) fallbacks.push(c.text);
    entries.push({
      plate: rule.type,
      params: { ...rule.params, clause: c.index },
      at: { t: c.start, why: c.text },
      snap: /^verse/.test(c.section) ? 'halfbar' : 'downbeat',
      hero: hero80,
    });
  }
  const outro = lyrics.findLine({ match: 'Hi', section: 'outro' });
  if (outro) entries.push({ plate: 'hello', params: { loop: true }, at: { line: 'Hi', section: 'outro', why: 'outro loop' }, fromSection: 'outro loop', hero: (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.62 });
  if (fallbacks.length) console.warn(`[clips] ${fallbacks.length} clause(s) matched no rule and use the closeup fallback:\n  ${fallbacks.join('\n  ')}`);
  const got = SECTION_PLATES.map((sp) => ({ ...sp, e: entries.find((e) => e.fromSection === sp.label) }));
  const lost = got.filter((g) => !g.e);
  if (lost.length) {
    const have = [...new Set(lyrics.lines.map((l) => l.section))].join(', ');
    console.warn(`[clips] MISSING section plate(s): ${lost.map((g) => `${g.label} (no line in section "${g.section}")`).join('; ')}. Line sections: ${have}`);
  }
  return entries;
}
// Song-level curves for v3 "the day", all derived from lyric content:
//   loopTime(t)  outro frames map frame-exactly onto intro frames (the video loops)
//   clock(t)     status-bar clock, minutes since midnight (the escalating number)
//   battery(t)   drains across the day, charges during naps
//   rec(t)       record dot + running timer (starts with the beat in the intro)
//   clause(t)    the time clause on screen (caption words, minutes)

import { clamp, lerp, bisect } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { clausesOf } from './clips.js';

const NAP_TYPES = new Set(['nap']);

export function makeStory(env) {
  const { L, B, T } = env;
  const clauses = clausesOf(L);
  const W = T.windows;
  const intro = W[0];
  const outro = W.find((w) => w.plate === 'hello' && w.params.loop) || null;

  // The outro is the intro again, frame-exactly: outro frame k shows intro frame k - shift
  // (same t -> same pixels, every input included). The shift lines the outro's "Hi" up with
  // the intro's: a real vocal does not start them at the same offset into their windows
  // (Suno runE2: 2.80 s into the intro, 0.18 s into the outro), and the caption is the
  // lyric. It is a whole number of frames, so every outro frame is an intro frame.
  const fps = env.fps || 30;
  const hiIntro = L.findLine({ match: 'Hi', section: 'intro' });
  const hiOutro = L.findLine({ match: 'Hi', section: 'outro' });
  const shiftFrames = outro
    ? Math.round(((hiIntro && hiOutro) ? hiOutro.start - hiIntro.start : outro.start - intro.start) * fps)
    : 0;
  function loopTime(t) {
    if (!outro || t < outro.start) return t;
    return Math.max(intro.start, (Math.round(t * fps) - shiftFrames) / fps);
  }
  const windowFor = (t) => (outro && t >= outro.start ? intro : T.windowAt(t));

  // ---- which clause is on screen ---------------------------------------------------
  function clauseAt(t, w = windowFor(t)) {
    const p = w.params;
    if (p.clause != null) return clauses[p.clause];
    if (p.clauses) {
      const list = p.clauses.map((i) => clauses[i]);
      let cur = list[0];
      for (const c of list) if (B.snap(c.start, 'downbeat') <= t + 1e-6 || c.start <= t) cur = c;
      return cur;
    }
    return null;
  }

  // ---- clock -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const INTRO_MIN = 119; // 1:59am: the phone wakes a minute before the first clip
  function clock(t) {
    const w = windowFor(t);
    if (w.plate === 'hello') return INTRO_MIN;
    if (w.plate === 'nap' && w.params.mode === 'long') {
      // the one long take ticks minute by minute between the sung times
      const list = w.params.clauses.map((i) => clauses[i]);
      const i = bisect(list, t, (c) => c.start);
      if (i < 0) return list[0].min;
      const a = list[i], b = list[i + 1];
      if (!b) return a.min + Math.floor(clamp((t - a.start) / (w.end - a.start)) * 1.99);
      return Math.floor(lerp(a.min, b.min, clamp((t - a.start) / (b.start - a.start))));
    }
    const c = clauseAt(t, w);
    return c ? c.min : INTRO_MIN;
  }

  // ---- battery: integrate drain / charge over the windows ------------------------------
  const DRAIN = 1.8, CHARGE = 2.0; // percent per second: ends the day in the red
  const isNap = (w) => NAP_TYPES.has(w.plate);
  const batt0 = [];
  let lvl = 100;
  for (const w of W) {
    batt0.push(lvl);
    if (w === outro) break;
    lvl = clamp(lvl + (isNap(w) ? CHARGE : -DRAIN) * w.dur, 4, 100);
  }
  function battery(t) {
    const w = windowFor(t);
    const i = W.indexOf(w);
    const v = batt0[i] + (isNap(w) ? CHARGE : -DRAIN) * (t - w.start);
    return { level: clamp(v, 4, 100) / 100, charging: isNap(w) };
  }

  // ---- record dot + timer -------------------------------------------------------------
  const untz = L.findLine({ match: 'untz', section: 'intro' }) || L.findLine({ match: 'untz' });
  const recStart = untz ? B.snap(untz.start, 'downbeat') : intro.start + B.barDur;
  function rec(t) {
    const tt = loopTime(t);
    if (tt < recStart) return { on: false, secs: 0 };
    return { on: true, secs: tt - recStart };
  }

  return { clauses, loopTime, loopShift: shiftFrames / fps, windowFor, clauseAt, clock, battery, rec, recStart, intro, outro };
}
// Phone status bar (camera space, top 110 px): clock = the escalating number, a red
// record dot with a running timer, signal and a battery that drains across the day and
// charges (bolt) during naps.

import { clamp } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { rrect } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
import { rgba } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';

export const SB = { h: 112, y: 70 };
const SANS = '"Inter", sans-serif';
const MONO = '"IBM Plex Mono", monospace';

/** 12-hour, no am/pm, no leading zero: the way a phone shows it. */
export function phoneClock(min) {
  const m = Math.floor(((min % 1440) + 1440) % 1440);
  const h = Math.floor(m / 60) % 12 || 12;
  return `${h}:${String(m % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}
export const recText = (secs) => `${String(Math.floor(secs / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(Math.floor(secs % 60)).padStart(2, '0')}`;

export function drawStatusBar(ctx, { min, battery, rec, alpha = 1, dark = false }) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  // a soft scrim so white glyphs read on a white noon wall
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 190);
  g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.34)');
  g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 1080, 190);
  const fg = dark ? '#111111' : '#ffffff';
  ctx.fillStyle = fg;
  ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  // clock
  ctx.font = `700 46px ${SANS}`;
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText(phoneClock(min), 74, SB.y + 6);
  // record pill (centre)
  if (rec && rec.on) {
    ctx.font = `600 36px ${MONO}`;
    const txt = recText(rec.secs);
    const tw = ctx.measureText(txt).width;
    const pw = tw + 86, px = 540 - pw / 2;
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)';
    rrect(ctx, px, SB.y - 38, pw, 56, 28); ctx.fill();
    const blink = (rec.secs % 1) < 0.6 ? 1 : 0.25;
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ff2d2d', blink);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px + 34, SB.y - 10, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.fillText(txt, px + 58, SB.y + 2);
  }
  // signal
  const sx = 800;
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    const h = 12 + i * 8;
    ctx.fillStyle = i < 3 ? fg : rgba(fg, 0.35);
    rrect(ctx, sx + i * 15, SB.y + 2 - h, 10, h, 2); ctx.fill();
  }
  // battery
  const lvl = clamp(battery.level);
  const bx = 900, by = SB.y - 30, bw = 84, bh = 38;
  ctx.font = `700 26px ${SANS}`;
  ctx.textAlign = 'right';
  ctx.fillStyle = fg;
  ctx.fillText(`${Math.round(lvl * 100)}`, bx - 10, SB.y + 1);
  ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(fg, 0.55);
  ctx.lineWidth = 3;
  rrect(ctx, bx, by, bw, bh, 10); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = rgba(fg, 0.55);
  rrect(ctx, bx + bw + 3, by + 12, 6, 14, 3); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = battery.charging ? '#34c759' : lvl < 0.2 ? '#ff3b30' : fg;
  rrect(ctx, bx + 5, by + 5, (bw - 10) * Math.max(0.06, lvl), bh - 10, 6); ctx.fill();
  if (battery.charging) {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    ctx.beginPath();
    const cx = bx + bw / 2, cy = by + bh / 2;
    ctx.moveTo(cx + 4, cy - 17); ctx.lineTo(cx - 10, cy + 3); ctx.lineTo(cx - 1, cy + 3);
    ctx.lineTo(cx - 5, cy + 17); ctx.lineTo(cx + 10, cy - 3); ctx.lineTo(cx + 1, cy - 3);
    ctx.closePath(); ctx.stroke(); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}
