BashWrite a 2am pixel-hash baseline script in the scratchpad
input
description Write a 2am pixel-hash baseline script in the scratchpad
mkdir -p /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jalex-projects-clawdpop/ca2089be-6e29-4607-84a4-ba095fc3fca1/scratchpad/pix && cat > /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jalex-projects-clawdpop/ca2089be-6e29-4607-84a4-ba095fc3fca1/scratchpad/pixcheck.mjs <<'EOF'
// Hash full-resolution 2am frames (RGBA sha1) at every window's hero time plus extra
// sample times, in both layouts. Usage: node pixcheck.mjs out.json [pngdir]
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import puppeteer from '/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/node_modules/puppeteer-core/lib/esm/puppeteer/puppeteer-core.js';
import { createServer, listen } from '/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/scripts/serve.mjs';

const out = process.argv[2];
const pngDir = process.argv[3] || null;
const srv = createServer('/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop');
const port = await listen(srv, 0);
const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome', headless: true,
  args: ['--hide-scrollbars', '--mute-audio', '--force-device-scale-factor=1', '--disable-gpu', '--no-first-run'] });
const res = {};
try {
  for (const [layout, w, h] of [['9x16', 1080, 1920], ['16x9', 1920, 1080]]) {
    const page = await browser.newPage();
    const errs = [];
    page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(String(e)));
    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/songs/2am-crying/video/index.html?render=1&w=${w}&h=${h}&fps=30&layout=${layout}`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
    await page.waitForFunction('window.__render && (window.__render.ready || window.__render.error)', { timeout: 60000 });
    const err = await page.evaluate(() => window.__render.error || null);
    if (err) throw new Error(err);
    const cells = await page.evaluate(() => window.__render.heroCells());
    const dur = await page.evaluate(() => window.__render.duration);
    const ts = [...cells.map((c) => c.t)];
    for (let i = 0; i < 24; i++) ts.push(Math.round(((dur * (i + 0.37)) / 24) * 30) / 30);
    for (const t of ts) {
      const b64 = await page.evaluate((x) => window.__render.frame(x), t);
      res[`${layout}@${t.toFixed(4)}`] = crypto.createHash('sha1').update(Buffer.from(b64, 'base64')).digest('hex');
    }
    if (pngDir) {
      fs.mkdirSync(pngDir, { recursive: true });
      for (const c of cells.filter((_, i) => i % 6 === 0)) {
        const url = await page.evaluate((x) => window.__render.png(x), c.t);
        fs.writeFileSync(path.join(pngDir, `${layout}-${c.t.toFixed(2)}-${c.label.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '_')}.png`), Buffer.from(url.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
      }
    }
    console.log(layout, ts.length, 'frames', errs.length ? 'ERRORS ' + errs.join(' | ') : '');
    await page.close();
  }
} finally {
  await browser.close();
  srv.close();
}
fs.writeFileSync(out, JSON.stringify(res, null, 1));
console.log('wrote', out, Object.keys(res).length);
EOF

// Hand-inked strokes for paper pages (the baby-facts video, "Replicate (n = 1)").
//
// A stroke is a filled ribbon along a polyline. Its points wobble perpendicular to the
// stroke by smooth value noise of the arclength, seeded by the pen's seed and the stroke's
// key; its width breathes a little and tapers at the ends, as a technical pen does. Nothing
// depends on wall time or on how much of the stroke is drawn: o.p < 1 draws an exact prefix
// of the finished stroke, so a stroke grows in time without wiggling.
//
//   const pen = makePen(ctx, 'feldman', { color: '#1d1a17', width: 3 });
//   pen.line(x0, y0, x1, y1, { p })            p = drawn fraction 0..1 (default 1)
//   pen.poly([[x, y], ...], { close })         pen.spline(points)  (Catmull-Rom through them)
//   pen.rect(x, y, w, h)  pen.rrect(x, y, w, h, r)  pen.ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry)  pen.circle(cx, cy, r)
//   pen.arc(cx, cy, r, a0, a1)  pen.curve(p0, c1, c2, p1)  pen.quad(p0, c, p1)  pen.arrow(x0, y0, x1, y1)
//   pen.hatch(region, { angle, gap, p })       region: [x, y, w, h] | polygon [[x, y], ...] | (ctx) => path, with o.bbox
//   pen.dotted(points, { gap, r })  pen.dashed(points, { dash, gap })
//   pen.with({ color, width, ... })            same seed and stroke counter, other defaults
//   pen.sub('label')                           an independent pen for one element (its own seed)
//
// Stroke keys: by default each call takes the next index of the pen's counter, so the n-th
// stroke of a plate always wobbles the same way. Strokes that come and go (an element that
// appears halfway through a figure) shift the index of every stroke after them; give those
// a stable key (o.k = 'label' or a number) or draw them with their own pen.sub('label').
//
// Options (pen defaults, overridable per stroke): color, width (design px), alpha, wobble
// (amplitude, px), wave (wavelength, px), jitter (end-point slop, px), taper (end width as a
// fraction of the width), over (overshoot past the end points, px), step (resampling, px).

import { clamp, lerp, noise1, hash, shash, strSeed, TAU } from './util.js';

export const INK_DEFAULTS = { color: '#1d1a17', width: 3, alpha: 1, wobble: 2.6, wave: 150, jitter: 1.4, taper: 0.45, over: 0, step: 5 };

const seedOf = (x) => (typeof x === 'number' ? (x >>> 0) : strSeed(String(x)));
const mixSeed = (a, b) => (Math.imul((a ^ seedOf(b)) >>> 0, 2654435761) ^ (seedOf(b) * 40503)) >>> 0;

/** Resample a polyline: keep every vertex, insert points so no gap exceeds `step`. */
function resample(pts, step) {
  const out = [pts[0]];
  for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) {
    const [ax, ay] = pts[i - 1], [bx, by] = pts[i];
    const d = Math.hypot(bx - ax, by - ay);
    const n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(d / step));
    for (let k = 1; k <= n; k++) out.push([ax + ((bx - ax) * k) / n, ay + ((by - ay) * k) / n]);
  }
  return out;
}

/** Extend the first and last segments by `a` and `b` px. */
function extend(pts, a, b) {
  if (pts.length < 2 || (!a && !b)) return pts;
  const out = pts.slice();
  const ext = (p, q, e) => { const d = Math.hypot(p[0] - q[0], p[1] - q[1]) || 1; return [p[0] + ((p[0] - q[0]) * e) / d, p[1] + ((p[1] - q[1]) * e) / d]; };
  if (a) out[0] = ext(pts[0], pts[1], a);
  if (b) out[out.length - 1] = ext(pts.at(-1), pts.at(-2), b);
  return out;
}

/**
 * Draw one stroke along `pts` with options `o` (all of them resolved) and seed `sd`.
 * The geometry is a pure function of (pts, o minus p, sd); o.p only truncates it.
 */
function drawStroke(ctx, pts, o, sd) {
  if (!pts || pts.length < 2) return;
  const p = clamp(o.p ?? 1);
  if (p <= 0 || o.alpha <= 0) return;
  // end-point slop: a hand never lands exactly on the mark
  const j = o.jitter;
  let P = pts.map((q) => [q[0], q[1]]);
  if (j && !o.closed) {
    P[0] = [P[0][0] + shash(sd, 11) * j, P[0][1] + shash(sd, 12) * j];
    P[P.length - 1] = [P.at(-1)[0] + shash(sd, 13) * j, P.at(-1)[1] + shash(sd, 14) * j];
  }
  P = extend(P, o.over ? o.over * (0.6 + 0.8 * hash(sd, 15)) : 0, o.over ? o.over * (0.6 + 0.8 * hash(sd, 16)) : 0);
  P = resample(P, o.step);
  const n = P.length;
  const s = new Float64Array(n);
  for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) s[i] = s[i - 1] + Math.hypot(P[i][0] - P[i - 1][0], P[i][1] - P[i - 1][1]);
  const S = s[n - 1];
  if (S < 0.5) return;
  // normals (averaged at vertices, miter-corrected so the ribbon keeps its width at corners)
  const nx = new Float64Array(n), ny = new Float64Array(n), nk = new Float64Array(n);
  const segN = (i) => { const dx = P[i + 1][0] - P[i][0], dy = P[i + 1][1] - P[i][1], d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1; return [-dy / d, dx / d]; };
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const a = i > 0 ? segN(i - 1) : segN(0), b = i < n - 1 ? segN(i) : segN(n - 2);
    let x = a[0] + b[0], y = a[1] + b[1];
    const d = Math.hypot(x, y);
    if (d < 1e-6) { x = b[0]; y = b[1]; } else { x /= d; y /= d; }
    nx[i] = x; ny[i] = y;
    nk[i] = 1 / Math.max(0.55, x * b[0] + y * b[1]);
  }
  // wobble (scaled down on short strokes) and width
  const A = o.wobble * clamp(S / 80, 0.25, 1);
  const ph = hash(sd, 21) * 50, ph2 = hash(sd, 22) * 50, ph3 = hash(sd, 23) * 50;
  const end = Math.min(10, S * 0.25);
  const L = [], R = [];
  const sMax = p * S;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    let si = s[i], x = P[i][0], y = P[i][1], k = i;
    if (si > sMax) {
      // the truncation point, interpolated on segment i-1 -> i
      const f = (sMax - s[i - 1]) / Math.max(1e-9, si - s[i - 1]);
      x = lerp(P[i - 1][0], x, f); y = lerp(P[i - 1][1], y, f); si = sMax; k = i - 1;
    }
    const off = A * (0.8 * noise1(si / o.wave + ph, sd) + 0.28 * noise1(si / (o.wave * 0.23) + ph2, sd + 7));
    const ends = Math.min(1, si / end, (S - si) / end);
    const w = o.width * (1 + 0.16 * noise1(si / 70 + ph3, sd + 3)) * lerp(o.taper, 1, Math.sqrt(clamp(ends))) * 0.5;
    const cx = x + nx[k] * off, cy = y + ny[k] * off;
    L.push([cx + nx[k] * w * nk[k], cy + ny[k] * w * nk[k]]);
    R.push([cx - nx[k] * w * nk[k], cy - ny[k] * w * nk[k]]);
    if (si >= sMax) break;
  }
  if (L.length < 2) return;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= o.alpha;
  ctx.fillStyle = o.color;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(L[0][0], L[0][1]);
  for (let i = 1; i < L.length; i++) ctx.lineTo(L[i][0], L[i][1]);
  for (let i = R.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) ctx.lineTo(R[i][0], R[i][1]);
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
}

function ellipsePts(cx, cy, rx, ry, a0, a1, rot = 0) {
  const n = Math.max(12, Math.ceil((Math.abs(a1 - a0) * Math.max(rx, ry)) / 6));
  const out = [];
  const c = Math.cos(rot), sn = Math.sin(rot);
  for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
    const a = lerp(a0, a1, i / n), x = Math.cos(a) * rx, y = Math.sin(a) * ry;
    out.push([cx + x * c - y * sn, cy + x * sn + y * c]);
  }
  return out;
}

function bezierPts(p0, c1, c2, p1) {
  const len = Math.hypot(c1[0] - p0[0], c1[1] - p0[1]) + Math.hypot(c2[0] - c1[0], c2[1] - c1[1]) + Math.hypot(p1[0] - c2[0], p1[1] - c2[1]);
  const n = Math.max(8, Math.ceil(len / 6));
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
    const t = i / n, u = 1 - t;
    out.push([u * u * u * p0[0] + 3 * u * u * t * c1[0] + 3 * u * t * t * c2[0] + t * t * t * p1[0],
      u * u * u * p0[1] + 3 * u * u * t * c1[1] + 3 * u * t * t * c2[1] + t * t * t * p1[1]]);
  }
  return out;
}

/** Catmull-Rom spline through the points (a smooth hand curve through data points). */
export function splinePts(pts, k = 8) {
  if (pts.length < 3) return pts.slice();
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
    const p0 = pts[Math.max(0, i - 1)], p1 = pts[i], p2 = pts[i + 1], p3 = pts[Math.min(pts.length - 1, i + 2)];
    for (let j = 0; j < k; j++) {
      const t = j / k, t2 = t * t, t3 = t2 * t;
      out.push([0.5 * (2 * p1[0] + (-p0[0] + p2[0]) * t + (2 * p0[0] - 5 * p1[0] + 4 * p2[0] - p3[0]) * t2 + (-p0[0] + 3 * p1[0] - 3 * p2[0] + p3[0]) * t3),
        0.5 * (2 * p1[1] + (-p0[1] + p2[1]) * t + (2 * p0[1] - 5 * p1[1] + 4 * p2[1] - p3[1]) * t2 + (-p0[1] + 3 * p1[1] - 3 * p2[1] + p3[1]) * t3)]);
    }
  }
  out.push(pts.at(-1));
  return out;
}

/** Split a fraction p over pieces of the given lengths, drawn one after the other. */
function seqP(p, lens) {
  const tot = lens.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1;
  let acc = 0;
  return lens.map((l) => { const v = clamp((p * tot - acc) / (l || 1e-9)); acc += l; return v; });
}
const plen = (pts) => { let d = 0; for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) d += Math.hypot(pts[i][0] - pts[i - 1][0], pts[i][1] - pts[i - 1][1]); return d; };

export function makePen(ctx, seed = 0, opts = {}, counter = { n: 0 }) {
  const base = { ...INK_DEFAULTS, ...opts };
  const s0 = seedOf(seed);
  const key = (o) => mixSeed(s0, o.k != null ? `k:${o.k}` : counter.n++);
  const res = (o) => ({ ...base, ...o });
  const pen = {
    ctx, seed: s0, opts: base,
    with(o2) { return makePen(ctx, s0, { ...base, ...o2 }, counter); },
    sub(label) { return makePen(ctx, mixSeed(s0, `sub:${label}`), base); },
    /** The core: one stroke along a polyline. */
    stroke(pts, o = {}) { const r = res(o); drawStroke(ctx, r.close ? [...pts, pts[0]] : pts, r, key(o)); return pen; },
    line(x0, y0, x1, y1, o = {}) { return pen.stroke([[x0, y0], [x1, y1]], o); },
    poly(pts, o = {}) { return pen.stroke(pts, o); },
    spline(pts, o = {}) { return pen.stroke(splinePts(pts, o.smooth ?? 8), o); },
    curve(p0, c1, c2, p1, o = {}) { return pen.stroke(bezierPts(p0, c1, c2, p1), o); },
    quad(p0, c, p1, o = {}) { return pen.stroke(bezierPts(p0, [p0[0] + (2 / 3) * (c[0] - p0[0]), p0[1] + (2 / 3) * (c[1] - p0[1])], [p1[0] + (2 / 3) * (c[0] - p1[0]), p1[1] + (2 / 3) * (c[1] - p1[1])], p1), o); },
    arc(cx, cy, r, a0, a1, o = {}) { return pen.stroke(ellipsePts(cx, cy, r, r, a0, a1), o); },
    /** A hand-drawn ellipse: starts at a seeded angle and overshoots its start a little. */
    ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, o = {}) {
      const sd = key(o);
      const a0 = (o.start ?? hash(sd, 31) * TAU), sweep = TAU + (o.overshoot ?? 0.18);
      drawStroke(ctx, ellipsePts(cx, cy, rx, ry, a0, a0 + sweep, o.rot || 0), { ...res(o), closed: true, jitter: 0 }, sd);
      return pen;
    },
    circle(cx, cy, r, o = {}) { return pen.ellipse(cx, cy, r, r, o); },
    /** Four strokes, each overshooting its corner a little (drafting style); drawn clockwise in sequence. */
    rect(x, y, w, h, o = {}) {
      const sides = [[[x, y], [x + w, y]], [[x + w, y], [x + w, y + h]], [[x + w, y + h], [x, y + h]], [[x, y + h], [x, y]]];
      const ps = seqP(o.p ?? 1, [w, h, w, h]);
      const over = o.over ?? 3;
      sides.forEach((sd, i) => pen.stroke(sd, { ...o, p: ps[i], over, k: o.k != null ? `${o.k}:${i}` : undefined }));
      return pen;
    },
    rrect(x, y, w, h, r, o = {}) {
      r = Math.max(0, Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2));
      const pts = [];
      const corner = (cx, cy, a0) => { for (const q of ellipsePts(cx, cy, r, r, a0, a0 + Math.PI / 2)) pts.push(q); };
      pts.push([x + r, y]);
      corner(x + w - r, y + r, -Math.PI / 2);
      corner(x + w - r, y + h - r, 0);
      corner(x + r, y + h - r, Math.PI / 2);
      corner(x + r, y + r, Math.PI);
      pts.push([x + r + 2, y]);
      return pen.stroke(pts, { ...o, jitter: 0 });
    },
    arrow(x0, y0, x1, y1, o = {}) {
      const p = o.p ?? 1;
      const L = Math.hypot(x1 - x0, y1 - y0) || 1, ux = (x1 - x0) / L, uy = (y1 - y0) / L;
      const head = o.head ?? Math.min(18, L * 0.3);
      const [pa, pb] = seqP(p, [L, head * 1.6]);
      pen.line(x0, y0, x1, y1, { ...o, p: pa });
      if (pb > 0) for (const sgn of [-1, 1]) {
        const a = sgn * 0.45;
        const hx = x1 - (ux * Math.cos(a) - uy * Math.sin(a)) * head, hy = y1 - (uy * Math.cos(a) + ux * Math.sin(a)) * head;
        pen.line(x1, y1, hx, hy, { ...o, p: pb, k: o.k != null ? `${o.k}:h${sgn}` : undefined });
      }
      return pen;
    },
    /**
     * Hatching: parallel thin strokes clipped to a region. region = [x, y, w, h], a polygon
     * [[x, y], ...] or a function that builds a path on ctx (then pass o.bbox = [x, y, w, h]).
     * o.angle (radians, default -45 deg), o.gap (px), o.p draws the lines in one by one.
     */
    hatch(region, o = {}) {
      const r = res({ width: Math.max(1, base.width * 0.45), wobble: base.wobble * 0.35, jitter: 2, ...o });
      let bbox = o.bbox, path;
      if (typeof region === 'function') path = region;
      else if (region.length === 4 && typeof region[0] === 'number') { bbox = region; path = (c) => { c.beginPath(); c.rect(region[0], region[1], region[2], region[3]); }; }
      else {
        const xs = region.map((q) => q[0]), ys = region.map((q) => q[1]);
        bbox = bbox || [Math.min(...xs), Math.min(...ys), Math.max(...xs) - Math.min(...xs), Math.max(...ys) - Math.min(...ys)];
        path = (c) => { c.beginPath(); region.forEach((q, i) => (i ? c.lineTo(q[0], q[1]) : c.moveTo(q[0], q[1]))); c.closePath(); };
      }
      if (!bbox) throw new Error('pen.hatch: a path function needs o.bbox');
      const [bx, by, bw, bh] = bbox;
      const ang = o.angle ?? -Math.PI / 4, gap = o.gap ?? 9;
      const ux = Math.cos(ang), uy = Math.sin(ang), vx = -uy, vy = ux;
      const cx = bx + bw / 2, cy = by + bh / 2, R = Math.hypot(bw, bh) / 2 + 4;
      const nLines = Math.ceil((2 * R) / gap);
      const sd0 = key(o);
      ctx.save();
      path(ctx);
      ctx.clip();
      const p = clamp(o.p ?? 1);
      for (let i = 0; i <= nLines; i++) {
        const lp = clamp(p * (nLines + 1) - i);
        if (lp <= 0) break;
        const d = -R + i * gap + shash(i, sd0) * gap * 0.12;
        const mx = cx + vx * d, my = cy + vy * d;
        drawStroke(ctx, [[mx - ux * R, my - uy * R], [mx + ux * R, my + uy * R]], { ...r, p: lp }, mixSeed(sd0, i));
      }
      ctx.restore();
      return pen;
    },
    /** Dots along a polyline every `gap` px (dotted leaders, trajectories). */
    dotted(pts, o = {}) {
      const r = res(o);
      const gap = o.gap ?? 11, rad = o.r ?? Math.max(1, r.width * 0.55);
      const P = resample(pts, 1.5);
      const sd = key(o);
      let acc = 0, next = 0;
      const total = plen(P), lim = clamp(o.p ?? 1) * total;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha *= r.alpha;
      ctx.fillStyle = r.color;
      let k = 0;
      for (let i = 1; i < P.length; i++) {
        acc += Math.hypot(P[i][0] - P[i - 1][0], P[i][1] - P[i - 1][1]);
        if (acc > lim) break;
        if (acc >= next) {
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(P[i][0] + shash(k, sd) * 0.8, P[i][1] + shash(k, sd + 1) * 0.8, rad * (0.85 + 0.3 * hash(k, sd + 2)), 0, TAU);
          ctx.fill();
          next += gap; k++;
        }
      }
      ctx.restore();
      return pen;
    },
    /** Dashes along a polyline. */
    dashed(pts, o = {}) {
      const dash = o.dash ?? 14, gap = o.gap ?? 9;
      const P = resample(pts, 2);
      const total = plen(P), lim = clamp(o.p ?? 1) * total;
      const sd = key(o);
      let acc = 0, cur = [P[0]], on = true, mark = dash, idx = 0;
      for (let i = 1; i < P.length; i++) {
        acc += Math.hypot(P[i][0] - P[i - 1][0], P[i][1] - P[i - 1][1]);
        if (acc > lim) break;
        if (on) cur.push(P[i]);
        if (acc >= mark) {
          if (on) { drawStroke(ctx, cur, { ...res(o), jitter: 0.5, p: 1 }, mixSeed(sd, idx++)); }
          on = !on; mark += on ? dash : gap; cur = [P[i]];
        }
      }
      if (on && cur.length > 1) drawStroke(ctx, cur, { ...res(o), jitter: 0.5, p: 1 }, mixSeed(sd, idx));
      return pen;
    },
  };
  return pen;
}

p = '/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/clawd/baby.js'
s = open(p).read()

def rep(old, new, count=1):
    global s
    assert s.count(old) == count, (old[:60], s.count(old))
    s = s.replace(old, new)

# 1. header comment + defaults

# 2. the cap drawing helper, before drawBaby
rep("""export function drawBaby(ctx, opts) {""", """/** The EEG cap: a white cap hugging the top of the head, ink electrodes, wires trailing off. */
function eegCap(ctx, cap, C, r, lw) {
  const o = cap === true ? {} : cap;
  const white = o.color || '#fbfaf6', shade = o.shade || '#ddd7ca', line = C.line, inkc = o.electrode || C.ink;
  const capPath = () => {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-1.31 * r, -0.4 * r);
    ctx.lineTo(-1.32 * r, -0.6 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-1.33 * r, -1.06 * r, -0.78 * r, -1.08 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, -1.22 * r, 0.78 * r, -1.08 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(1.33 * r, -1.06 * r, 1.32 * r, -0.6 * r);
    ctx.lineTo(1.31 * r, -0.4 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, -0.52 * r, -1.31 * r, -0.4 * r);
    ctx.closePath();
  };
  ctx.save();
  capPath(); ctx.fillStyle = white; ctx.fill();
  ctx.save(); capPath(); ctx.clip();
  // a soft shade on the far side and along the rim band
  ctx.fillStyle = rgba(shade, 0.7);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(1.15 * r, -0.62 * r, 0.42 * r, 0.5 * r, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(shade, 1); ctx.lineWidth = 0.07 * r;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-1.4 * r, -0.47 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, -0.6 * r, 1.4 * r, -0.47 * r); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
  capPath(); ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.lineJoin = 'round'; ctx.stroke();
  // the rim band's upper seam
  ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.55;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-1.3 * r, -0.53 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, -0.66 * r, 1.3 * r, -0.53 * r); ctx.stroke();
  // electrodes: ink discs with a pale ring and a glint
  const E = [[-0.98, -0.7], [-0.5, -0.86], [0, -0.92], [0.5, -0.86], [0.98, -0.7], [-0.28, -1.05], [0.28, -1.05], [-0.8, -0.98], [0.8, -0.98]];
  const er = 0.075 * r;
  // wires first (under the electrodes): each runs up from its electrode, the bundle trails off
  if (o.wires !== false) {
    const to = o.to || [1.7, -4.6];
    const sway = o.sway ?? 0;
    const hub = [0.55 * r, -1.55 * r];
    ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    E.forEach(([ex, ey], i) => {
      const x0 = ex * r, y0 = ey * r;
      const hx = hub[0] + (i - 4) * 0.035 * r, hy = hub[1];
      const tx = to[0] * r + (i - 4) * 0.05 * r, ty = to[1] * r + (i - 4) * 0.02 * r;
      ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.42;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x0, y0);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x0 * 0.7 + hx * 0.3, y0 - 0.28 * r, hx, hy);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo(hx + (0.2 + 0.1 * Math.sin(sway)) * r, hy - 0.8 * r, tx - (0.6 + 0.15 * Math.sin(sway + 1)) * r, ty + 1.1 * r, tx, ty);
      ctx.stroke();
    });
  }
  for (const [ex, ey] of E) {
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(shade, 1);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex * r, ey * r, er * 1.45, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = inkc;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex * r, ey * r, er, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', 0.75);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex * r - er * 0.35, ey * r - er * 0.35, er * 0.28, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

export function drawBaby(ctx, opts) {""")

function freeNub(ctx, f, side, root, r, clay, line, lw) {
  const arm = G0.arm;
  const t = arm.t * r;
  if (f.mode === 'down') {
    nub(ctx, root(side), [side * 1.62 * r, 0.82 * r], t, t, clay, line, lw);
  } else if (f.mode === 'wave' || f.mode === 'up') {
    const a = f.angle ?? 0;
    const lift = f.mode === 'wave' ? 0.95 + 0.4 * Math.sin(a) : 1.2 + 0.25 * Math.sin(a);
    const len = 0.62 * r;
    nub(ctx, root(side), [side * (arm.x * r + Math.cos(lift) * len), arm.y * r - Math.sin(lift) * len], t, t * 1.05, clay, line, lw);
  } else if (f.mode === 'point') {
    // the nub coming at the lens: it grows, its flat end toward us
    const k = clamp(f.reach ?? 1);
    const hx = lerp(side * 1.5, side * 0.3, k) * r, hy = lerp(0.9, 1.0, k) * r, hr = lerp(0.2, 0.7, k) * r;
    nub(ctx, root(side), [hx, hy], t, hr * 1.6, clay, line, lw);
    rr(ctx, hx - hr, hy - hr * 0.9, 2 * hr, 1.8 * hr, hr * 0.45);
    ctx.fillStyle = mix(clay, '#ffffff', 0.08); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.stroke();
  } else if (f.mode === 'hold') {
    // the nub reaches the point the scene gives (head-relative units); the scene draws the prop
    const hx = (f.at?.[0] ?? side * 0.6) * r, hy = (f.at?.[1] ?? 1.2) * r;
    nub(ctx, root(side), [hx, hy], t, t * 1.15, clay, line, lw);
  }
}
  // ---- the EEG cap (opt-in) ---------------------------------------------------------------------
  if (o.cap) eegCap(ctx, o.cap, C, r, lw);

  // ---- the phone nub: stretched to the bottom corner, the phone out of frame -------------------
  const root = (k) => [k * arm.x * r, arm.y * r];
  if (o.phone !== false) nub(ctx, root(s), [s * 2.0 * r, 3.6 * r], arm.t * 1.05 * r, arm.t * 2.1 * r, clay, line, lw);
  else freeNub(ctx, o.other, s, root, r, clay, line, lw);

  // ---- the face -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    }
  }
  for (const k of [-1, 1]) eyeAt(ctx, k, o, C, r);
  const brows = o.brows || (o.mouth === 'press' ? 'furrow' : null);
  if (brows) for (const k of [-1, 1]) brow(ctx, k, brows, C, r);
  if (o.tears > 0) tears(ctx, o.tears, C, r);
  if (!(o.pacifier >= 0.5)) mouthAt(ctx, o, C, r);
  if (o.pacifier > 0) pacifier(ctx, o.pacifier, C, r);

  // ---- the free nub ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  freeNub(ctx, o.free, fs, root, r, clay, line, lw);
  ctx.restore();

 * style: 'ink' (opt-in; default is the clay drawing above) draws Clawd as ink line art on

s += """
/** Adult Clawd as ink line art on paper (style: 'ink'). */
function inkAdult(ctx, { x, y, u, pose = 'stand', ink = '#1d1a17', paper = '#f4efe3', pen = null, width = null, hatch = true, p = 1,
  eyes = 'open', eyeColor = null, lean = 0, mouth = null, breath = 0.5, arms = [0.25, 0.25], h = 6, look = null, shine = null }) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.rotate(lean);
  const lw = width ?? Math.max(2, u * 0.17);
  const P = (pen || makePen(ctx, 'adult-clawd', { color: ink })).with({ color: ink, width: lw, wobble: Math.min(2.6, u * 0.22) });
  const H = P.with({ width: Math.max(1, lw * 0.42), wobble: 0.6 });
  const legH = pose === 'stand' ? 2.2 : 0;
  const top = -(h + legH) * u, bot = -legH * u;
  const fillR = (x0, y0, w0, h0, r0) => { rrect(ctx, x0, y0, w0, h0, r0); ctx.fillStyle = paper; ctx.fill(); };
  // arm nubs, behind the body
  if (arms) for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
    const a = arms[k < 0 ? 0 : 1];
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(k * 4.9 * u, top + 3.5 * u);
    ctx.rotate(k < 0 ? a : -a);
    const ax = k < 0 ? -2.2 * u : 0;
    fillR(ax, -0.5 * u, 2.2 * u, u, 0.2 * u);
    P.rrect(ax, -0.5 * u, 2.2 * u, u, 0.2 * u, { p, k: `arm${k}` });
    ctx.restore();
  }
  // legs: hatched (the darker clay)
  for (const lx of [-4, -2, 1, 3]) {
    const r0 = pose === 'stand' ? [lx * u, -2.4 * u, u, 2.4 * u] : [(lx - 0.1) * u, bot - 0.5 * u, 1.2 * u, 1.1 * u];
    fillR(r0[0], r0[1], r0[2], r0[3], 0.15 * u);
    if (hatch) H.hatch(r0, { gap: Math.max(4, u * 0.38), p, k: `leg${lx}` });
    P.rect(r0[0], r0[1], r0[2], r0[3], { p, over: Math.min(3, u * 0.2), k: `legr${lx}` });
  }
  // body: paper, a hatched band low on it, the outline
  fillR(-5 * u, top, 10 * u, h * u, 0.3 * u);
  if (hatch) H.hatch([-5 * u, top + (h === 6 ? 4.2 : h - 1.8) * u, 10 * u, 1.8 * u], { gap: Math.max(4, u * 0.42), angle: -0.9, p, k: 'band' });
  P.rect(-5 * u, top, 10 * u, h * u, { p, over: Math.min(4, u * 0.3), k: 'body' });
  if (p >= 0.6) {
    ctx.globalAlpha *= Math.min(1, (p - 0.6) / 0.25);
    adultFace(ctx, { u, top, ink, eyeColor: eyeColor || ink, eyes, look, shine, mouth, breath, clay: paper, lw: lw * 0.8 });
  }
  ctx.restore();
}
"""

Now the page module (palette, type, paper texture).

BashWrite page.js with palette, fonts and the paper texture
input
description Write page.js with palette, fonts and the paper texture
V=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video; cat > $V/page.js <<'EOF'
// The page: cream lab-notebook stock with a faint 5 mm grid, a deterministic grain and a
// slight darkening toward the edges. It never changes; figures are drawn onto it and
// erased from it. The texture is baked once per output size (a pure function of the size,
// so every worker bakes the same pixels) and blitted every frame.

import { hash, clamp } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { rgba, mix } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';

/** The palette. Only the baby is coloured; everything else is ink on paper. */
export const P = {
  paper: '#f4efe3', grid: '#e6dfcf', ink: '#1d1a17', ink2: '#8a8378', ink3: '#b9b1a3',
  // baby Clawd (the 2am clay family): the one coloured thing on the page
  skin: '#d77757', skinShade: '#b35a3d', skinLight: '#f5b394', mouth: '#4a1f2a', white: '#fff8f0',
  blush: '#f0848a', tear: '#a9d6f2', cloth: '#fbf8f1', clothShade: '#ddd6c8', cap: '#fbfaf6', capShade: '#ddd7ca',
};

export const SERIF = '"Paper Serif", "Iowan Old Style", Georgia, serif';
export const MONO = '"Paper Mono", "SF Mono", Menlo, monospace';
/** Faces declared in index.html (local() system fonts under alias families). */
export const FONTS = [
  { family: 'Paper Serif', weight: 400 }, { family: 'Paper Serif', weight: 400, style: 'italic' },
  { family: 'Paper Serif', weight: 700 }, { family: 'Paper Serif', weight: 700, style: 'italic' },
  { family: 'Paper Mono', weight: 400 }, { family: 'Paper Mono', weight: 700 },
];

/** Grid pitch in design px: 5 mm on a page about 300 mm wide. */
export const GRID_PX = 32;

const cache = new Map();

/**
 * The paper texture for an output of pw x ph pixels showing a W x H design page.
 * Grain is per output pixel (so it survives downscaling), mottling is low-frequency.
 */
export function paperTexture(pw, ph, W, H) {
  const key = `${pw}x${ph}:${W}x${H}`;
  let c = cache.get(key);
  if (c) return c;
  c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = pw; c.height = ph;
  const g = c.getContext('2d');
  const sx = pw / W, sy = ph / H;
  g.fillStyle = P.paper;
  g.fillRect(0, 0, pw, ph);
  // mottling: a coarse random field, smoothly upscaled
  const mw = 64, mh = Math.max(8, Math.round((64 * ph) / pw));
  const m = document.createElement('canvas');
  m.width = mw; m.height = mh;
  const mg = m.getContext('2d');
  const md = mg.createImageData(mw, mh);
  for (let i = 0; i < mw * mh; i++) {
    const v = hash(i, 9001);
    const base = v < 0.5 ? 0 : 255;
    md.data[i * 4] = base; md.data[i * 4 + 1] = base; md.data[i * 4 + 2] = base;
    md.data[i * 4 + 3] = Math.round(Math.abs(v - 0.5) * 2 * 255);
  }
  mg.putImageData(md, 0, 0);
  g.save();
  g.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
  g.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high';
  g.globalAlpha = 0.05;
  g.drawImage(m, 0, 0, pw, ph);
  g.restore();
  // the 5 mm grid, slightly broken up (printed on cheap stock)
  g.save();
  g.scale(sx, sy);
  g.strokeStyle = P.grid;
  g.lineWidth = 1.6;
  g.beginPath();
  const x0 = ((W / 2) % GRID_PX), y0 = ((H / 2) % GRID_PX);
  for (let x = x0; x < W; x += GRID_PX) { g.moveTo(x, 0); g.lineTo(x, H); }
  for (let y = y0; y < H; y += GRID_PX) { g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(W, y); }
  g.stroke();
  g.restore();
  // grain: per-pixel noise, a little warmer in the darks
  const img = g.getImageData(0, 0, pw, ph);
  const d = img.data;
  for (let i = 0, n = pw * ph; i < n; i++) {
    const v = (hash(i, 77) + hash(i, 78) - 1) * 7;   // triangular, about +-7 levels
    d[i * 4] = clamp(d[i * 4] + v, 0, 255);
    d[i * 4 + 1] = clamp(d[i * 4 + 1] + v * 0.96, 0, 255);
    d[i * 4 + 2] = clamp(d[i * 4 + 2] + v * 0.88, 0, 255);
  }
  g.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  // darker toward the edges
  const rg = g.createRadialGradient(pw / 2, ph / 2, Math.min(pw, ph) * 0.35, pw / 2, ph / 2, Math.hypot(pw, ph) * 0.62);
  rg.addColorStop(0, rgba(P.ink, 0));
  rg.addColorStop(0.7, rgba('#6b5a3e', 0.035));
  rg.addColorStop(1, rgba('#6b5a3e', 0.11));
  g.fillStyle = rg;
  g.fillRect(0, 0, pw, ph);
  cache.set(key, c);
  return c;
}

/** Blit the paper (the engine's transform maps design px to output px). */
export function drawPaper(ctx, env) {
  const tex = paperTexture(env.width, env.height, env.W, env.H);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
  ctx.drawImage(tex, 0, 0, env.W, env.H);
  ctx.restore();
}

/** The bridge's ink wash over the paper (k = 0..1 of the 15 percent). */
export function drawWash(ctx, env, k) {
  if (k <= 0) return;
  ctx.fillStyle = rgba(P.ink, 0.15 * clamp(k));
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, env.W, env.H);
}

/** Paper / ink mix (a lighter ink for things that fade to the background). */
export const fadeInk = (k) => mix(P.ink, P.paper, clamp(k));
EOF
echo ok
result
ok

// The rule table: lyric text -> lab figure (as clips.js maps clauses to clip types in the
// 2am song). Patterns test the normalized line text (core/lyrics.js norm(): lowercase,
// punctuation and apostrophes dropped, hyphens become spaces, so "Hirsh-Pasek" is
// "hirsh pasek"). First match wins; only lines in a verse section are tested. A verse line
// that matches nothing continues the previous lab of its section (logged), else it gets the
// 'unmatched' placeholder (logged loudly).
//
// Every lab of all three sheets is listed (the keeper's suno_lyrics_v1_short.txt, the radio
// cut suno_lyrics_v2_radio.txt and the full LYRICS.md): a lab whose lines are all absent from
// the take simply has no window, and the figure numbers after it close up.
//
// Fields: key (= plate id = scenes/labs/<key>.js), title and cite (the caption: "Fig. k.
// <title> (<cite>)"), note (an extra parenthetical after the citation; {key} is the number
// of another figure, and a [bracketed] part is dropped when a figure it names is absent),
// match (patterns), brief (what the real figure shows, from TREATMENT.md).

export const LABS = [
  // ---- Verse 1: Love -------------------------------------------------------------------
  { key: 'feldman', section: 'verse1', title: 'Oxytocin in new mothers and fathers', cite: 'Gordon et al., 2010',
    match: [/\boxytocin/, /\bfeldman/, /identical in dads/, /fatherhood is biological/],
    brief: 'Two curves rising on one chart, "mothers" and "fathers", drawn in step; on "identical in dads" the second snaps onto the first. A cotton swab at the origin.' },
  { key: 'abraham', section: 'verse1', title: 'Amygdala response in primary-caregiving fathers', cite: 'Abraham et al., 2014',
    match: [/gay fathers/, /amygdala/],
    brief: 'Not in the treatment (the oxytocin bullet stops at "dads"): Abraham et al. 2014, 48 gay primary-caregiving fathers, mother-like amygdala. Its own figure because it is its own paper.' },
  { key: 'tronick', section: 'verse1', title: 'We repair', cite: 'Tronick et al., 1978',
    match: [/\btronick/, /emotion stuff/, /froze a mother/, /i smile i bid/, /she comes back/, /we repair/],
    brief: 'Three panels a, b, c: adult Clawd (ink) leans in; on "froze" the face goes flat and a 2:00 timer runs, the baby cycles smile, bid (point), fuss, cry (pale blue tears), hands to mouth; on "Then she comes back" the adult leans back in and the baby settles.' },
  // ---- Verse 2: Crawling -----------------------------------------------------------------
  { key: 'barburoth', section: 'verse2', title: 'Air-stepping over optic flow, and the crawling skateboard', cite: 'Barbu-Roth et al., 2009; Forma et al., 2019',
    match: [/barbu roth/, /dots stream/, /astronaut/, /quadrupeds/, /skateboard/, /crossed the mattress/],
    brief: 'Harness over a floor of streaming dots (perspective grid), legs swim on "I swim", ink astronaut inset on "astronaut"; on "skateboard" the harness lifts away, belly-down on a skateboard across a mattress in plan view, tally marks on "Twice. Three times.", eyes close on "a rest". One figure for both papers (one treatment bullet).' },
  { key: 'lampl', section: 'verse2', title: 'Growth saltation in one infant', cite: 'Lampl, 1992',
    match: [/\blampl/, /terrible at this/, /centimeters overnight/, /sixty three days/, /ninety five percent/, /growth free/, /front page/],
    brief: 'Length vs days: smooth curve in light ink, the data as a staircase; mono row 59.8 / 59.8 / 59.8 / 60.8; a step rises on "two and a half centimeters overnight", a long flat run with a bracket on "sixty-three days of flat"; if the "front page" line is present a masthead stamps in and a speech bubble is struck through. The baby stands on the current step, growing.' },
  { key: 'adolph', section: 'verse2', title: 'The adjustable slope', cite: 'Adolph et al., 2012',
    match: [/\badolph/, /six degrees/, /grant approached/, /steps an hour/, /seventeen falls/, /crawling isnt mandatory/, /bum shuffle/],
    brief: 'A hinged ramp with a protractor and a mono angle readout, +6 deg per "six degrees" to near vertical at "sheer drop", the baby peering over the top; if the Grant line is present an ink toddler goes off the edge on a dotted arc with a "*" footnote; counters to 2,368 /h and 17; on "Crawling isn\'t mandatory" icons for bum-shuffle, bear-crawl, crawl, the baby bum-shuffling.' },
  // ---- Verse 3: Words, Sleep ---------------------------------------------------------------
  { key: 'hirshpasek', section: 'verse3', title: 'Clauses are perceptual units', cite: 'Hirsh-Pasek et al., 1987',
    match: [/hirsh pasek/, /cinderella/, /breathes right/, /know the laws/],
    brief: 'A Cinderella sentence as an ink waveform envelope with inserted pauses (ticks at clause boundaries, a cross mid-clause); two loudspeakers; the baby turns to the natural one. Absent from the radio cut.' },
  { key: 'saffran', section: 'verse3', title: 'Statistical learning of word boundaries', cite: 'Saffran, Aslin & Newport, 1996',
    match: [/\bsaffran/, /pabeecoo/, /golatoo/, /probability one/, /carving speech/],
    brief: 'Syllables pa bee coo go la too scrolling in mono with arcs of transitional probability (1.0 inside words, 0.33 across); a repetition counter to 45; on "I\'m carving speech" boundaries cut in and the words stack.' },
  { key: 'jarvis', section: 'verse3', title: 'Vocal learners', cite: 'Jarvis, 2019',

/** Bridge myths: one journal cover / product per line, each stamped; the HUD takes the n. */

  { key: 'imitation', title: 'Neonatal imitation', cite: 'Meltzoff & Moore, 1977; Oostenbroek et al., 2016', match: [/neonatal imitation/],
    hud: [{ word: 'six', text: 'n = 6 (1977)' }, { word: 'hundred', text: 'n = 106 (2016)' }], stamps: [{ word: 'six', text: 'n = 6 (1977)' }, { word: 'nothing', text: 'n = 106 (2016): nothing' }] },
  { key: 'helper', title: 'The helper and the hinderer, again', cite: 'Hamlin et al., 2007; Lucca et al., 2025', note: '[(cf. Fig. {hamlin})]', match: [/helper and the hinderer/, /manybabies/],
    hud: [{ word: 'hundred', text: 'n = 567 · 37 labs' }], stamps: [{ word: 'nature', text: 'Nature, 2007' }, { word: 'forty nine', text: '49.3 %' }] },
  { key: 'thirtymillion', title: 'Thirty million words', cite: 'Hart & Risley, 1995; Sperry et al., 2019', match: [/thirty million words/],
    hud: [{ word: 'forty two', text: 'n = 42 · 1 h / month' }], stamps: [{ word: 'forty two', text: 'n = 42 · 1 h / month' }] },
];

/** Section figures that are not labs. */
export const SECTION_FIGS = {
  bench: { title: 'Experimental apparatus', cite: 'this study' },
  replication: { title: 'Replication by division', cite: 'n = 1' },
  benchFinal: { title: 'Experimental apparatus, again', cite: 'this study' },
  replicationFinal: { title: 'Sixteen replications of one result', cite: 'this study' },
  brain: { title: 'Brain growth in year one', cite: 'Knickmeyer et al., 2008; Huttenlocher & Dabholkar, 1997' },
};

export const LAB = Object.fromEntries(LABS.map((l) => [l.key, l]));
export const MYTH = Object.fromEntries(MYTHS.map((m) => [m.key, m]));

/** The lab a verse line belongs to by its own text (null: no rule matched). */
export function labOf(line) {
  for (const lab of LABS) if (lab.match.some((re) => re.test(line.n))) return lab.key;
  return null;
}

/** The bridge myth a line names (null: not a myth line, e.g. "Now the part you skip"). */
export function mythOf(line) {
  for (const m of MYTHS) if (m.match.some((re) => re.test(line.n))) return m.key;
  return null;
}

/** Chorus lines from the first "Will it replicate?" on are the replication figure. */
export const isReplicateLine = (line) => /^will it replicate/.test(line.n);

// LYRICS.md: lines starting with "> " under **[Section]** headers
function lyricsMd() {
  const out = []; let sec = '';
  for (const r of fs.readFileSync(B + 'LYRICS.md', 'utf8').split('\n')) {

    if (r.startsWith('> ')) out.push({ text: r.slice(2).replace(/\*/g, ''), section: sec });
  }
  return out;
}

Every verse line in all three sheets matches its lab explicitly. Now edit.js: windows, figure numbering, cross-references, the loop tail.

BashWrite edit.js: plan, windows, figure numbering, loop tail
input
description Write edit.js: plan, windows, figure numbering, loop tail
V=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video; cat > $V/edit.js <<'EOF'
// The edit: windows (plates) and figures, both derived from lyric content.
//
// A window is what the engine draws (one plate per window). A figure is what the caption
// numbers: a verse lab is one window and one figure; a chorus is one window with two figures
// (the apparatus bench, then the replication); the bridge is one window with a figure per
// myth; the drop is one figure; the final chorus is the bench and the grid. The title page,
// the break and the outro have no figure. Figures are numbered in song order over the lines
// present in the take, so a lab the take drops (the radio cut) has no window and every
// number after it closes up; cross-references in captions ({key}) resolve after numbering.

import { LAB, MYTH, SECTION_FIGS, labOf, mythOf, isReplicateLine } from './labs.js';

/** Plates that exist only because of a line's section id (render.mjs lists them, MISSING if absent). */
export const SECTION_PLATES = [
  { label: 'intro title', section: 'intro', plate: 'title' },
  { label: 'chorus bench', section: 'chorus1', plate: 'chorus' },
  { label: 'bridge', section: 'bridge', plate: 'bridge' },
  { label: 'break', section: 'break', plate: 'blank' },
  { label: 'drop', section: 'drop', plate: 'drop' },
  { label: 'final grid', section: 'final', plate: 'final' },
  { label: 'outro page', section: 'outro', plate: 'outro' },
  { label: 'outro loop', section: 'outro', plate: 'title' },
];

export const LEAD = 0.2;          // a window / figure opens this long before its first word
export const LEGEND_LEAD = 0.25, LEGEND_HOLD = 0.6, LEGEND_FADE = 0.14;

const KIND = (sec) => (sec === 'intro' ? 'title' : /^verse/.test(sec) ? 'lab' : /^chorus/.test(sec) ? 'chorus'
  : sec === 'bridge' ? 'bridge' : sec === 'break' ? 'blank' : sec === 'drop' ? 'drop' : sec === 'final' ? 'final'
    : sec === 'outro' ? 'outro' : 'lab');

/** Resolve "{key}" figure references; drop a [bracketed] part that names an absent figure. */
export function resolveRefs(s, num) {
  if (!s) return '';
  const one = (x) => x.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (_, k) => (num[k] != null ? String(num[k]) : '\u0000'));
  return s.replace(/\[([^\]]*)\]/g, (_, inner) => { const r = one(inner); return r.includes('\u0000') ? '' : r; })
    .replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (_, k) => (num[k] != null ? String(num[k]) : '?')).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
}

/**
 * The plan: groups of consecutive lines (one per window), the figures and the loop tail.
 * Pure function of the lyrics and the beat grid (the timeline and story.js both build it).
 */
export function makePlan(L, B, { quiet = false } = {}) {
  const warn = (m) => { if (!quiet && typeof console !== 'undefined') console.warn(m); };
  const groups = [];
  let prevLab = null, prevSec = null;
  const unmatched = [], inherited = [];
  for (const l of L.lines) {
    const sec = l.section || '';
    const kind = KIND(sec);
    let key = kind;
    if (kind === 'lab') {
      if (sec !== prevSec) prevLab = null;
      let k = /^verse/.test(sec) ? labOf(l) : null;
      if (!k && prevLab) { k = prevLab; inherited.push(`${l.text} -> ${k}`); }
      if (!k) { k = 'unmatched'; unmatched.push(`[${sec || 'no section'}] ${l.text}`); }
      key = k; prevLab = k;
    } else if (kind === 'chorus') key = sec;
    prevSec = sec;
    const g = groups.at(-1);
    if (g && g.kind === kind && g.key === key && g.section === sec) g.lines.push(l);
    else groups.push({ kind, key, section: sec, lines: [l] });
  }
  if (inherited.length) warn(`[labs] ${inherited.length} verse line(s) matched no rule and continue the previous lab:\n  ${inherited.join('\n  ')}`);
  if (unmatched.length) warn(`[labs] ${unmatched.length} line(s) matched no lab rule and use the 'unmatched' placeholder:\n  ${unmatched.join('\n  ')}`);

  // ---- windows ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const entries = [];
  const title = groups.find((g) => g.kind === 'title');
  entries.push({ plate: 'title', at: { time: 0 }, params: { loop: false, lines: (title?.lines || []).map((l) => l.k) },
    ...(title ? { fromSection: 'intro title' } : {}), hero: (w) => w.start + Math.min(w.dur * 0.85, 12) });
  let prevEnd = 0;
  for (const g of groups) {
    const first = g.lines[0], last = g.lines.at(-1);
    const lines = g.lines.map((l) => l.k);
    const at = (t, why) => ({ t: Math.max(t, 0.05), why });
    if (g.kind === 'title') { prevEnd = last.end; continue; }
    let e;
    if (g.kind === 'lab') e = { plate: g.key, params: { lab: g.key, lines }, at: at(first.start - LEAD, `${g.key}: ${first.text}`), hero: 0.75 };
    else if (g.kind === 'chorus') e = { plate: 'chorus', params: { section: g.section, lines }, at: at(first.start - LEAD, g.section), fromSection: 'chorus bench', hero: 0.4 };
    else if (g.kind === 'bridge') e = { plate: 'bridge', params: { lines }, at: at(first.start - LEAD, 'bridge'), fromSection: 'bridge', hero: 0.3 };
    else if (g.kind === 'blank') e = { plate: 'blank', params: { lines }, at: at(first.start - LEAD, 'break'), fromSection: 'break', hero: 0.5 };
    else if (g.kind === 'drop') e = { plate: 'drop', params: { lines }, at: at(first.start - LEAD, 'drop'), fromSection: 'drop', hero: 0.55 };
    else if (g.kind === 'final') e = { plate: 'final', params: { lines }, at: at(first.start - LEAD, 'final chorus'), fromSection: 'final grid', hero: 0.97 };
    else if (g.kind === 'outro') e = { plate: 'outro', params: { lines }, at: at(Math.max(prevEnd + 0.4, first.start - 1.2), 'outro'), fromSection: 'outro page', hero: (w) => (w.lines[0] ? w.lines[0].end : w.start + 2) };
    e.snap = 'none';
    entries.push(e);
    prevEnd = last.end;
  }

  // ---- the loop tail: outro frames that are intro frames -------------------------------
  // After the last outro line has left the legend, the song shows only paper and the HUD;
  // so does the intro before its title draws in. The tail repeats the intro's head frame by
  // frame. Its shift is a whole number of beats (the intro's beat-locked heartbeat trace
  // lands on the tail's beats), rounded to frames in story.js.
  const outroG = groups.filter((g) => g.kind === 'outro').at(-1);
  let loop = null;
  if (outroG) {
    const clear = outroG.lines.at(-1).end + LEGEND_HOLD + LEGEND_FADE + 0.05;
    const b0 = B.beatTime(0);
    let m = Math.ceil(B.beatIndex(clear + b0) - 1e-9);
    let shift = B.beatTime(m) - b0;
    if (!(shift < B.duration - 0.2)) shift = clear;           // no room for a beat-aligned tail
    if (shift < B.duration - 0.2) {
      loop = { start: shift, shift };
      entries.push({ plate: 'title', params: { loop: true, lines: [] }, at: { t: shift, why: 'outro loop' }, snap: 'none', fromSection: 'outro loop', hero: 0.5 });
    }
  }

  // ---- figures ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const figs = [];
  const add = (f) => { figs.push(f); return f; };
  const fEntry = (g) => entries.find((e) => e.params?.lines?.[0] === g.lines[0].k);
  for (const g of groups) {
    const e = fEntry(g);
    if (!e) continue;
    const t0 = e.at.t;
    if (g.kind === 'lab') {
      const lab = LAB[g.key] || { key: g.key, title: 'Unmatched line', cite: 'no rule in labs.js' };
      add({ key: g.key, kind: 'lab', title: lab.title, cite: lab.cite, note: lab.note || '', start: t0, lines: g.lines });
    } else if (g.kind === 'chorus' || g.kind === 'final') {
      const fin = g.kind === 'final';
      const ri = g.lines.findIndex(isReplicateLine);
      const bench = ri < 0 ? g.lines : g.lines.slice(0, ri), repl = ri < 0 ? [] : g.lines.slice(ri);
      if (bench.length) add({ key: `bench@${g.section}`, kind: 'bench', ...(fin ? SECTION_FIGS.benchFinal : SECTION_FIGS.bench), start: t0, lines: bench });
      if (repl.length) add({ key: `replication@${g.section}`, kind: 'replication', ...(fin ? SECTION_FIGS.replicationFinal : SECTION_FIGS.replication), start: bench.length ? repl[0].start - LEAD : t0, lines: repl });
    } else if (g.kind === 'bridge') {
      for (const l of g.lines) {
        const k = mythOf(l);
        if (!k) continue;
        const m = MYTH[k];
        add({ key: k, kind: 'myth', title: m.title, cite: m.cite, note: m.note || '', start: l.start - LEAD, lines: [l] });
      }
    } else if (g.kind === 'drop') {
      add({ key: 'brain', kind: 'drop', ...SECTION_FIGS.brain, start: t0, lines: g.lines });
    }
  }
  // a figure runs until the next figure or the end of its window
  const wStarts = entries.map((e) => e.at.t ?? 0).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  figs.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
  figs.forEach((f, i) => {
    const nextFig = figs[i + 1]?.start ?? Infinity;
    const nextWin = wStarts.find((s) => s > f.start + 1e-6) ?? B.duration;
    f.end = Math.min(nextFig, nextWin);
  });
  // numbers: in order; a lab that recurs keeps its first number
  const num = {};
  let n = 0;
  for (const f of figs) { if (num[f.key] == null) num[f.key] = ++n; f.num = num[f.key]; }
  for (const f of figs) { f.title = resolveRefs(f.title, num); f.cite = resolveRefs(f.cite, num); f.note = resolveRefs(f.note, num); }
  return { groups, entries, figs, num, loop, inherited, unmatched };
}

/** Timeline spec function: the engine calls it with the parsed lyrics and the beat grid. */
export function timeline({ lyrics, beats }) {
  const plan = makePlan(lyrics, beats);
  const got = SECTION_PLATES.map((sp) => ({ ...sp, e: plan.entries.find((e) => e.fromSection === sp.label) }));
  const lost = got.filter((g) => !g.e);
  if (lost.length) {
    const have = [...new Set(lyrics.lines.map((l) => l.section))].join(', ');
    console.warn(`[edit] MISSING section plate(s): ${lost.map((g) => `${g.label} (no line in section "${g.section}")`).join('; ')}. Line sections: ${have}`);
  }
  return plan.entries;
}

description Write story.js with loop, figures, HUD, heartbeat and rule curves
V=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video; cat > $V/story.js <<'EOF'
// Song-level curves, all derived from lyric content and the beat grid:
//   loopTime(t)   tail frames map frame-exactly onto intro frames (the video loops)
//   figureAt(t)   the figure the caption shows (number, title, citation)
//   hud(t)        the HUD text: n = 1, the bridge's sample sizes, n = ? in the break, the drop's roll
//   heart         the heartbeat trace: heart time h(t), the beats, the sweep
//   ruleP(t)      how much of the caption rule is drawn (it draws in with the title, erases with it)

import { clamp, seg, hash } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { makePlan, LEGEND_LEAD } from './edit.js';
import { MYTH } from './labs.js';

export function makeStory(env) {
  const { L, B, T } = env;
  const plan = makePlan(L, B, { quiet: true });
  const W = T.windows;
  const intro = W[0];
  const tail = W.find((w) => w.plate === 'title' && w.params.loop) || null;
  const fps = env.fps || 30;
  const D = B.duration;

  // ---- the loop ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const shiftFrames = tail ? Math.round(tail.start * fps) - Math.round(intro.start * fps) : 0;
  function loopTime(t) {
    if (!tail || t < tail.start) return t;
    return Math.max(intro.start, (Math.round(t * fps) - shiftFrames) / fps);
  }
  const windowFor = (t) => (tail && t >= tail.start ? intro : T.windowAt(t));
  /** The intro's head (what the tail repeats) shows only paper and the HUD until introOpen. */
  const introLines = intro.lines || [];
  const firstWord = introLines[0]?.words?.[0]?.start ?? 1.0;
  const headEnd = tail ? D - tail.start : 0;
  const introOpen = Math.max(firstWord - 0.15, headEnd + 0.05);
  if (tail && firstWord - LEGEND_LEAD < headEnd) console.warn(`[story] the loop tail (${headEnd.toFixed(2)} s of intro head) reaches the intro legend (${(firstWord - LEGEND_LEAD).toFixed(2)} s): the song's last frames show the first lyric`);

  // ---- figures ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const figs = plan.figs;
  function figureAt(t) {
    let cur = null;
    for (const f of figs) if (f.start <= t + 1e-9 && t < f.end) cur = f;
    return cur;
  }
  const prevFigure = (f) => (f ? figs[figs.indexOf(f) - 1] || null : null);

  // ---- outro: the title block erases on "n equals one" -------------------------------------
  const outroW = W.find((w) => w.plate === 'outro') || null;
  const nLine = outroW ? (outroW.lines.find((l) => /^n equals one/.test(l.n)) || outroW.lines.at(-1)) : null;
  const outroFade = nLine ? { a: nLine.start, b: nLine.end + 0.45 } : { a: Infinity, b: Infinity };

  // ---- the heartbeat trace -------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Heart time runs through the intro, pauses for the song, and continues in the outro, so the
  // outro's trace picks up where the intro's left off. The beats are the beat grid (audio.json
  // has no heartbeat onsets; the take's intro heartbeat is not separable from the voice).
  const introEnd = W[1] ? W[1].start : intro.end;
  const outroDur = outroW ? (tail ? tail.start : D) - outroW.start : 0;
  const hSpan = introEnd + outroDur;
  function heartTime(t) {
    const w = windowFor(t);
    if (w === intro) return clamp(t, 0, introEnd);
    if (outroW && w === outroW) return introEnd + (t - outroW.start);
    return null;
  }
  const heartBeats = [];
  for (const b of B.beats) if (b <= introEnd) heartBeats.push(b);
  if (outroW) for (const b of B.beats) if (b >= outroW.start && b < outroW.start + outroDur) heartBeats.push(introEnd + (b - outroW.start));
  const heart = { introEnd, outroDur, hSpan, beats: heartBeats, time: heartTime };

  // ---- the caption rule ----------------------------------------------------------------------
  function ruleP(t) {
    const w = windowFor(t);
    if (w === intro) return seg(t, introOpen, introOpen + 0.7);
    if (outroW && w === outroW) return 1 - seg(t, outroFade.a, outroFade.b);
    return 1;
  }
  /** Title-page elements (title block, trace, quote, baby) in the outro: 1 -> 0 on "n equals one". */
  const outroAlpha = (t) => 1 - seg(t, outroFade.a, outroFade.b);

  // ---- the HUD --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const bridgeW = W.find((w) => w.plate === 'bridge') || null;
  const breakW = W.find((w) => w.plate === 'blank') || null;
  const dropW = W.find((w) => w.plate === 'drop') || null;
  const events = [];   // { t, text, until? } in the bridge
  if (bridgeW) {
    for (const f of figs.filter((x) => x.kind === 'myth')) {
      const line = f.lines[0];
      for (const h of MYTH[f.key].hud || []) {
        const wd = L.findWord(line, h.word);
        if (!wd) continue;
        events.push({ t: wd.start, text: h.text, until: h.beats ? wd.start + h.beats * B.beatDur : null });
      }
    }
    events.sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t);
  }
  const persistent = events.filter((e) => !e.until).map((e) => e.text);
  const askLine = bridgeW ? bridgeW.lines.find((l) => /^so when you ask/.test(l.n)) : null;
  const replWord = dropW ? (dropW.lines.map((l) => L.findWord(l, 'replication')).find(Boolean) || null) : null;
  const dropSettle = dropW ? (replWord ? replWord.start : dropW.start + dropW.dur * 0.6) : Infinity;
  function hud(t) {
    const w = windowFor(t);
    if (bridgeW && w === bridgeW) {
      if (askLine && t >= askLine.start && persistent.length) {
        // the myths' sample sizes in a fast shuffle
        const i = Math.floor((t - askLine.start) * 8);
        return persistent[Math.floor(hash(i, 4242) * persistent.length)];
      }
      let text = 'n = 1';
      for (const e of events) if (e.t <= t && (!e.until || t < e.until)) text = e.text;
      return text;
    }
    if (breakW && w === breakW) return 'n = ?';
    if (dropW && w === dropW && t < dropSettle) {
      // rolling digits, slowing down into the settle
      const left = dropSettle - t;
      const rate = left < 0.6 ? 4 + 16 * (left / 0.6) : 20;
      const i = Math.floor(t * rate);
      const digits = 1 + Math.floor(hash(i, 515) * 6);
      const v = Math.floor(hash(i, 616) * 10 ** digits);
      return `n = ${v.toLocaleString('en-US')}`;
    }
    return 'n = 1';
  }

  return {
    plan, figs, num: plan.num, loopTime, loopShift: shiftFrames / fps, windowFor, intro, tail, introOpen, headEnd,
    figureAt, prevFigure, heart, ruleP, outroW, outroFade, outroAlpha, hud, bridgeW, breakW, dropW, askLine, dropSettle, events,
  };
}

V=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video; cat > $V/legend.js <<'EOF'
// The lyric legend: the current lyric line set in the serif under the figure caption, as a
// figure legend. Left-aligned, at most `rows` rows (two in 16:9), rows breaking at a clause
// (sentence end, colon, semicolon, comma, or before a parenthetical), never below minSize.
// Unsung words are the light ink and turn full ink as they are sung: a wipe through the
// active word, per syllable when the aligner gave syllables (core/karaoke.js wordProgress).
// Parentheticals (backing vocals) are italic and smaller. Pure function of t.

import { clamp } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { font } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
import { wordProgress } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/karaoke.js';
import { P, SERIF } from './page.js';
import { LEGEND_LEAD, LEGEND_HOLD, LEGEND_FADE } from './edit.js';

export const LEGEND = {
  x: 96, y: 968, maxW: 1728, size: 40, minSize: 34, rowGap: 1.24, rows: 2, backingScale: 0.84,
  lead: LEGEND_LEAD, hold: LEGEND_HOLD, fade: LEGEND_FADE, family: SERIF,
};

/** Typographic form of a lyric word: curly quotes and apostrophes, an em dash for "--". */
export function typo(w) {
  return String(w)
    .replace(/^"/, '“').replace(/"(?=[.,;:!?)]*$)/, '”').replace(/"/g, '”')
    .replace(/^'(?=\w)/, '‘').replace(/'/g, '’')
    .replace(/--/g, '—');
}

// break penalty after a word ending in this punctuation (lower is a better place to wrap)
function breakCost(word, next) {
  if (next && /^\(/.test(next.w)) return 0.05;
  const s = String(word.w).replace(/["'”’)]+$/, '');
  if (/[.?!]$/.test(s)) return 0;
  if (/[:;]$/.test(s)) return 0.15;
  if (/,$/.test(s)) return 0.35;
  if (/—$|-$/.test(s)) return 0.5;
  return 1.0;
}

function measure(ctx, words, size, o) {
  const fr = font(400, size, o.family), fb = font(400, size * o.backingScale, o.family, 'italic');
  ctx.font = fr;
  const space = ctx.measureText(' ').width;
  const ws = words.map((w) => { ctx.font = w.backing ? fb : fr; return ctx.measureText(typo(w.w)).width; });
  return { ws, space, fr, fb };
}

/** Best split of the words into <= R rows of <= maxW (dynamic programming over break points). */
function split(words, m, maxW, R) {
  const n = words.length;
  const rowW = (i, j) => { let s = 0; for (let k = i; k < j; k++) s += m.ws[k] + (k > i ? m.space : 0); return s; };
  const best = Array.from({ length: n + 1 }, () => new Array(R + 1).fill(Infinity));
  const from = Array.from({ length: n + 1 }, () => new Array(R + 1).fill(-1));
  best[0][0] = 0;
  for (let j = 1; j <= n; j++) {
    for (let r = 1; r <= R; r++) {
      for (let i = j - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const w = rowW(i, j);
        if (w > maxW && j - i > 1) break;
        if (!isFinite(best[i][r - 1])) continue;
        const last = j === n;
        const slack = last ? 0 : 0.35 * ((maxW - w) / maxW) ** 2;
        const c = best[i][r - 1] + 0.6 + slack + (last ? 0 : breakCost(words[j - 1], words[j])) + (w > maxW ? 50 : 0);
        if (c < best[j][r]) { best[j][r] = c; from[j][r] = i; }
      }
    }
  }
  let r = 1, bc = Infinity;
  for (let k = 1; k <= R; k++) if (best[n][k] < bc) { bc = best[n][k]; r = k; }
  if (!isFinite(bc)) return null;
  const rows = [];
  let j = n;
  for (let k = r; k >= 1; k--) { const i = from[j][k]; rows.unshift([i, j]); j = i; }
  return { rows, cost: bc };
}

const layoutCache = new Map();
/** Rows and sizes for a line (cached per line and options; depends only on the text). */
export function layoutLegend(ctx, line, o) {
  const key = `${line.k}|${o.maxW}|${o.size}|${o.minSize}|${o.rows}`;
  let L = layoutCache.get(key);
  if (L) return L;
  let pick = null;
  for (let size = o.size; size >= o.minSize - 1e-6; size -= 1) {
    const m = measure(ctx, line.words, size, o);
    const s = split(line.words, m, o.maxW, o.rows);
    if (!s) continue;
    const score = s.cost + 0.12 * (o.size - size);
    if (!pick || score < pick.score - 1e-9) pick = { ...s, size, m, score };
  }
  if (!pick) {
    const size = o.minSize, m = measure(ctx, line.words, size, o);
    pick = { ...(split(line.words, m, o.maxW, 12) || { rows: [[0, line.words.length]] }), size, m, score: 99 };
  }
  L = { rows: pick.rows, size: pick.size, ws: pick.m.ws, space: pick.m.space, fr: pick.m.fr, fb: pick.m.fb };
  layoutCache.set(key, L);
  return L;
}

/**
 * Draw the legend for time t. lyrics = makeLyrics() object; opts override LEGEND (x, y,
 * maxW, rows, alpha, colors). Returns the block's box or null.
 */
export function drawLegend(ctx, t, lyrics, opts = {}) {
  const o = { ...LEGEND, ...opts };
  const line = lyrics.displayLine(t, o.lead, o.hold);
  if (!line || !line.words.length) return null;
  const fadeIn = clamp((t - (line.start - o.lead)) / o.fade);
  const fadeOut = clamp((line.end + o.hold - t) / o.fade);
  const alpha = Math.min(fadeIn, fadeOut) * (o.alpha ?? 1);
  if (alpha <= 0.001) return null;
  const L = layoutLegend(ctx, line, o);
  const ink = o.ink || P.ink, light = o.light || P.ink2;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  const rowH = L.size * o.rowGap;
  let maxX = 0;
  L.rows.forEach(([i, j], r) => {
    let x = o.x;
    const y = o.y + r * rowH;
    for (let k = i; k < j; k++) {
      const w = line.words[k];
      const txt = typo(w.w);
      ctx.font = w.backing ? L.fb : L.fr;
      const p = wordProgress(w, t);
      const ww = L.ws[k];
      ctx.fillStyle = p >= 1 ? ink : light;
      ctx.fillText(txt, x, y);
      if (p > 0 && p < 1) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.rect(x - 2, y - L.size * 1.1, (ww + 4) * p, L.size * 1.5);
        ctx.clip();
        ctx.fillStyle = ink;
        ctx.fillText(txt, x, y);
        ctx.restore();
      }
      x += ww + L.space;
    }
    maxX = Math.max(maxX, x - L.space);
  });
  ctx.restore();
  return { x: o.x, y: o.y - L.size, w: maxX - o.x, h: rowH * L.rows.length, rows: L.rows.length, size: L.size };
}
EOF
cat > $V/caption.js <<'EOF'
// The figure caption: a rule across the page, then "Fig. k." in italic serif, the title in
// roman, the citation (and any note) in the lighter ink. It changes with every figure: the
// old one fades, the new one is written in left to right. One row in 16:9 (shrinks to fit),
// up to two in 9:16.

import { clamp, seg, ease } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { font } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
import { P, SERIF } from './page.js';

export const CAPTION = { x: 96, ruleY: 864, y: 912, maxW: 1728, size: 34, minSize: 27, rows: 1, rowGap: 1.25, family: SERIF };

const endPunct = (s) => /[.?!]$/.test(s);

/** The caption's runs: [{ text, style, color }]. */
export function captionRuns(fig) {
  const title = fig.title || '';
  const runs = [
    { text: `Fig. ${fig.num}.`, style: 'italic', color: P.ink },
    { text: ` ${title}${endPunct(title) ? '' : '.'}`, style: 'normal', color: P.ink },
  ];
  if (fig.cite) runs.push({ text: ` (${fig.cite}).`, style: 'normal', color: P.ink2 });
  if (fig.note) runs.push({ text: ` ${fig.note}`, style: 'normal', color: P.ink2 });
  return runs;
}

function layoutRuns(ctx, runs, o) {
  // split runs into word tokens so a two-row caption can wrap
  const toks = [];
  for (const r of runs) for (const m of r.text.match(/\s*\S+/g) || []) toks.push({ ...r, text: m });
  for (let size = o.size; size >= o.minSize - 1e-6; size -= 0.5) {
    const ws = toks.map((tk) => { ctx.font = font(400, size, o.family, tk.style === 'italic' ? 'italic' : ''); return ctx.measureText(tk.text).width; });
    const rows = [];
    let cur = [], w = 0;
    toks.forEach((tk, i) => {
      if (cur.length && w + ws[i] > o.maxW) { rows.push(cur); cur = []; w = 0; }
      cur.push(i); w += ws[i];
    });
    if (cur.length) rows.push(cur);
    if (rows.length <= o.rows || size - 0.5 < o.minSize) return { toks, ws, rows, size };
  }
}

function drawRuns(ctx, fig, o, reveal, alpha) {
  if (!fig || alpha <= 0) return;
  const runs = captionRuns(fig);
  const Lr = layoutRuns(ctx, runs, o);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  const total = Lr.rows.reduce((a, r) => a + r.reduce((b, i) => b + Lr.ws[i], 0), 0);
  let shown = reveal * total;
  Lr.rows.forEach((row, ri) => {
    const y = o.y + ri * Lr.size * o.rowGap;
    let x = o.x;
    const rw = row.reduce((b, i) => b + Lr.ws[i], 0);
    const vis = clamp(shown, 0, rw);
    shown -= rw;
    if (vis <= 0) return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(o.x - 4, y - Lr.size * 1.2, vis + 4, Lr.size * 1.6); ctx.clip();
    for (const i of row) {
      const tk = Lr.toks[i];
      ctx.font = font(400, Lr.size, o.family, tk.style === 'italic' ? 'italic' : '');
      ctx.fillStyle = tk.color;
      ctx.fillText(tk.text, x, y);
      x += Lr.ws[i];
    }
    ctx.restore();
  });
  ctx.restore();
}

/**
 * Draw the caption for figure `fig` at t (null: no figure, only the rule). `prev` is the
 * figure before it (fades out as `fig` is written in). pen draws the rule; ruleP = drawn fraction.
 */
export function drawCaption(ctx, t, fig, prev, { pen, ruleP = 1, alpha = 1, ...opts } = {}) {
  const o = { ...CAPTION, ...opts };
  if (ruleP > 0 && pen) pen.line(o.x, o.ruleY, o.x + o.maxW, o.ruleY, { p: ruleP, width: 2.2, wobble: 1.6, k: 'caption-rule' });
  if (!fig) return;
  const t0 = fig.start;
  if (prev && prev.end >= t0 - 1e-6 && t < t0 + 0.2) drawRuns(ctx, prev, o, 1, alpha * (1 - seg(t, t0, t0 + 0.16)));
  drawRuns(ctx, fig, o, ease.outCubic(seg(t, t0 + 0.1, t0 + 0.65)), alpha);
}
EOF
cat > $V/hud.js <<'EOF'
// The HUD, top-left: the sample size in mono in a thin hand-ruled ink box. n = 1 for the
// whole song except the bridge (each myth's n as it is named), the break (n = ?) and the
// drop (rolling digits that settle back to n = 1). story.js decides the text.

import { font } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
import { P, MONO } from './page.js';

export const HUD = { x: 64, y: 42, size: 30, padX: 16, padY: 11 };

export function drawHud(ctx, text, pen, opts = {}) {
  const o = { ...HUD, ...opts };
  ctx.save();
  ctx.font = font(400, o.size, MONO);
  const tw = ctx.measureText(text).width;
  const w = tw + o.padX * 2, h = o.size + o.padY * 2;
  pen.rect(o.x, o.y, w, h, { width: 1.7, wobble: 0.9, over: 2.5, k: 'hud' });
  ctx.fillStyle = P.ink;
  ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.fillText(text, o.x + o.padX, o.y + o.padY + o.size * 0.8);
  ctx.restore();
  return { x: o.x, y: o.y, w, h };
}

V=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video; sed -i '' 's/const score = s.cost + 0.12 \* (o.size - size);/const score = s.cost + 0.2 * (o.size - size);/' $V/legend.js && grep -n "0.2 \* (o.size" $V/legend.js && cat > $V/kit.js <<'EOF'
// The plate kit: drawing helpers shared by every plate (section plates and lab figures).
// FROZEN for the second pass: lab agents use these and do not edit this file; a helper only
// one lab needs lives in that lab's own file. All helpers are pure (same input, same pixels).

import { clamp, lerp, ease, seg, hash, TAU } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { mix, rgba } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';
import { font } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
import { makePen } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/ink.js';
import { drawBaby, BABY_GEOM } from '../../../shared/clawd/baby.js';
import { adultClawd } from '../../../shared/clawd/adult.js';
import { P, SERIF, MONO } from './page.js';

export { P, SERIF, MONO, makePen, BABY_GEOM };

/** The page (16:9 design px). Plates draw in these coordinates in every layout. */
export const PAGE = { W: 1920, H: 1080 };
/** The figure area: everything a figure draws stays inside it. */
export const AREA = { x: 120, y: 112, w: 1680, h: 712 };
/** The caption rule (the legend and caption sit below it). */
export const RULE_Y = 864;

// ---- the baby ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Baby colours on paper: k = 1 the clay baby (the one coloured thing), k = 0 an ink outline copy. */
export function babyColors(k = 1) {
  const c = (clayHex, inkHex) => (k >= 1 ? clayHex : k <= 0 ? inkHex : mix(inkHex, clayHex, k));
  return {
    skin: c(P.skin, P.paper), skinShade: c(P.skinShade, P.paper), skinLight: c(P.skinLight, P.paper),
    ink: P.ink, line: P.ink, mouth: c(P.mouth, P.ink), white: c(P.white, P.paper),
    cloth: c(P.cloth, P.paper), clothShade: c(P.clothShade, P.paper),
    blush: c(P.blush, P.paper), tear: P.tear, paci: P.ink2, paciRing: P.paper, towel: P.paper, stain: P.paper,
  };
}

/**
 * Baby Clawd on the page: no phone (both nubs free), the EEG cap on by default, clay
 * colours (o.ink = 1 draws an ink outline copy instead; between 0 and 1 the clay fills in).
 * (o.x, o.y) is the face centre, o.r the scale (the body is 2.5r wide; the feet are 1.9r
 * below the face centre, the cap top about 1.2r above it). Every drawBaby option works:
 * eyes, open, look, mouth, mouthOpen, brows, tears, blush, free, other, tilt, outfit, cap.
 */
export function drawPaperBaby(ctx, o) {
  const k = o.ink != null ? 1 - clamp(o.ink) : 1;
  const cap = o.cap === undefined ? { color: k >= 1 ? P.cap : mix(P.paper, P.cap, k), shade: k > 0 ? P.capShade : P.paper, electrode: P.ink } : o.cap;
  drawBaby(ctx, { outfit: 'naked', ...o, C: o.C || babyColors(k), phone: false, cap: cap || null });
}
/** Where the baby's feet touch the ground, for a face centre y and scale r. */
export const babyFeetY = (y, r) => y + (BABY_GEOM.body.b + BABY_GEOM.legH) * r;
/** Face centre y for a baby standing with its feet at groundY. */
export const babyStandY = (groundY, r) => groundY - (BABY_GEOM.body.b + BABY_GEOM.legH) * r;

/** A deterministic blink: 1 (open) or a short close every few seconds, seeded per baby. */
export function blink(t, seed = 0) {
  const period = 3.1 + 1.7 * hash(seed, 3);
  const ph = ((t + hash(seed, 4) * period) % period + period) % period;
  return ph < 0.13 ? Math.abs(ph - 0.065) / 0.065 : 1;
}

/** Adult Clawd in ink line art (shared/clawd/adult.js style: 'ink'). */
export function drawInkAdult(ctx, o) {
  adultClawd(ctx, { style: 'ink', ink: P.ink, paper: P.paper, eyes: 'open', ...o });
}

// ---- type -------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Set a line of type. o: size (32), style ('normal' | 'italic'), weight (400), mono, color
 * (ink), align ('left' | 'center' | 'right'), alpha, track (letter spacing, em), reveal
 * (0..1 left-to-right wipe, for type that is written in). Returns the width.
 */
export function text(ctx, s, x, y, o = {}) {
  const size = o.size ?? 32;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.font = font(o.weight ?? 400, size, o.mono ? MONO : SERIF, o.style === 'italic' ? 'italic' : '');
  if (o.track) ctx.letterSpacing = `${(o.track * size).toFixed(2)}px`;
  ctx.fillStyle = o.color ?? P.ink;
  ctx.globalAlpha *= o.alpha ?? 1;
  ctx.textAlign = o.align ?? 'left';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  const w = ctx.measureText(s).width;
  if (o.reveal != null && o.reveal < 1) {
    if (o.reveal <= 0) { ctx.restore(); return w; }
    const x0 = o.align === 'center' ? x - w / 2 : o.align === 'right' ? x - w : x;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(x0 - 4, y - size * 1.3, (w + 8) * o.reveal, size * 1.8); ctx.clip();
  }
  ctx.fillText(s, x, y);
  ctx.restore();
  return w;
}

// ---- rubber stamps ------------------------------------------------------------------------

const stampCache = new Map();
function stampImage(label, size, color) {
  const key = `${label}|${size}|${color}`;
  let c = stampCache.get(key);
  if (c) return c;
  const sc = 2;   // supersampled so it stays crisp when rotated
  const m = document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d');
  m.font = font(700, size * sc, MONO);
  const tw = m.measureText(label).width;
  const pad = size * 0.55 * sc, w = Math.ceil(tw + pad * 2), h = Math.ceil(size * 1.75 * sc);
  c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = w + 8; c.height = h + 8;
  const g = c.getContext('2d');
  g.translate(4, 4);
  g.strokeStyle = color; g.fillStyle = color;
  g.lineWidth = size * 0.11 * sc;
  g.strokeRect(g.lineWidth / 2, g.lineWidth / 2, w - g.lineWidth, h - g.lineWidth);
  g.lineWidth = size * 0.045 * sc;
  const ins = size * 0.2 * sc;
  g.strokeRect(ins, ins, w - ins * 2, h - ins * 2);
  g.font = font(700, size * sc, MONO);
  g.textBaseline = 'middle'; g.textAlign = 'center';
  g.fillText(label, w / 2, h / 2 + size * 0.06 * sc);
  // worn rubber: knock out specks and a few scuffs
  g.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
  const n = Math.round((w * h) / (90 * sc * sc));
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const x = hash(i, 51) * w, y = hash(i, 52) * h, r = (0.5 + 1.6 * hash(i, 53) ** 3) * sc;
    g.globalAlpha = 0.5 + 0.5 * hash(i, 54);
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y, r, 0, TAU); g.fill();
  }
  g.globalAlpha = 0.35;
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    g.lineWidth = (1 + 2 * hash(i, 61)) * sc;
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(hash(i, 62) * w, hash(i, 63) * h); g.lineTo(hash(i, 64) * w, hash(i, 65) * h); g.stroke();
  }
  c.dims = { w: (w + 8) / sc, h: (h + 8) / sc };
  stampCache.set(key, c);
  return c;
}

/**
 * A rubber stamp: invisible before t0 - 0.1, falls onto the page and hits at t0 with a small
 * squash, then stays. Ink, never colour. o: t (now), t0 (the hit; put it on a beat with
 * C.onBeat), size (40), rot (radians, default a seeded small angle), alpha, color.
 */
export function stamp(ctx, label, x, y, o = {}) {
  const t = o.t ?? Infinity, t0 = o.t0 ?? -Infinity;
  if (!(t >= t0 - 0.1)) return;
  const size = o.size ?? 40, color = o.color ?? P.ink;
  const img = stampImage(label, size, color);
  const k = clamp((t - (t0 - 0.1)) / 0.1);          // falling
  const after = t - t0;
  const fall = 1 + 0.35 * (1 - ease.inQuad(k));
  const squash = after >= 0 ? 1 - 0.1 * Math.exp(-after / 0.05) * Math.cos(after * 40) : 1;
  const rot = o.rot ?? (hash(label.length * 31 + label.charCodeAt(0), 7) - 0.5) * 0.24;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= (o.alpha ?? 0.88) * (after >= 0 ? 1 : 0.35 + 0.65 * k);
  ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.rotate(rot);
  ctx.scale(fall * (2 - squash), fall * squash);
  ctx.drawImage(img, -img.dims.w / 2, -img.dims.h / 2, img.dims.w, img.dims.h);
  ctx.restore();
}

// ---- figure furniture ---------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Plain axes: origin (x0, y0), lengths w (right) and h (up), ticks, labels in the serif. */
export function axes(pen, ctx, x0, y0, w, h, o = {}) {
  const p = o.p ?? 1;
  pen.arrow(x0, y0, x0 + w, y0, { p, head: 12, k: 'ax-x' });
  pen.arrow(x0, y0, x0, y0 - h, { p, head: 12, k: 'ax-y' });
  if (o.xLabel) text(ctx, o.xLabel, x0 + w, y0 + 40, { size: 26, style: 'italic', align: 'right', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(p, 0.6, 1) });
  if (o.yLabel) text(ctx, o.yLabel, x0 - 14, y0 - h + 6, { size: 26, style: 'italic', align: 'right', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(p, 0.6, 1) });
}

/** Panel letter (a, b, c ...) in bold italic serif, as in a multi-panel figure. */
export function panelLetter(ctx, letter, x, y, o = {}) {
  text(ctx, letter, x, y, { size: o.size ?? 38, weight: 700, style: 'italic', alpha: o.alpha ?? 1 });
}

/**
 * The placeholder figure: the framed figure area with the baby in the cap standing in it,
 * curious; the panel letter of the current line, and the lab's file name in light mono.
 * Lab modules call it (C.placeholder()) until they draw their real figure.
 */
export function placeholder(ctx, C, o = {}) {
  const A = C.area;
  const pen = C.pen('placeholder-frame', { width: 2.4 });
  const inP = C.inP;
  pen.rect(A.x, A.y, A.w, A.h, { p: inP, k: 'frame' });
  // corner ticks inside the frame (a plotting area)
  const tk = 18;
  for (const [cx, cy, sx, sy] of [[A.x + 30, A.y + 30, 1, 1], [A.x + A.w - 30, A.y + 30, -1, 1], [A.x + 30, A.y + A.h - 30, 1, -1], [A.x + A.w - 30, A.y + A.h - 30, -1, -1]]) {
    pen.with({ width: 1.4, color: P.ink2 }).poly([[cx + sx * tk, cy], [cx, cy], [cx, cy + sy * tk]], { p: inP, k: `tick${cx},${cy}` });
  }
  const letter = 'abcdefgh'[C.lineIndex] || '';
  if (C.lines.length > 1) panelLetter(ctx, letter, A.x + 48, A.y + 76, { alpha: inP });
  text(ctx, `placeholder · scenes/labs/${C.plate}.js${C.lines.length > 1 ? ` · line ${C.lineIndex + 1} of ${C.lines.length}` : ''}`, A.x + A.w - 44, A.y + 64,
    { size: 22, mono: true, align: 'right', color: P.ink2, alpha: inP });
  // the baby, standing on the frame's floor, curious: looking about, bobbing on each word
  const r = o.r ?? 112;
  const ground = A.y + A.h - 46;
  pen.with({ width: 1.6, color: P.ink2 }).line(A.x + A.w * 0.32, ground + 2, A.x + A.w * 0.68, ground + 2, { p: inP, k: 'floor' });
  const lastWord = C.words.filter((w) => w.start <= C.t).at(-1);
  const bob = lastWord ? Math.exp(-Math.max(0, C.t - lastWord.start) / 0.16) * Math.min(1, (C.t - lastWord.start) / 0.03 + 0.2) : 0;
  const lookX = Math.sin(C.t * 0.9 + C.seed * 0.01) * 0.6, lookY = -0.3 + 0.25 * Math.sin(C.t * 0.53 + 1.3);
  const x = A.x + A.w / 2, y = babyStandY(ground, r) - 7 * bob;
  drawPaperBaby(ctx, {
    x, y, r, tilt: 0.05 * Math.sin(C.t * 0.7 + C.seed * 0.02),
    eyes: 'open', open: blink(C.t, C.seed), look: [lookX, lookY], mouth: 'o', mouthOpen: 0.22 + 0.25 * bob,
    free: { mode: 'up', angle: C.t * 1.3 }, other: { mode: 'down' },
    cap: o.cap, ...o.baby,
  });
}
