V=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video; cat > $V/plate.js <<'EOF'
// The plate context C: everything a plate's draw(ctx, C) may read. Built fresh for every
// frame from (song time, window); nothing in it carries state between frames. The lookups
// never throw: a word or line the take does not have comes back null (word, lineFor), false
// (has), a fallback (wordTime: Infinity, i.e. "never"), or 0 (wseg).
//
//   time      t (song time, loop-mapped), fps, start, end, dur, lt (t - start), p (lt / dur),
//             inP (0..1 draw-in over the first 0.6 s), erasing (true while this plate is the
//             previous one being erased; t is then held at its end)
//   window    win, plate (= lab key for labs), params, lab (the labs.js entry), seed
//   figures   fig (the figure the caption shows now, or null), figs (all), num ({ key: n }),
//             figNum(key) -> number | null
//   lines     lines (this window's lines in the take), line (the current one: the last that
//             has started, else the first), lineIndex, lineP (0..1 through it), words (its words)
//   lookups   has(text), lineFor(text), word(text, { line, nth, backing }), wordTime(text, o),
//             wordEnd(text, o), since(text, o), wseg(text, dur, o), seg(a, b), eseg(a, b, fn)
//             text matches normalized words ("thirty six" matches "thirty-six"); o.line is an
//             index into lines or a text match; o.fallback replaces "absent"
//   beat      B (beat grid), beat (B.beatPos(t)), onBeat(time, mode = 'beat') -> snapped time
//   drawing   area (the figure area), page, ruleY, P (palette), SERIF, MONO,
//             pen(label, opts) a wobble pen seeded by plate + label, text(s, x, y, o),
//             stamp(label, x, y, o) (t defaults to C.t), baby(o), inkBaby(o), adult(o),
//             axes(pen, x0, y0, w, h, o), placeholder(o)
//   engine    env, L (lyrics), story

import { clamp, seg, eseg, ease, strSeed } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { norm } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/lyrics.js';
import { makePen } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/ink.js';
import { LAB } from './labs.js';
import { LEGEND_LEAD } from './edit.js';
import { P, SERIF, MONO, PAGE, AREA, RULE_Y, text, stamp, axes, placeholder, drawPaperBaby, drawInkAdult } from './kit.js';

const matches = (w, m) => { const nw = norm(w.w); return nw === m || nw.includes(m); };

export function makePlateContext(ctx, env, t, w, { erasing = false } = {}) {
  const st = env.story, B = env.B, L = env.L;
  const lines = w.lines || [];
  let li = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) if (lines[i].start - LEGEND_LEAD <= t) li = i;
  const line = lines[li] || null;
  const seed = strSeed(w.plate + ':' + (w.params.section || w.params.lab || ''));
  const pool = (o) => (o.line == null ? lines : typeof o.line === 'number' ? [lines[o.line]].filter(Boolean)
    : lines.filter((l) => l.n.includes(norm(o.line))));
  const C = {
    t, fps: env.fps, start: w.start, end: w.end, dur: w.dur, lt: t - w.start, p: clamp((t - w.start) / w.dur),
    inP: ease.outCubic(seg(t, w.start, w.start + 0.6)), erasing,
    win: w, plate: w.plate, params: w.params, lab: LAB[w.plate] || null, seed,
    fig: st.figureAt(t), figs: st.figs, num: st.num, figNum: (k) => st.num[k] ?? null,
    lines, line, lineIndex: li, lineP: line ? seg(t, line.start, line.end) : 0, words: line ? line.words : [],
    has: (m) => lines.some((l) => l.n.includes(norm(m))),
    lineFor: (m) => lines.find((l) => l.n.includes(norm(m))) || null,
    word(m, o = {}) {
      const mm = norm(m);
      let n = o.nth ?? 0;
      for (const l of pool(o)) for (const wd of l.words) {
        if (o.backing != null && !!wd.backing !== !!o.backing) continue;
        if (matches(wd, mm) && n-- === 0) return wd;
      }
      // a multi-word match ("thirty six" is one word, "one year" two): first word of the run
      const parts = mm.split(' ');
      if (parts.length > 1) for (const l of pool(o)) for (let k = 0; k + parts.length <= l.words.length; k++) {
        if (parts.every((p, j) => norm(l.words[k + j].w) === p)) return l.words[k];
      }
      return null;
    },
    wordTime(m, o = {}) { const wd = C.word(m, o); return wd ? wd.start : (o.fallback ?? Infinity); },
    wordEnd(m, o = {}) { const wd = C.word(m, o); return wd ? wd.end : (o.fallback ?? Infinity); },
    since(m, o = {}) { return t - C.wordTime(m, o); },
    wseg(m, dur = 0.5, o = {}) { const t0 = C.wordTime(m, o); return isFinite(t0) ? clamp((t - t0) / dur) : 0; },
    seg: (a, b) => seg(t, a, b), eseg: (a, b, fn) => eseg(t, a, b, fn),
    B, beat: B.beatPos(t), onBeat: (time, mode = 'beat') => (isFinite(time) ? B.snap(time, mode) : time),
    area: AREA, page: PAGE, ruleY: RULE_Y, P, SERIF, MONO,
    pen: (label = 'main', opts = {}) => makePen(ctx, `${w.plate}/${label}`, { color: P.ink, ...opts }),
    text: (s, x, y, o) => text(ctx, s, x, y, o),
    stamp: (label, x, y, o = {}) => stamp(ctx, label, x, y, { t, ...o }),
    baby: (o) => drawPaperBaby(ctx, o),
    inkBaby: (o) => drawPaperBaby(ctx, { ...o, ink: o.ink ?? 1 }),
    adult: (o) => drawInkAdult(ctx, { pen: makePen(ctx, `${w.plate}/adult${o.k ?? ''}`, { color: P.ink }), ...o }),
    axes: (pen, x0, y0, w0, h0, o) => axes(pen, ctx, x0, y0, w0, h0, o),
    placeholder: (o) => placeholder(ctx, C, o),
    env, L, story: st,
  };
  return C;
}

V=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video; cat > $V/scenes/title.js <<'EOF'
// The title page (intro, and frame-exactly the loop tail): paper and the HUD first, then the
// paper title "Replicate (n = 1)" written in, the author line segment by segment as the
// narrator names them, a heartbeat trace drawing itself along the bottom on the beat, and on
// "And me" the baby in the cap pokes in from the bottom-right corner and waves one nub.
// The outro (scenes/outro.js) draws the same title block and continues the trace.

import { clamp, seg, ease, lerp } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { P, text, drawPaperBaby, babyStandY, blink, RULE_Y } from '../kit.js';

export const TITLE = { y: 356, size: 116, authorY: 452, authorSize: 42, ruleTop: 214, ruleBot: 500, x0: 470, x1: 1450 };
export const TRACE = { x0: 140, x1: 1780, y: 712 };
const AUTHOR = [
  { text: 'Fifteen babies', word: 'fifteen' },
  { text: 'thirty-six world-renowned scientists', word: 'thirty six' },
  { text: 'one year', word: 'one' },
];

/** The title block. reveal(i) -> 0..1 for 0 = rules, 1 = title, 2.. = author segments. */
export function drawTitleBlock(ctx, C, reveal, alpha = 1) {
  if (alpha <= 0) return;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  const pen = C.pen('title-rules', { width: 1.8, color: P.ink2, wobble: 1.4 });
  pen.line(TITLE.x0, TITLE.ruleTop, TITLE.x1, TITLE.ruleTop, { p: reveal(0), k: 'top' });
  pen.line(TITLE.x0 + 160, TITLE.ruleBot, TITLE.x1 - 160, TITLE.ruleBot, { p: reveal(0), k: 'bot' });
  text(ctx, 'METHODS · A STUDY OF ONE', 960, TITLE.ruleTop - 22, { size: 20, mono: true, align: 'center', color: P.ink2, track: 0.32, reveal: reveal(0) });
  text(ctx, 'Replicate (n = 1)', 960, TITLE.y, { size: TITLE.size, align: 'center', reveal: reveal(1) });
  // the author line: three segments, centred as a whole, each written in on its word
  ctx.font = `italic 400 ${TITLE.authorSize}px ${C.SERIF}`;
  const sep = '  ·  ';
  const ws = AUTHOR.map((a) => ctx.measureText(a.text).width), sw = ctx.measureText(sep).width;
  let x = 960 - (ws.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) + sw * (AUTHOR.length - 1)) / 2;
  AUTHOR.forEach((a, i) => {
    const r = reveal(2 + i);
    if (i > 0) { text(ctx, sep, x, TITLE.authorY, { size: TITLE.authorSize, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, alpha: clamp(r * 3) }); x += sw; }
    text(ctx, a.text, x, TITLE.authorY, { size: TITLE.authorSize, style: 'italic', color: P.ink, reveal: r });
    x += ws[i];
  });
  ctx.restore();
}

/** The full trace polyline (fixed for the whole song: heart time 0 .. hSpan). */
let traceCache = null;
function tracePoints(heart) {
  if (traceCache && traceCache.heart === heart) return traceCache.pts;
  const { x0, x1, y } = TRACE;
  const X = (h) => x0 + ((x1 - x0) * h) / heart.hSpan;
  const pts = [[x0, y]];
  // ECG complex in px around the beat's x: P wave, Q, R spike, S, T wave
  const shape = [[-15, 0], [-12, -6], [-9, -8], [-6, 0], [-3.5, 0], [-2.2, 6], [0, -64], [2.4, 18], [4.6, 0], [10, 0], [14, -11], [18, -13], [22, 0]];
  for (const hb of heart.beats) {
    const bx = X(hb);
    if (bx - 16 < pts.at(-1)[0]) continue;
    for (const [dx, dy] of shape) pts.push([bx + dx, y + dy]);
  }
  pts.push([x1, y]);
  traceCache = { heart, pts };
  return pts;
}

/** The trace up to heart time h (an exact prefix of the whole trace), with the pen tip. */
export function drawTrace(ctx, C, h, alpha = 1) {
  if (h == null || alpha <= 0) return;
  const heart = C.story.heart;
  const { x0, x1 } = TRACE;
  const xNow = x0 + ((x1 - x0) * clamp(h / heart.hSpan)) ;
  const all = tracePoints(heart);
  const pts = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < all.length; i++) {
    if (all[i][0] <= xNow) { pts.push(all[i]); continue; }
    const a = all[i - 1], b = all[i];
    const f = (xNow - a[0]) / Math.max(1e-9, b[0] - a[0]);
    pts.push([xNow, a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * f]);
    break;
  }
  if (pts.length < 2) return;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  const pen = C.pen('heart', { width: 2.3, wobble: 1.1, wave: 90, jitter: 0 });
  pen.stroke(pts, { k: 'trace' });
  // the pen tip, while it is writing
  if (xNow < x1 - 1) {
    const tip = pts.at(-1);
    ctx.fillStyle = P.ink;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(tip[0], tip[1], 4.2, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const st = C.story;
    const t = C.t;
    const open = st.introOpen;
    const l0 = C.lines[0] || null;
    const wt = (m, fb) => { const w = l0 ? C.word(m, { line: 0 }) : null; return w ? w.start : fb; };
    const tAuthor = AUTHOR.map((a, i) => Math.max(open + 0.4, wt(a.word, open + 0.8 + i * 1.2)));
    const reveal = (i) => (i === 0 ? ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, open, open + 1.1))
      : i === 1 ? ease.outCubic(seg(t, open + 0.05, open + 0.95))
        : ease.outCubic(seg(t, tAuthor[i - 2] - 0.05, tAuthor[i - 2] + 0.5)));
    drawTitleBlock(ctx, C, reveal);
    drawTrace(ctx, C, st.heart.time(t));
    // "And me": the baby pokes in from the bottom-right corner, behind the caption rule
    const andMe = C.word('and', { line: 1 });
    if (andMe && t >= andMe.start - 0.15) {
      const r = 92;
      const k = ease.outBack(seg(t, andMe.start - 0.15, andMe.start + 0.45), 1.3);
      const yUp = babyStandY(RULE_Y + 1.25 * r, r);
      const y = lerp(RULE_Y + 1.6 * r, yUp, k);
      const waving = t < andMe.start + 2.6;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(C.area.x, 0, 1920, RULE_Y - 3); ctx.clip();
      drawPaperBaby(ctx, {
        x: 1660, y, r, tilt: -0.08,
        eyes: 'open', open: blink(t, 11), look: [-0.7, -0.45], mouth: 'smile',
        free: waving ? { mode: 'wave', angle: (t - andMe.start) * 7 } : { mode: 'up', angle: 0.4 }, other: { mode: 'down' },
        cap: { to: [3.4, -7.5], sway: t * 0.8 },
      });
      ctx.restore();
    }
  },
};

// The outro: the title page again. The heartbeat trace continues from where the intro left
// it; "Hardest problem in the universe. We should let them sleep." is set as a pull quote,
// word by word as it is spoken, attributed in the lighter ink; the baby is asleep in the
// corner in the cap. On "n equals one" everything but the HUD erases (story.outroAlpha; the
// compositor erases the caption rule with it) and the page is the intro's first frame.

import { clamp, seg, ease } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { P, text, drawPaperBaby, babyStandY, RULE_Y } from '../kit.js';
import { drawTitleBlock, drawTrace, TITLE } from './title.js';
import { typo } from '../legend.js';

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const st = C.story;
    const t = C.t;
    const a = st.outroAlpha(t);
    if (a <= 0) return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
    drawTitleBlock(ctx, C, () => 1);
    drawTrace(ctx, C, st.heart.time(t));
    // the pull quote
    const q = C.lines.find((l) => /hardest problem/.test(l.n)) || null;
    if (q) {
      const words = q.words.map((w) => typo(w.w).replace(/^“|”$/g, ''));
      const size = 50, y0 = 584;
      ctx.font = `italic 400 ${size}px ${C.SERIF}`;
      const sp = ctx.measureText(' ').width;
      const ws = words.map((s) => ctx.measureText(s).width);
      // two rows, broken after the sentence
      const br = Math.max(1, q.words.findIndex((w) => /\.["”]?$/.test(w.w)) + 1);
      const rows = [[0, br], [br, words.length]];
      rows.forEach(([i, j], r) => {
        const rw = ws.slice(i, j).reduce((s, x) => s + x, 0) + sp * (j - i - 1);
        let x = 960 - rw / 2;
        const y = y0 + r * size * 1.22;
        if (r === 0) text(ctx, '“', x - 30, y, { size, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, alpha: seg(t, q.start - 0.3, q.start) });
        for (let k = i; k < j; k++) {
          const w = q.words[k];
          text(ctx, words[k], x, y, { size, style: 'italic', color: P.ink, alpha: ease.outCubic(seg(t, w.start - 0.05, w.start + 0.25)) });
          x += ws[k] + sp;
        }
        if (r === rows.length - 1) text(ctx, '”', x - sp + 4, y, { size, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, alpha: seg(t, q.end - 0.2, q.end + 0.1) });
      });
      text(ctx, '— Andre Fenton, Babies, Ep. 5', 960, y0 + 2 * size * 1.22 + 6, { size: 28, align: 'center', color: P.ink2, alpha: seg(t, q.end, q.end + 0.6) });
    }
    // the baby, asleep in the corner, in the cap
    const r = 70;
    const breath = Math.sin(t * 2.1) * 0.5 + 0.5;
    drawPaperBaby(ctx, {
      x: 1660, y: babyStandY(RULE_Y - 26, r) + 2 * breath, r, tilt: -0.32,
      eyes: 'closed', mouth: 'o', mouthOpen: 0.15 + 0.1 * breath,
      free: { mode: 'down' }, other: { mode: 'down' }, cap: { to: [4.5, -3.2], sway: 0 },
    });
    // z's
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const ph = ((t * 0.45 + i / 3) % 1);
      text(ctx, 'z', 1700 + 24 * i + 18 * ph, 640 - 70 * ph - 26 * i, { size: 26 + 8 * i, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, alpha: Math.sin(Math.PI * ph) });
    }
    ctx.restore();
  },
};

V=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video; cat > $V/scenes/chorus.js <<'EOF'
// The chorus (and the final chorus): the apparatus bench, then the replication.
//
// Bench: a long ink bench with the stations named in the lyric, left to right: scanner ring,
// slope, EEG cap on a stand, swab, a ruler marked 7 and 10 (months). The baby rides a cart
// and reaches each station on its word ("scanner", "slope", "cap" (the cap drops on),
// "swab" (it taps the cheek), "seven, then at ten" (the ruler ticks)). A station whose word
// the take does not sing (chorus 2 sings only the first line) is reached at an interpolated
// time. On "subject", "sample" and "n of one" the baby gets labelled.
//
// Replication: on "Will it replicate?" the bench erases, the baby moves to the centre and
// divides like a cell on each "replicate" (one, two, four, eight, sixteen), each copy an ink
// outline, the original orange, into a 4x4 grid. Final chorus: all four divisions on the
// first "Will it replicate?" (half a beat apart), then a row turns orange on each "(I did.)",
// on "One year" and on "Ask me in a year": all sixteen orange, the page lighting up.

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease, TAU } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { mix } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';
import { P, text, drawPaperBaby, babyColors, blink } from '../kit.js';
import { isReplicateLine } from '../labs.js';

const BENCH = { top: 612, x0: 170, x1: 1750, legs: [262, 1658], floor: 806 };
const ST = { scanner: 470, slope: 790, cap: 1080, swab: 1350, ruler: 1600 };
const CART = { w: 196, h: 30, wheel: 17, start: 262 };
const BR = 58;                      // the baby on the cart
const GRID = { cx: 960, cy: 470, dx: 300, dy: 172, r: [92, 74, 60, 50, 43] };

// ---- timing ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Everything time-like for one chorus window, from its lines (pure; cheap enough per frame). */
function plan(C) {
  const lines = C.lines;
  const ri = lines.findIndex(isReplicateLine);
  const bench = ri < 0 ? lines : lines.slice(0, ri), repl = ri < 0 ? [] : lines.slice(ri);
  const benchStart = C.start, replStart = repl.length ? repl[0].start - 0.2 : Infinity;
  const benchEnd = Math.min(replStart, C.end);
  const inBench = (m) => { for (let k = 0; k < bench.length; k++) { const w = C.word(m, { line: lines.indexOf(bench[k]) }); if (w) return w.start; } return null; };
  // station arrival times: on the word, else interpolated
  const names = ['scanner', 'slope', 'cap', 'swab', 'seven', 'ten'];
  const tw = names.map((n) => inBench(n));
  const first = bench[0] ? bench[0].start : benchStart;
  const lo = Math.min(benchEnd - 0.5, (bench[0] ? first + Math.min(bench[0].end - first, 2.4) * 0.35 : benchStart + 0.6));
  const hi = benchEnd - 0.35;
  const known = tw.map((x, i) => (x != null ? [i, x] : null)).filter(Boolean);
  const times = tw.map((x, i) => {
    if (x != null) return x;
    const a = [...known].reverse().find(([j]) => j < i), b = known.find(([j]) => j > i);
    const ia = a ? a[0] : -1, ta = a ? a[1] : lo, ib = b ? b[0] : names.length, tb = b ? b[1] : hi;
    return lerp(ta, tb, (i - ia) / (ib - ia));
  });
  // labels on the first line
  const l0 = bench[0] ? lines.indexOf(bench[0]) : -1;
  const lab = (m) => (l0 >= 0 ? C.word(m, { line: l0 }) : null);
  const labels = [['subject', lab('subject')], ['sample', lab('sample')], ['n = 1', lab('one')]];
  // divisions
  const reps = [];
  for (const l of repl) for (const w of l.words) if (/^\(?replicate/i.test(w.w)) reps.push(w.start);
  let div;
  if (C.plate === 'final' || reps.length < 4) {
    const t1 = reps.length ? reps[0] : (isFinite(replStart) ? replStart + 0.6 : Infinity);
    div = [0, 1, 2, 3].map((k) => t1 + (k * C.B.beatDur) / 2);
  } else div = reps.slice(0, 4);
  // final: the rows turn orange
  let rows = null;
  if (C.plate === 'final') {
    const did = [];
    for (const l of repl) for (const w of l.words) if (w.backing && /did/i.test(w.w)) did.push(w.start);
    const yearL = repl.find((l) => /^fifteen babies/.test(l.n)), askL = repl.find((l) => /ask me in a year/.test(l.n));
    const pick = (l, m) => (l ? (l.words.find((w) => C.L.norm(w.w) === m)?.start ?? l.start) : null);
    const trig = [did[0] ?? null, did[1] ?? null, pick(yearL, 'year'), pick(askL, 'ask')];
    const a = div[3] + 0.8, b = C.end - 1.2;
    rows = trig.map((x, i) => (x != null ? x : lerp(a, b, i / 3)));
    for (let i = 1; i < 4; i++) rows[i] = Math.max(rows[i], rows[i - 1] + 0.2);
  }
  return { bench, repl, benchStart, benchEnd, replStart, times, labels, div, rows };
}

// cart x at t: arrives at each station on its time, dwells, moves on
function cartX(t, T) {
  const xs = [ST.scanner, ST.slope, ST.cap, ST.swab, ST.ruler, ST.ruler];
  let x = CART.start, prevT = T.times[0] - Math.min(0.9, Math.max(0.3, T.times[0] - T.benchStart - 0.2));
  let prevX = CART.start;
  for (let i = 0; i < xs.length; i++) {
    const ti = T.times[i];
    if (t >= ti) { x = xs[i]; prevX = xs[i]; prevT = ti; continue; }
    const leave = i === 0 ? prevT : Math.min(prevT + 0.22, ti - 0.12);
    return lerp(prevX, xs[i], ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, leave, ti)));
  }
  return x;
}

const rampY = (x) => { const d = Math.abs(x - ST.slope); return d < 92 ? -70 * (1 - d / 92) : 0; };

// ---- the stations ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

function drawBench(ctx, C, p, alpha) {
  if (alpha <= 0) return;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  const pen = C.pen('bench', { width: 2.6 });
  const thin = pen.with({ width: 1.5, color: P.ink2 });
  const b = BENCH;
  pen.line(b.x0, b.top, b.x1, b.top, { p: seg(p, 0, 0.5), k: 'top' });
  pen.line(b.x0 + 6, b.top + 20, b.x1 - 6, b.top + 20, { p: seg(p, 0.05, 0.55), k: 'top2' });
  pen.line(b.x0, b.top, b.x0 + 6, b.top + 20, { p: seg(p, 0.05, 0.2), k: 'endL' });
  pen.line(b.x1, b.top, b.x1 - 6, b.top + 20, { p: seg(p, 0.4, 0.55), k: 'endR' });
  for (const lx of b.legs) {
    pen.line(lx - 40, b.top + 20, lx - 58, b.floor, { p: seg(p, 0.2, 0.6), k: `legA${lx}` });
    pen.line(lx + 40, b.top + 20, lx + 58, b.floor, { p: seg(p, 0.25, 0.65), k: `legB${lx}` });
    thin.line(lx - 50, b.top + 110, lx + 50, b.top + 110, { p: seg(p, 0.4, 0.7), k: `brace${lx}` });
  }
  thin.line(b.x0 - 40, b.floor, b.x1 + 40, b.floor, { p: seg(p, 0.3, 0.9), k: 'floor' });
  // station labels, under the bench top
  const L = [['scanner', ST.scanner], ['slope', ST.slope], ['EEG cap', ST.cap], ['swab', ST.swab], ['ruler (mo)', ST.ruler]];
  L.forEach(([s, x], i) => text(ctx, s, x, b.top + 58, { size: 27, style: 'italic', align: 'center', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(p, 0.45 + i * 0.08, 0.7 + i * 0.08) }));
  ctx.restore();
}

function ringPts(cx, cy, rx, ry, a0, a1) { const out = []; for (let i = 0; i <= 40; i++) { const a = lerp(a0, a1, i / 40); out.push([cx + Math.cos(a) * rx, cy + Math.sin(a) * ry]); } return out; }

/** The scanner ring: the back half (behind the cart) or the front half. */
function drawScanner(ctx, C, p, half) {
  const cx = ST.scanner, cy = BENCH.top - 168;
  const pen = C.pen('scanner', { width: 2.6 });
  const [a0, a1] = half === 'back' ? [Math.PI / 2, (3 * Math.PI) / 2] : [-Math.PI / 2, Math.PI / 2];
  const outer = ringPts(cx, cy, 64, 168, a0, a1), inner = ringPts(cx, cy, 40, 140, a0, a1);
  if (half === 'front') pen.hatch([...outer, ...inner.slice().reverse()], { gap: 8, p, angle: -1.1, k: 'hatch' });
  pen.stroke(outer, { p, k: `o-${half}` });
  pen.stroke(inner, { p, k: `i-${half}` });
  if (half === 'back') {
    // the pedestal
    pen.poly([[cx - 52, BENCH.top], [cx - 30, cy + 150], [cx + 30, cy + 150], [cx + 52, BENCH.top]], { p, k: 'ped' });
  }
}

function drawSlope(ctx, C, p) {
  const pen = C.pen('slope', { width: 2.6 });
  const x = ST.slope, y = BENCH.top;
  const tri = [[x - 92, y], [x, y - 70], [x + 92, y]];
  pen.hatch(tri, { gap: 9, p, k: 'hatch' });
  pen.poly(tri, { p, k: 'ramp' });
}

/** The cap stand; the cap hangs from it until it drops (drop = 0..1). */
function drawCapStand(ctx, C, p, drop, headY) {
  const pen = C.pen('capstand', { width: 2.4 });
  const px = ST.cap + 110, topY = 232;
  pen.line(px, BENCH.top, px, topY, { p, k: 'post' });
  pen.line(px - 30, BENCH.top, px + 30, BENCH.top, { p, k: 'foot' });
  pen.line(px, topY, ST.cap, topY, { p, k: 'arm' });
  if (drop >= 1) { pen.with({ width: 1.3 }).line(ST.cap, topY, ST.cap, topY + 30, { p, k: 'hook' }); return; }
  const hang = topY + 92;
  const capY = lerp(hang, headY, ease.inQuad(drop));
  pen.with({ width: 1.3 }).line(ST.cap, topY, ST.cap, capY - 1.15 * BR, { p, k: 'string' });
  drawLooseCap(ctx, C, ST.cap, capY, BR, p);
}

/** An empty EEG cap in ink (paper inside, electrodes), shaped like the baby's (BR units). */
function drawLooseCap(ctx, C, x, y, r, p) {
  const pen = C.pen('loosecap', { width: 2.2 });
  const pts = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= 24; i++) {
    const a = Math.PI + (i / 24) * Math.PI;
    pts.push([x + Math.cos(a) * 1.3 * r, y - 0.42 * r + Math.sin(a) * 0.75 * r]);
  }
  ctx.save();
  ctx.beginPath(); pts.forEach((q, i) => (i ? ctx.lineTo(q[0], q[1]) : ctx.moveTo(q[0], q[1]))); ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle = P.cap; ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
  pen.stroke([...pts, pts[0]], { p, k: 'cap' });
  ctx.fillStyle = P.ink;
  for (const [ex, ey] of [[-0.85, -0.75], [-0.4, -1.0], [0, -1.08], [0.4, -1.0], [0.85, -0.75]]) {
    ctx.globalAlpha = p;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x + ex * r, y + ey * r, 0.075 * r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}

/** The swab on its arm; tap = 0..1..0 moves the bud to the cheek at (cx, cy). */
function drawSwab(ctx, C, p, tap, cheek) {
  const pen = C.pen('swab', { width: 2.4 });
  const px = ST.swab + 128, topY = 300;
  pen.line(px, BENCH.top, px, topY, { p, k: 'post' });
  pen.line(px - 28, BENCH.top, px + 28, BENCH.top, { p, k: 'foot' });
  const rest = [ST.swab + 92, 420];
  const bud = [lerp(rest[0], cheek[0] + 14, tap), lerp(rest[1], cheek[1], tap)];
  const handle = [bud[0] + 70, bud[1] - 64];
  pen.line(px, topY + 6, handle[0], handle[1], { p, k: 'arm' });
  pen.with({ width: 3.4 }).line(handle[0], handle[1], bud[0], bud[1], { p, k: 'stick' });
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(bud[0], bud[1]);
  ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(handle[1] - bud[1], handle[0] - bud[0]));
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-4, 0, 15, 10, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
  pen.with({ width: 2 }).ellipse(bud[0] - 3, bud[1] + 2, 14, 10, { p, k: 'bud', rot: 0.7 });
}

/** The ruler: ticks, 7 and 10 marked; marks = [k7, k10] 0..1 as each is measured. */
function drawRuler(ctx, C, p, marks, heads) {
  const pen = C.pen('ruler', { width: 2.4 });
  const x = ST.ruler + 96, y0 = BENCH.top, y1 = 230;
  pen.rect(x - 18, y1, 36, y0 - y1, { p, k: 'body' });
  const thin = pen.with({ width: 1.3 });
  for (let i = 1, y = y0 - 18; y > y1 + 6; i++, y -= 18) thin.line(x - 18, y, x - 18 + (i % 5 === 0 ? 20 : 10), y, { p, k: `t${i}` });
  [[0, '7'], [1, '10']].forEach(([i, s]) => {
    const k = marks[i];
    const y = heads[i];
    text(ctx, s, x + 30, y + 10, { size: 30, weight: k > 0 ? 700 : 400, color: k > 0 ? P.ink : P.ink2, alpha: p });
    if (k > 0) pen.with({ width: 1.6 }).dashed([[x - 22, y], [lerp(x - 22, ST.ruler + 4, k), y]], { dash: 9, gap: 6, k: `m${i}` });
  });
}

function drawCart(ctx, C, x, y, tilt, p) {
  const pen = C.pen('cart', { width: 2.6 });
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.rotate(tilt);
  const w = CART.w, h = CART.h;
  ctx.fillStyle = P.paper;
  ctx.fillRect(-w / 2, -h - CART.wheel * 2 + 4, w, h);
  pen.rect(-w / 2, -h - CART.wheel * 2 + 4, w, h, { p, k: 'body' });
  pen.with({ width: 1.2 }).hatch([-w / 2, -h - CART.wheel * 2 + 4, w, h], { gap: 10, p, angle: -0.8, k: 'hatch' });
  for (const wx of [-w / 2 + 34, w / 2 - 34]) {
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(wx, -CART.wheel, CART.wheel, 0, TAU); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fill();
    pen.circle(wx, -CART.wheel, CART.wheel, { p, k: `w${wx}` });
    const a = x / CART.wheel;
    pen.with({ width: 1.4 }).line(wx + Math.cos(a) * CART.wheel * 0.8, -CART.wheel + Math.sin(a) * CART.wheel * 0.8, wx - Math.cos(a) * CART.wheel * 0.8, -CART.wheel - Math.sin(a) * CART.wheel * 0.8, { p, k: `s${wx}` });
  }
  ctx.restore();
  return y - h - CART.wheel * 2 + 4;   // the deck
}

// ---- the replication ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Node tree for the 4x4 grid: level L node i covers cols [c0, c1) x rows [r0, r1). Child 0 is the half nearer the centre. */
const TREE = (() => {
  const levels = [[{ c0: 0, c1: 4, r0: 0, r1: 4 }]];
  for (let L = 1; L <= 4; L++) {
    const out = [];
    for (const n of levels[L - 1]) {
      const byCol = L % 2 === 1;
      const [a0, a1] = byCol ? [n.c0, n.c1] : [n.r0, n.r1];
      const m = (a0 + a1) / 2;
      let lo = byCol ? { ...n, c1: m } : { ...n, r1: m }, hi = byCol ? { ...n, c0: m } : { ...n, r0: m };
      const dist = (q) => Math.abs((byCol ? (q.c0 + q.c1) / 2 : (q.r0 + q.r1) / 2) - 2);
      if (dist(hi) < dist(lo) - 1e-9) [lo, hi] = [hi, lo];
      out.push(lo, hi);
    }
    levels.push(out);
  }
  return levels.map((ns) => ns.map((n) => ({ ...n, x: GRID.cx + (((n.c0 + n.c1) / 2) - 2) * GRID.dx, y: GRID.cy + (((n.r0 + n.r1) / 2) - 2) * GRID.dy })));
})();

/** Ink cell membranes for a level (or a dividing pair at progress q). */
function drawCells(ctx, C, L, q, alpha) {
  if (alpha <= 0) return;
  const pen = C.pen('cells', { width: 2.2 });
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  const R = (lv) => GRID.r[lv] * 1.55;
  const nodes = TREE[L];
  if (q == null) { nodes.forEach((n, i) => pen.circle(n.x, n.y + 0.2 * GRID.r[L], R(L), { k: `c${L}-${i}` })); ctx.restore(); return; }
  // dividing: the children of each parent, partway apart, outlined as one pinched membrane
  const parents = TREE[L - 1];
  parents.forEach((pn, pi) => {
    const a = nodes[2 * pi], b = nodes[2 * pi + 1];
    const r = lerp(R(L - 1), R(L), q) * (1 - 0.12 * Math.sin(Math.PI * q));
    const pa = [lerp(pn.x, a.x, q), lerp(pn.y, a.y, q) + 0.2 * GRID.r[L]], pb = [lerp(pn.x, b.x, q), lerp(pn.y, b.y, q) + 0.2 * GRID.r[L]];
    const d = Math.hypot(pb[0] - pa[0], pb[1] - pa[1]);
    const th = Math.atan2(pb[1] - pa[1], pb[0] - pa[0]);
    if (d >= 2 * r) { pen.circle(pa[0], pa[1], r, { k: `d${L}-${pi}a` }); pen.circle(pb[0], pb[1], r, { k: `d${L}-${pi}b` }); return; }
    const al = Math.acos(clamp(d / (2 * r), 0, 1)) + 0.1 * (1 - q);
    pen.arc(pa[0], pa[1], r, th + al, th + TAU - al, { k: `d${L}-${pi}a` });
    pen.arc(pb[0], pb[1], r, th + Math.PI + al, th + Math.PI + TAU - al, { k: `d${L}-${pi}b` });
  });
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawBabies(ctx, C, L, q, colorAt) {
  const nodes = TREE[L];
  const parents = q != null ? TREE[L - 1] : null;
  const bounce = 3 * C.B.beatEnv(C.t, 0.14);
  nodes.forEach((n, i) => {
    let x = n.x, y = n.y, r = GRID.r[L];
    if (q != null) { const pn = parents[i >> 1]; x = lerp(pn.x, n.x, q); y = lerp(pn.y, n.y, q); r = lerp(GRID.r[L - 1], GRID.r[L], q); }
    const orig = i === 0;
    const k = orig ? 1 : colorAt ? colorAt(n) : 0;
    const o = TREE[L][0];
    const lx = orig ? 0 : clamp((o.x - x) / 400, -1, 1), ly = orig ? 0.2 : clamp((o.y - y) / 300, -1, 1);
    drawPaperBaby(ctx, {
      x, y: y - bounce * (i % 2 ? 1 : 0.6), r, ink: 1 - k, C: babyColors(k),
      eyes: 'open', open: blink(C.t, 100 + i), look: [lx, ly], mouth: orig ? 'smile' : 'line',
      free: { mode: orig ? 'up' : 'down', angle: C.t * 2 }, other: { mode: 'down' },
      cap: { to: [lerp(-2.5, 2.5, (i * 7) % 5 / 4), -4], wires: L <= 2, color: mix(P.paper, P.cap, k), shade: k > 0 ? P.capShade : P.paper, electrode: P.ink },
    });
  });
}

// ---- the plate ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function draw(ctx, C) {
  const T = plan(C);
  const t = C.t;
  const benchP = ease.outCubic(seg(t, T.benchStart, T.benchStart + 1.3));
  const out = isFinite(T.replStart) ? ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, T.replStart, T.replStart + 0.55)) : 0;
  const benchA = 1 - out;
  // ---- bench phase
  const cx = cartX(t, T);
  const ry = rampY(cx), tilt = Math.abs(cx - ST.slope) < 92 ? -Math.sign(cx - ST.slope) * 0.55 * (1 - Math.abs(cx - ST.slope) / 92) : 0;
  const bob = 2.5 * C.B.beatEnv(t, 0.12);
  // the baby's growth on "ten" and the cap
  const grow = ease.outBack(seg(t, T.times[5], T.times[5] + 0.35));
  const r = BR * (1 + 0.1 * grow);
  const capDrop = seg(t, T.times[2] - 0.05, T.times[2] + 0.3);
  const capOn = capDrop >= 1;
  if (benchA > 0) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= benchA;
    if (out > 0) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(C.area.x - 40 + out * (C.area.w + 80), 0, 4000, 2000); ctx.clip(); }
    drawBench(ctx, C, benchP, 1);
    drawScanner(ctx, C, seg(benchP, 0.1, 0.6), 'back');
    drawSlope(ctx, C, seg(benchP, 0.2, 0.7));
    const deckY = BENCH.top + ry;
    // where the head is when the cart is under the cap
    const headAtCap = BENCH.top - CART.h - CART.wheel * 2 + 4 - 1.35 * BR - 0.05 * BR;
    drawCapStand(ctx, C, seg(benchP, 0.3, 0.8), capDrop, headAtCap);
    const tap = Math.max(0, Math.min(seg(t, T.times[3] - 0.18, T.times[3]), 1 - seg(t, T.times[3] + 0.18, T.times[3] + 0.45)));
    const heads = [BENCH.top - CART.h - CART.wheel * 2 + 4 - 2.55 * BR, BENCH.top - CART.h - CART.wheel * 2 + 4 - 2.55 * BR * 1.1];
    drawRuler(ctx, C, seg(benchP, 0.45, 0.95), [seg(t, T.times[4], T.times[4] + 0.3), seg(t, T.times[5], T.times[5] + 0.3)], heads);
    ctx.restore();
    // the cart and the baby (they fly on into the replication)
    if (out < 1) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha *= benchA;
      const deck = drawCart(ctx, C, cx, deckY - bob, tilt, benchP);
      ctx.restore();
      if (!isFinite(T.replStart) || t < T.replStart) {
        const fy = deck - 1.35 * r + 0.05 * BR * Math.sin(tilt);
        drawPaperBaby(ctx, {
          x: cx + Math.sin(tilt) * 30, y: fy, r, tilt: tilt * 0.8,
          eyes: 'open', open: blink(t, 7), look: [0.5, -0.2], mouth: tap > 0.5 ? 'o' : 'smile', mouthOpen: 0.4,
          free: { mode: 'up', angle: t * 3 }, other: { mode: 'down' },
          cap: capOn ? { to: [3, -6], sway: t } : null,
        });
        // the labels on "subject", "sample", "n of one"
        const fadeL = 1 - seg(t, T.times[0] - 0.5, T.times[0] - 0.1);
        if (fadeL > 0 && cx < ST.scanner - 60) {
          const pos = [[cx - 150, fy - 150, 'right'], [cx + 160, fy - 150, 'left'], [cx + 170, fy + 10, 'left']];
          T.labels.forEach(([s, w], i) => {
            if (!w || t < w.start) return;
            const a = fadeL * ease.outCubic(seg(t, w.start, w.start + 0.25));
            const [lx, ly, al] = pos[i];
            ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
            text(ctx, s, lx, ly, { size: 32, style: 'italic', align: al });
            const lp = C.pen('labels', { width: 1.6, color: P.ink2 });
            lp.line(lx + (al === 'right' ? 8 : -8), ly - 10, lerp(lx, cx, 0.55), lerp(ly - 10, fy - 20, 0.6), { p: seg(t, w.start, w.start + 0.3), k: `lead${i}` });
            ctx.restore();
          });
        }
      }
    }
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= benchA;
    if (out > 0) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(C.area.x - 40 + out * (C.area.w + 80), 0, 4000, 2000); ctx.clip(); }
    drawScanner(ctx, C, seg(benchP, 0.1, 0.6), 'front');
    drawSwab(ctx, C, seg(benchP, 0.35, 0.85), tap, [cx + 0.85 * r, BENCH.top - CART.h - CART.wheel * 2 + 4 - 1.35 * r + 0.36 * r]);
    ctx.restore();
  }
  if (!isFinite(T.replStart) || t < T.replStart) return;

  // ---- replication phase
  const fly = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, T.replStart, T.replStart + 0.55));
  let L = 0, q = null;
  for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
    const a = T.div[k] - 0.08, b = T.div[k] + 0.32;
    if (t >= b) L = k + 1;
    else if (t >= a) { L = k + 1; q = ease.outBack(seg(t, a, b), 1.1); break; }
  }
  const counter = ['1', '2', '4', '8', '16'];
  let s = '';
  for (let k = 0; k <= L; k++) s += (k ? ' → ' : 'n = ') + counter[k];
  text(ctx, s, C.area.x + C.area.w - 20, C.area.y + 46, { size: 26, mono: true, align: 'right', color: P.ink2, alpha: fly });
  if (L === 0) {
    // the baby flies from the cart to the centre and grows into the first cell
    const fx = lerp(cx, GRID.cx, fly), fy0 = BENCH.top - CART.h - CART.wheel * 2 + 4 - 1.35 * r;
    const fy = lerp(fy0, GRID.cy, fly), fr = lerp(r, GRID.r[0], fly);
    drawCells(ctx, C, 0, null, fly);
    drawPaperBaby(ctx, { x: fx, y: fy, r: fr, eyes: 'open', open: blink(t, 7), look: [0, 0.2], mouth: 'smile', free: { mode: 'up', angle: t * 3 }, other: { mode: 'down' }, cap: { to: [3, -6], sway: t } });
    return;
  }
  const cellA = 1 - 0.65 * seg(t, T.div[3] + 0.6, T.div[3] + 1.4);
  drawCells(ctx, C, L, q, cellA);
  const colorAt = T.rows ? (n) => {
    const row = Math.round((n.r0 + n.r1) / 2 - 0.5);
    const col = Math.round((n.c0 + n.c1) / 2 - 0.5);
    if (L < 4 || q != null) return 0;
    return ease.outCubic(seg(t, T.rows[row] + col * 0.07, T.rows[row] + col * 0.07 + 0.3));
  } : null;
  drawBabies(ctx, C, L, q, colorAt);
}

export default { draw };
export const finalScene = { draw };

V=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video; cat > $V/scenes/bridge.js <<'EOF'
// The bridge ("the part you skip"): the page dims (an ink wash, drawn by the compositor), a
// heading on "Now the part you skip", then one journal cover or product per myth, drawn as
// ink line art and stamped on the beat with its sample size (the HUD takes the n as each
// lands, story.js). The baby watches, unimpressed. On "So when you ask me... will it
// replicate?" everything fades to the lighter ink except the HUD, which shuffles the n's.
// Covers are drawn simply for now (the spine); MYTHS in labs.js holds the stamps.

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease, hash, TAU } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { P, text, drawPaperBaby, babyStandY, blink } from '../kit.js';
import { MYTH } from '../labs.js';

const CX = 880, CY = 470;

function cover(ctx, C, key, p, t, f) {
  const pen = C.pen(`cover-${key}`, { width: 2.6 });
  const thin = pen.with({ width: 1.5 });
  const w0 = C.word.bind(C);
  const lineIdx = C.lines.indexOf(f.lines[0]);
  const wt = (m) => { const w = w0(m, { line: lineIdx }); return w ? w.start : Infinity; };
  if (key === 'wonderweeks') {
    const w = 400, h = 500, x = CX - w / 2, y = CY - h / 2;
    ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
    pen.rect(x, y, w, h, { p, k: 'book' });
    thin.line(x + 22, y, x + 22, y + h, { p, k: 'spine' });
    text(ctx, 'THE WONDER', CX + 10, y + 70, { size: 40, align: 'center', track: 0.06, reveal: seg(p, 0.3, 0.8) });
    text(ctx, 'WEEKS', CX + 10, y + 116, { size: 40, align: 'center', track: 0.06, reveal: seg(p, 0.35, 0.85) });
    // a calendar, ten leaps circled
    const gx = x + 62, gy = y + 160, cw = 42, ch = 44;
    for (let r = 0; r < 7; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 7; c++) thin.rect(gx + c * cw, gy + r * ch, cw, ch, { p: seg(p, 0.3 + 0.01 * (r * 7 + c), 0.6 + 0.01 * (r * 7 + c)), over: 0, k: `g${r}${c}`, wobble: 0.6 });
    const leaps = [3, 9, 12, 19, 22, 28, 33, 37, 41, 46];
    const shown = Math.floor(10 * seg(t, wt('ten') - 0.1, wt('ten') + 1.2));
    leaps.slice(0, shown).forEach((d, i) => pen.with({ width: 2 }).ellipse(gx + (d % 7) * cw + cw / 2, gy + Math.floor(d / 7) * ch + ch / 2, 19, 17, { k: `leap${i}` }));
  } else if (key === 'mozart') {
    const s = 420, x = CX - s / 2, y = CY - s / 2;
    const slide = 120 * ease.outCubic(seg(t, f.start + 0.4, f.start + 1.2));
    ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(CX + slide, CY, 175, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    pen.circle(CX + slide, CY, 175, { p, k: 'disc' });
    thin.circle(CX + slide, CY, 60, { p, k: 'label' });
    thin.circle(CX + slide, CY, 16, { p, k: 'hole' });
    ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fillRect(x, y, s, s);
    pen.rect(x, y, s, s, { p, k: 'case' });
    thin.rect(x + 18, y + 18, s - 36, s - 36, { p, k: 'inner' });
    text(ctx, 'MOZART', CX, y + 150, { size: 54, align: 'center', track: 0.08, reveal: seg(p, 0.3, 0.8) });
    text(ctx, 'for babies', CX, y + 200, { size: 34, style: 'italic', align: 'center', reveal: seg(p, 0.4, 0.9) });
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) thin.spline([[x + 90 + i * 90, y + 300], [x + 110 + i * 90, y + 270], [x + 130 + i * 90, y + 300]], { p: seg(p, 0.5, 1), k: `note${i}` });
  } else if (key === 'einstein') {
    const w = 320, h = 450, x = CX - w / 2, y = CY - h / 2;
    ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
    pen.rrect(x, y, w, h, 18, { p, k: 'case' });
    thin.rect(x + 20, y + 20, w - 40, h - 40, { p, k: 'sleeve' });
    text(ctx, 'BABY', CX, y + 92, { size: 46, align: 'center', track: 0.1, reveal: seg(p, 0.3, 0.8) });
    text(ctx, 'EINSTEIN', CX, y + 142, { size: 46, align: 'center', track: 0.1, reveal: seg(p, 0.35, 0.85) });
    text(ctx, 'DVD', CX, y + h - 44, { size: 26, mono: true, align: 'center', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(p, 0.5, 1) });
    pen.circle(CX, CY + 40, 70, { p: seg(p, 0.4, 1), k: 'ball' });
    thin.arc(CX, CY + 40, 70, -0.6, 2.2, { p: seg(p, 0.6, 1), k: 'ballline' });
  } else if (key === 'imitation') {
    const w = 440, h = 380, x = CX - w / 2, y = CY - h / 2;
    ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
    pen.rect(x, y, w, h, { p, k: 'photo' });
    thin.rect(x + 20, y + 20, w - 40, h - 80, { p, k: 'print' });
    // an ink sketch: a newborn's face, tongue out
    pen.ellipse(CX, CY - 20, 110, 120, { p: seg(p, 0.2, 0.8), k: 'face' });
    pen.line(CX - 50, CY - 50, CX - 22, CY - 50, { p: seg(p, 0.5, 0.9), k: 'eyeL' });
    pen.line(CX + 22, CY - 50, CX + 50, CY - 50, { p: seg(p, 0.5, 0.9), k: 'eyeR' });
    pen.with({ width: 2.2 }).spline([[CX - 26, CY + 30], [CX, CY + 80], [CX + 26, CY + 30]], { p: seg(p, 0.6, 1), k: 'tongue' });
    text(ctx, '1977', x + w - 34, y + h - 22, { size: 26, mono: true, align: 'right', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(p, 0.6, 1) });
  } else if (key === 'helper') {
    const w = 400, h = 520, x = CX - w / 2, y = CY - h / 2;
    ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
    pen.rect(x, y, w, h, { p, k: 'journal' });
    text(ctx, 'Nature', CX, y + 100, { size: 82, weight: 700, style: 'italic', align: 'center', reveal: seg(p, 0.3, 0.8) });
    thin.line(x + 26, y + 128, x + w - 26, y + 128, { p, k: 'mast' });
    const many = seg(t, wt('manybabies'), wt('manybabies') + 1.6);
    if (many <= 0) {
      // the hill, a round puppet, a square, a triangle
      const hx = CX, hy = y + 400;
      pen.spline([[x + 40, hy], [hx - 40, hy - 150], [hx + 60, hy - 170], [x + w - 40, hy]], { p: seg(p, 0.4, 0.9), k: 'hill' });
      pen.circle(hx - 30, hy - 190, 20, { p: seg(p, 0.6, 1), k: 'round' });
      pen.rect(hx + 50, hy - 210, 32, 32, { p: seg(p, 0.6, 1), k: 'square' });
      pen.poly([[hx - 110, hy - 70], [hx - 88, hy - 110], [hx - 66, hy - 70]], { p: seg(p, 0.6, 1), close: true, k: 'tri' });
    } else {
      // thirty-seven tiny hills (ManyBabies 4, 37 labs)
      const n = Math.floor(37 * many);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const c = i % 7, r = Math.floor(i / 7);
        const sx = x + 48 + c * 47, sy = y + 190 + r * 60;
        thin.spline([[sx - 18, sy], [sx - 4, sy - 22], [sx + 8, sy - 24], [sx + 20, sy]], { k: `h${i}` });
      }
    }
    // the coin
    const tc = wt('coin');
    if (t >= tc - 0.1) {
      const k = t - tc;
      const fy = CY - 330 - 80 * Math.sin(Math.min(Math.PI, k * 3)), sq = Math.abs(Math.cos(k * 11));
      ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(CX + 320, fy + 200, 46, 46 * sq + 3, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      pen.ellipse(CX + 320, fy + 200, 46, 46 * sq + 3, { k: 'coin' });
    }
  } else if (key === 'thirtymillion') {
    const w = 640, h = 220, x = CX - w / 2, y = CY - h / 2;
    ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
    pen.rect(x, y, w, h, { p, k: 'counter' });
    const v = Math.round(30000000 * ease.outCubic(seg(t, f.start + 0.2, f.start + 1.6)));
    text(ctx, v.toLocaleString('en-US'), CX, CY + 30, { size: 88, mono: true, align: 'center', alpha: seg(p, 0.3, 0.8) });
    text(ctx, 'words', CX, y + h - 24, { size: 30, style: 'italic', align: 'center', color: P.ink2, alpha: seg(p, 0.5, 1) });
  }
}

function stamps(ctx, C, key, f, t) {
  const m = MYTH[key];
  const lineIdx = C.lines.indexOf(f.lines[0]);
  (m.stamps || []).forEach((s, i) => {
    let w = C.word(s.word, { line: lineIdx });
    if (s.line) { const l2 = C.word(s.line, { line: lineIdx }); if (l2) w = C.word(s.word, { line: lineIdx, nth: 0 }) && l2; }
    if (!w) return;
    const t0 = C.onBeat(w.start + 0.05);
    const pos = [[CX + 210, CY - 150], [CX - 150, CY + 190], [CX + 230, CY + 160]][i % 3];
    C.stamp(s.text, pos[0], pos[1], { t0, size: s.text.length > 14 ? 34 : 42, rot: (hash(i, key.length) - 0.5) * 0.3 });
  });
}

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const t = C.t, st = C.story;
    const ask = st.askLine;
    const dim = ask ? seg(t, ask.start, ask.start + 0.9) : 0;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= 1 - 0.62 * dim;
    // the heading
    const head = C.lines.find((l) => /now the part you skip/.test(l.n));
    const myths = C.figs.filter((f) => f.kind === 'myth' && f.start >= C.start - 1e-6 && f.start < C.end);
    const firstMyth = myths[0]?.start ?? Infinity;
    if (head) {
      const k = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, firstMyth - 0.1, firstMyth + 0.5));
      const a = ease.outCubic(seg(t, head.start - 0.1, head.start + 0.5));
      const hx = lerp(960, C.area.x + 30, k), hy = lerp(420, C.area.y + 54, k), sz = lerp(70, 30, k);
      text(ctx, 'SUPPLEMENTARY MATERIAL', hx, hy - sz * 1.2, { size: lerp(22, 15, k), mono: true, track: 0.3, align: k > 0.5 ? 'left' : 'center', color: P.ink2, alpha: a });
      text(ctx, 'The part you skip', hx, hy, { size: sz, style: 'italic', align: k > 0.5 ? 'left' : 'center', reveal: a });
    }
    // the current myth's cover, the previous one erasing
    let cur = null;
    for (const f of myths) if (f.start <= t) cur = f;
    if (cur) {
      const i = myths.indexOf(cur);
      const prev = myths[i - 1];
      if (prev && t < cur.start + 0.35) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.globalAlpha *= 1 - seg(t, cur.start, cur.start + 0.35);
        cover(ctx, C, prev.key, 1, cur.start, prev);
        stamps(ctx, C, prev.key, prev, cur.start);
        ctx.restore();
      }
      const p = ease.outCubic(seg(t, cur.start + (prev ? 0.2 : 0), cur.start + (prev ? 0.2 : 0) + 0.9));
      cover(ctx, C, cur.key, p, t, cur);
      stamps(ctx, C, cur.key, cur, t);
    }
    ctx.restore();
    // the baby, unimpressed (Reviewer 2)
    const r = 62;
    const enter = ease.outCubic(seg(t, C.start + 0.3, C.start + 1.2));
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= enter * (1 - 0.5 * dim);
    drawPaperBaby(ctx, {
      x: 1600, y: babyStandY(C.area.y + C.area.h - 30, r), r, tilt: 0.04,
      eyes: 'half', open: 0.5 * blink(t, 31) + 0.001, look: [-0.8, 0.1], mouth: 'line', brows: null,
      free: { mode: 'down' }, other: { mode: 'down' }, cap: { to: [3.2, -5], sway: t * 0.3 },
    });
    ctx.restore();
  },
};

// The break ("Here's what did."): the page goes blank. Only the paper, the caption rule and
// the HUD at n = ? (the compositor draws those and suppresses the legend). One beat of nothing.
export default { draw() {} };

cat > $V/scenes/drop.js <<'EOF'
// The drop: a single ink cell in the centre; the baby appears in it, orange, on "Me."; it
// divides on the beat, 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, the cells packing into a brain in profile;
// counters roll: brain volume to +101 %, 2,500 synapses / neuron that then grow ("then
// more") and are visibly pruned ("then pruned"); "weight x3" (and "length x1.5" if the take
// sings it) as a small table. The HUD settles on n = 1 on "I'm the replication" (story.js).

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease, hash } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { splinePts } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/ink.js';
import { P, text, drawPaperBaby, blink } from '../kit.js';

const BC = { x: 830, y: 462, s: 760 };
const OUTLINE = [[-0.5, 0.02], [-0.47, -0.14], [-0.38, -0.27], [-0.24, -0.35], [-0.06, -0.38], [0.12, -0.36], [0.28, -0.29], [0.4, -0.18], [0.48, -0.04],
  [0.49, 0.08], [0.44, 0.17], [0.36, 0.2], [0.42, 0.26], [0.38, 0.33], [0.26, 0.35], [0.17, 0.3], [0.11, 0.34], [0.08, 0.45], [0.0, 0.45], [0.0, 0.32],
  [-0.12, 0.27], [-0.28, 0.24], [-0.38, 0.2], [-0.46, 0.12], [-0.5, 0.02]].map(([x, y]) => [BC.x + x * BC.s, BC.y + y * BC.s]);
const R = [130, 104, 82, 64, 50, 39];

function inside(pt, poly) {
  let c = false;
  for (let i = 0, j = poly.length - 1; i < poly.length; j = i++) {
    const [xi, yi] = poly[i], [xj, yj] = poly[j];
    if ((yi > pt[1]) !== (yj > pt[1]) && pt[0] < ((xj - xi) * (pt[1] - yi)) / (yj - yi) + xi) c = !c;
  }
  return c;
}

/** 32 leaf cells inside the cerebrum, and the division tree above them (centroids). */
const TREE = (() => {
  const cand = [];
  for (let gy = -0.34; gy <= 0.24; gy += 0.075) for (let gx = -0.46; gx <= 0.46; gx += 0.08) {
    const p = [BC.x + (gx + (hash(cand.length, 5) - 0.5) * 0.02) * BC.s, BC.y + gy * BC.s];
    if (inside(p, OUTLINE) && !(gx > 0.0 && gy > 0.2)) cand.push(p);
  }
  // keep 32, evenly through the candidates
  const leaves = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) leaves.push(cand[Math.floor((i * cand.length) / 32)]);
  const levels = [];
  const build = (pts, depth, idx) => {
    const cx = pts.reduce((s, p) => s + p[0], 0) / pts.length, cy = pts.reduce((s, p) => s + p[1], 0) / pts.length;
    (levels[depth] ||= [])[idx] = [cx, cy];
    if (pts.length === 1) return;
    const xs = pts.map((p) => p[0]), ys = pts.map((p) => p[1]);
    const byX = Math.max(...xs) - Math.min(...xs) >= Math.max(...ys) - Math.min(...ys);
    const s = pts.slice().sort((a, b) => (byX ? a[0] - b[0] : a[1] - b[1]));
    build(s.slice(0, s.length / 2), depth + 1, idx * 2);
    build(s.slice(s.length / 2), depth + 1, idx * 2 + 1);
  };
  build(leaves, 0, 0);
  // synapses: neighbouring leaves
  const links = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < 32; j++) {
    const d = Math.hypot(leaves[i][0] - leaves[j][0], leaves[i][1] - leaves[j][1]);
    if (d < 92) links.push([i, j]);
  }
  return { levels, leaves, links };
})();

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const t = C.t, B = C.B;
    const l0 = C.lines[0] || null;
    const me = C.word('me', { line: 0 }) || (l0 ? l0.words[0] : null);
    const tMe = me ? me.start : C.start + 0.2;
    // division on the beats after "Me."
    const b0 = B.snap(tMe + B.beatDur * 0.75, 'beat');
    const div = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((k) => b0 + (k - 1) * B.beatDur);
    let L = 0, q = null;
    for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) { const a = div[k] - 0.06, b = div[k] + 0.3; if (t >= b) L = k + 1; else if (t >= a) { L = k + 1; q = ease.outBack(seg(t, a, b), 1.2); break; } }
    const pen = C.pen('cells', { width: 2.2 });
    const appear = ease.outCubic(seg(t, C.start, C.start + 0.4));
    // the brain outline draws in as the cells pack
    const outP = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, div[2], div[4] + 0.6));
    if (outP > 0) C.pen('brain', { width: 3 }).spline(OUTLINE, { p: outP, k: 'outline', smooth: 6 });
    // cells
    const lv = TREE.levels;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= appear;
    if (q == null) lv[L].forEach(([x, y], i) => pen.circle(x, y, R[L], { k: `c${L}-${i}` }));
    else lv[L].forEach(([x, y], i) => {
      const [px, py] = lv[L - 1][i >> 1];
      pen.circle(lerp(px, x, q), lerp(py, y, q), lerp(R[L - 1], R[L], q) * (1 - 0.1 * Math.sin(Math.PI * q)), { k: `c${L}-${i}` });
    });
    ctx.restore();
    // synapses: grow on "then more", pruned on "pruned"
    const tMore = C.wordTime('more', { line: 0 }), tPruned = C.wordTime('pruned', { line: 0 });
    const grow = seg(t, tMore - 0.2, tMore + 0.8), prune = seg(t, tPruned, tPruned + 0.6);
    if (L === 5 && grow > 0) {
      const sp = C.pen('synapses', { width: 1.3, color: P.ink2, wobble: 0.8 });
      TREE.links.forEach(([i, j], n) => {
        const pruned = hash(n, 3) < 0.5;
        const a = pruned ? 1 - prune : 1;
        if (a <= 0) return;
        ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
        const [x0, y0] = TREE.leaves[i], [x1, y1] = TREE.leaves[j];
        sp.line(x0, y0, x1, y1, { p: seg(grow, (n % 7) / 10, (n % 7) / 10 + 0.4), k: `s${n}` });
        ctx.restore();
      });
    }
    // the baby, orange, in the centre on "Me."
    const k = ease.outBack(seg(t, tMe - 0.05, tMe + 0.3), 1.6);
    if (k > 0) drawPaperBaby(ctx, { x: BC.x, y: BC.y - 10, r: 40 * k, eyes: 'open', open: blink(t, 55), look: [0, 0.1], mouth: 'smile', free: { mode: 'up', angle: t * 2 }, other: { mode: 'down' }, cap: { to: [1.4, -2.2], wires: false } });
    // counters
    const cx = 1440, rows = [];
    const tVol = C.wordTime('volume', { line: 0 }), tPct = C.wordTime('percent', { line: 0 });
    if (isFinite(tVol)) rows.push(['brain volume', `+${Math.round(101 * ease.outCubic(seg(t, tVol, isFinite(tPct) ? tPct : tVol + 1.5)))} %`, tVol]);
    const tSyn = C.wordTime('twenty five', { line: 0 }, { fallback: C.wordTime('synapses', { line: 0 }) });
    const tSyn2 = C.wordTime('synapses', { line: 0 });
    if (isFinite(tSyn)) {
      const base = 2500 * ease.outCubic(seg(t, tSyn, isFinite(tSyn2) ? tSyn2 + 0.3 : tSyn + 1));
      const v = base + 1500 * ease.outCubic(grow) - 1500 * ease.inOutCubic(prune);
      rows.push(['synapses / neuron', Math.round(v).toLocaleString('en-US'), tSyn]);
    }
    const tW = C.wordTime('tripled', { line: 0 });
    if (isFinite(tW)) rows.push(['weight', '× 3', tW]);
    const tLen = C.wordTime('length');
    if (isFinite(tLen)) rows.push(['length', '× 1.5', tLen]);
    rows.forEach(([label, val, t0], i) => {
      const a = ease.outCubic(seg(t, t0 - 0.1, t0 + 0.3));
      const y = 300 + i * 112;
      text(ctx, label, cx, y, { size: 30, style: 'italic', color: P.ink2, alpha: a });
      text(ctx, val, cx, y + 48, { size: 44, mono: true, alpha: a });
      if (a > 0) C.pen('table', { width: 1.4, color: P.ink2 }).line(cx, y + 66, cx + 320, y + 66, { p: a, k: `r${i}` });
    });
  },
};
EOF

const wrap = (s, n = 92) => { const out = []; let cur = ''; for (const w of s.split(/\s+/)) { if ((cur + ' ' + w).trim().length > n) { out.push(cur.trim()); cur = w; } else cur += ' ' + w; } if (cur.trim()) out.push(cur.trim()); return out; };

// "Replicate (n = 1)": a music video typeset like the methods section of a paper. Song module
// for the shared renderer. Every frame: map the loop tail onto the intro, then paint the page
// (paper, the bridge's wash), the plates (the previous one erasing for a moment, the current
// one drawing), the caption rule and the figure caption, the lyric legend and the HUD.
// 16:9 is the primary layout; 9:16 stacks the 16:9 page's figure over a taller caption.

import { seg, ease, trap } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { makePen } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/ink.js';
import { FONTS, P, drawPaper, drawWash } from './page.js';
import { timeline, SECTION_PLATES } from './edit.js';
import { makeStory } from './story.js';
import { drawLegend } from './legend.js';
import { drawCaption } from './caption.js';
import { drawHud } from './hud.js';
import { makePlateContext } from './plate.js';
import title from './scenes/title.js';
import outro from './scenes/outro.js';
import chorus, { finalScene } from './scenes/chorus.js';
import bridge from './scenes/bridge.js';
import blank from './scenes/blank.js';
import drop from './scenes/drop.js';
import { LAB_SCENES } from './scenes/labs/index.js';

export const scenes = { title, outro, chorus, final: finalScene, bridge, blank, drop, ...LAB_SCENES };

/** 9:16: where the 16:9 page's plates go, and the taller caption block under them. */
const TALL = {
  page: { y: 300, s: 1080 / 1920 },
  caption: { x: 56, ruleY: 1000, y: 1056, maxW: 968, size: 38, minSize: 30, rows: 2 },
  legend: { x: 56, y: 1214, maxW: 968, size: 54, minSize: 44, rows: 5 },
  hud: { x: 52, y: 64, size: 36 },
};
const ERASE = 0.4;

function drawPlates(ctx, env, t, w) {
  const W = env.T.windows;
  const i = W.indexOf(w);
  const prev = i > 0 ? W[i - 1] : null;
  if (prev && t < w.start + ERASE) {
    // the previous figure is erased left to right while the new one starts drawing
    const k = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, w.start, w.start + ERASE));
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= 1 - k;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(-60 + k * 2040, -100, 4000, 2400); ctx.clip();
    scenes[prev.plate].draw(ctx, makePlateContext(ctx, env, prev.end - 1e-4, prev, { erasing: true }));
    ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.save();
  scenes[w.plate].draw(ctx, makePlateContext(ctx, env, t, w));
  ctx.restore();
}

export default {
  id: 'baby-facts',
  layouts: { '16x9': { w: 1920, h: 1080 }, '9x16': { w: 1080, h: 1920 } },
  defaultLayout: '16x9',
  fonts: FONTS,
  timeline,
  sectionPlates: SECTION_PLATES,   // render.mjs reports these at start-up (MISSING if absent)
  scenes,
  audioCandidates(audio) {
    const named = /\(([^()]+\.(?:mp3|wav|m4a))\)/.exec(String(audio.source || ''));
    return named ? [named[1]] : [];
  },
  setup(env) {
    env.story = makeStory(env);
  },
  /** The song time whose frame a loop-tail frame repeats (render.mjs --check compares the two). */
  loopSource(env, t) { return env.story.loopTime(t); },
  /** Extra contact-sheet cells: the stages inside the long section plates. */
  sheetCells(env) {
    const out = [];
    const st = env.story;
    for (const w of env.T.windows) {
      if (w.plate === 'title' && !w.params.loop) for (const t of [3, 12]) if (t < w.end) out.push({ t, label: `intro ${t}s` });
      if (w.plate === 'chorus' || w.plate === 'final') for (const f of [0.22, 0.6, 0.8]) out.push({ t: w.start + w.dur * f, label: `${w.plate} ${Math.round(f * 100)}%` });
      if (w.plate === 'drop') for (const f of [0.25, 0.8]) out.push({ t: w.start + w.dur * f, label: `drop ${Math.round(f * 100)}%` });
    }
    for (const f of st.figs.filter((x) => x.kind === 'myth')) out.push({ t: Math.min(f.end - 0.2, f.start + 3), label: `myth ${f.key}` });
    if (st.tail) out.push({ t: env.duration - 0.05, label: 'last frame' });
    return out;
  },
  frame(ctx, t, env) {
    const st = env.story;
    const tm = st.loopTime(t);
    const w = st.windowFor(tm);
    const tall = env.layout === '9x16';
    drawPaper(ctx, env);
    if (st.bridgeW) drawWash(ctx, env, trap(tm, st.bridgeW.start + 0.2, st.bridgeW.end, 0.7, 0.4));
    ctx.save();
    if (tall) { ctx.translate(0, TALL.page.y); ctx.scale(TALL.page.s, TALL.page.s); }
    drawPlates(ctx, env, tm, w);
    ctx.restore();
    const pen = makePen(ctx, 'page', { color: P.ink });
    // "So when you ask me...": everything but the HUD fades to the lighter ink
    const ask = st.askLine && w === st.bridgeW ? seg(tm, st.askLine.start, st.askLine.start + 0.9) : 0;
    const fig = st.figureAt(tm);
    drawCaption(ctx, tm, fig, st.prevFigure(fig), { pen, ruleP: st.ruleP(tm), alpha: 1 - 0.6 * ask, ...(tall ? TALL.caption : {}) });
    if (w.plate !== 'blank') drawLegend(ctx, tm, env.L, { alpha: 1 - 0.5 * ask, ...(tall ? TALL.legend : {}) });
    drawHud(ctx, st.hud(tm), pen.sub('hud'), tall ? TALL.hud : {});
  },
};
EOF

import re
V = '/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/'
def edit(path, pairs):
    s = open(path).read()
    for old, new in pairs:
        assert s.count(old) == 1, (path, old[:70], s.count(old))
        s = s.replace(old, new)
    open(path, 'w').write(s)

# 1. paper mottling: two subtle octaves
edit(V + 'page.js', [("""  // mottling: a coarse random field, smoothly upscaled
  const mw = 64, mh = Math.max(8, Math.round((64 * ph) / pw));
  const m = document.createElement('canvas');
  m.width = mw; m.height = mh;
  const mg = m.getContext('2d');
  const md = mg.createImageData(mw, mh);
  for (let i = 0; i < mw * mh; i++) {
    const v = hash(i, 9001);
    const base = v < 0.5 ? 0 : 255;
    md.data[i * 4] = base; md.data[i * 4 + 1] = base; md.data[i * 4 + 2] = base;
    md.data[i * 4 + 3] = Math.round(Math.abs(v - 0.5) * 2 * 255);
  }
  mg.putImageData(md, 0, 0);
  g.save();
  g.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
  g.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high';
  g.globalAlpha = 0.05;
  g.drawImage(m, 0, 0, pw, ph);
  g.restore();""", """  // mottling: two faint random fields, smoothly upscaled (the stock is not perfectly even)
  for (const [cols, alpha, salt] of [[24, 0.022, 9001], [96, 0.014, 9002]]) {
    const mw = cols, mh = Math.max(4, Math.round((cols * ph) / pw));
    const m = document.createElement('canvas');
    m.width = mw; m.height = mh;
    const mg = m.getContext('2d');
    const md = mg.createImageData(mw, mh);
    for (let i = 0; i < mw * mh; i++) {
      const v = hash(i, salt);
      const base = v < 0.5 ? 60 : 255;
      md.data[i * 4] = base; md.data[i * 4 + 1] = base * 0.95; md.data[i * 4 + 2] = base * 0.85;
      md.data[i * 4 + 3] = Math.round(Math.abs(v - 0.5) * 2 * 255);
    }
    mg.putImageData(md, 0, 0);
    g.save();
    g.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
    g.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high';
    g.globalAlpha = alpha;
    g.drawImage(m, 0, 0, pw, ph);
    g.restore();
  }""")])

Now the cap wires: individual leads to a hub, then a narrow ribbon cable trailing off.

BashRewrite the cap wires and re-render review stills
input
description Rewrite the cap wires and re-render review stills
timeout 300000
/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/.venv/bin/python -I - <<'EOF'
p = '/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/clawd/baby.js'
s = open(p).read()
i0 = s.index("  // wires first (under the electrodes): each runs up from its electrode, the bundle trails off\n")
i1 = s.index("  for (const [ex, ey] of E) {", i0)
new = """  // wires first (under the electrodes): each lead runs to a hub above the cap, and from there
  // a narrow ribbon cable trails off toward `to` (far away = off-frame), sagging a little
  if (o.wires !== false) {
    const to = o.to || [1.7, -4.6];
    const sway = o.sway ?? 0;
    const hub = [0.42 * r, -1.38 * r];
    ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(0.6, lw * 0.32);
    E.forEach(([ex, ey]) => {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(ex * r, ey * r);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(ex * r * 0.75 + hub[0] * 0.25, Math.min(ey * r, hub[1]) - 0.12 * r, hub[0], hub[1]);
      ctx.stroke();
    });
    const tx = to[0] * r, ty = to[1] * r;
    const d = Math.hypot(tx - hub[0], ty - hub[1]) || 1;
    const nx = -(ty - hub[1]) / d, ny = (tx - hub[0]) / d;
    const c1 = [hub[0] + (tx - hub[0]) * 0.3 + nx * (0.25 + 0.08 * Math.sin(sway)) * r, hub[1] + (ty - hub[1]) * 0.3 + ny * 0.25 * r + 0.35 * r];
    const c2 = [hub[0] + (tx - hub[0]) * 0.7 - nx * (0.2 + 0.08 * Math.sin(sway + 1.3)) * r, hub[1] + (ty - hub[1]) * 0.7 + 0.3 * r];
    for (const k of [-1, 0, 1]) {
      const off = k * 0.028 * r;
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(0.6, lw * (k ? 0.3 : 0.38));
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(hub[0] + nx * off * 0.3, hub[1] + ny * off * 0.3);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo(c1[0] + nx * off, c1[1] + ny * off, c2[0] + nx * off, c2[1] + ny * off, tx + nx * off, ty + ny * off);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
"""
s = s[:i0] + new + s[i1:]
open(p, 'w').write(s)
print('ok')
EOF

BashBuild the radio-cut simulation data and run check and stills on it
input
description Build the radio-cut simulation data and run check and stills on it
timeout 300000
V=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video; cat > $V/tools/radio_sim.mjs <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Simulate the radio cut on the keeper's timing: drop every line of data/lyrics.json whose
// text the radio sheet (suno_lyrics_v2_radio.txt) does not have, and write the pair to
// data-radio-sim/. Used to test that figures keyed to absent lines degrade gracefully:
//   node songs/baby-facts/video/tools/radio_sim.mjs
//   node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/baby-facts/video --layout 16x9 --data data-radio-sim --check
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { parseSheet } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/sheet.js';
import { norm } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/lyrics.js';

const here = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const video = path.join(here, '..');
const key = (s) => norm(s).replace(/:/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
const radio = new Set(parseSheet(fs.readFileSync(path.join(video, '..', 'suno_lyrics_v2_radio.txt'), 'utf8')).map((l) => key(l.text)));
const lyrics = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(video, 'data', 'lyrics.json'), 'utf8'));
const kept = lyrics.lines.filter((l) => radio.has(key(l.text)));
const dropped = lyrics.lines.filter((l) => !radio.has(key(l.text)));
const out = path.join(video, 'data-radio-sim');
fs.mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true });
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'lyrics.json'), JSON.stringify({ ...lyrics, lines: kept, notes: `radio-cut simulation of data/lyrics.json (tools/radio_sim.mjs): ${dropped.length} lines dropped` }));
fs.copyFileSync(path.join(video, 'data', 'audio.json'), path.join(out, 'audio.json'));
console.log(`kept ${kept.length}, dropped ${dropped.length}:\n  ${dropped.map((l) => `[${l.section}] ${l.text}`).join('\n  ')}`);
EOF
node $V/tools/radio_sim.mjs && S=/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jalex-projects-clawdpop/ca2089be-6e29-4607-84a4-ba095fc3fca1/scratchpad && node /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video --layout 16x9 --w 960 --h 540 --data data-radio-sim --check --stills 85,104,125,170,205 --stills-dir $S/radio 2>&1 | grep -v "render\] still"

Radio-sim runs clean: Hirsh-Pasek's window vanishes, check passes. A small tool to print the figure table (useful for the README and the second pass):

BashAdd a figure-table tool and print both edits
input
description Add a figure-table tool and print both edits
V=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video; cat > $V/tools/figures.mjs <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Print the edit for a timing pair: windows (plates) and the numbered figures with their
// captions, as the renderer will build them. No browser needed.
//   node songs/baby-facts/video/tools/figures.mjs [--data data-radio-sim]
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { makeBeats } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/beats.js';
import { makeLyrics } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/lyrics.js';
import { fmtTime } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { makePlan } from '../edit.js';
import { captionRuns } from '../caption.js';

const here = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
const dir = path.join(here, '..', argv.includes('--data') ? argv[argv.indexOf('--data') + 1] : 'data');
const audio = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, 'audio.json'), 'utf8'));
const lyrics = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, 'lyrics.json'), 'utf8'));
const B = makeBeats(audio);
const L = makeLyrics(lyrics, B);
const plan = makePlan(L, B);
console.log(`windows (${plan.entries.length}):`);
for (const e of plan.entries) console.log(`  ${fmtTime(e.at.t ?? e.at.time ?? 0).padStart(8)}  ${e.plate.padEnd(12)} ${e.fromSection ? `[${e.fromSection}]` : ''}  ${(e.params.lines || []).length} line(s)`);
console.log(`figures (${plan.figs.length}):`);
for (const f of plan.figs) console.log(`  ${fmtTime(f.start).padStart(8)}  ${f.key.padEnd(20)} ${captionRuns(f).map((r) => r.text).join('')}`);
if (plan.loop) console.log(`loop tail at ${fmtTime(plan.loop.start)} (shift ${plan.loop.shift.toFixed(3)} s)`);
EOF
node $V/tools/figures.mjs; echo; node $V/tools/figures.mjs --data data-radio-sim | sed -n '/figures/,$p'
