While the draft renders, the song README with the scene interface for the second pass.

BashWrite the song README with the scene interface
input
description Write the song README with the scene interface
cat > /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/README.md <<'EOF'
# Replicate (n = 1): the video

The treatment is `../TREATMENT.md`: a music video typeset like the methods section of a paper,
every lyric line a figure, the baby the one coloured thing on the page. It is built on the
shared renderer (`shared/renderer/`, the song-code contract is described in
`songs/2am-crying/video/README.md`). Every frame is a pure function of song time.

This directory is the **spine**. The page, the three permanent elements, every section plate
and the loop are done. Every verse figure is a placeholder: the right caption and number,
the legend, an empty framed figure area, and the baby in the cap standing in it, curious.
The second pass replaces the placeholders, one lab module at a time (see "The scene
interface" below).

## Commands

All from the repo root (`/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop`). `--layout 16x9` is required:
render.mjs picks its default size from the flag, not from the song.

```sh
node shared/renderer/scripts/serve.mjs     # preview:
# http://localhost:8123/songs/baby-facts/video/index.html?t=50
# http://localhost:8123/songs/baby-facts/video/index.html?plate=tronick      (a lab's hero frame)
# http://localhost:8123/songs/baby-facts/video/index.html?layout=9x16&t=12
# http://localhost:8123/songs/baby-facts/video/index.html?data=data-radio-sim/&t=100

A=songs/baby-facts/audio/runA/replicate-n-1-6b228558.mp3
caffeinate -i node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/baby-facts/video --audio $A \
  --layout 16x9 --w 960 --h 540 --fps 30 --out songs/baby-facts/video/renders/draft-spine-540p.mp4
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/baby-facts/video --layout 16x9 --w 960 --h 540 --check
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/baby-facts/video --layout 16x9 --w 960 --h 540 \
  --stills 12,50,57,62 --stills-dir songs/baby-facts/video/renders/stills
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/baby-facts/video --layout 16x9 \
  --sheet songs/baby-facts/video/renders/plates.png --title plates
node shared/renderer/scripts/smoke.mjs --song songs/baby-facts/video --q t=3 --q "layout=9x16&t=57"
node songs/baby-facts/video/tools/figures.mjs [--data data-radio-sim]   # the edit: windows, figure numbers, captions
node songs/baby-facts/video/tools/radio_sim.mjs                          # rebuild data-radio-sim/
```

The full 540p draft takes about 7 minutes with 9 workers on this Mac. Run it under
`caffeinate -i` (the laptop sleeps otherwise).

## Data

- `data/`: the keeper take, runA `6b228558` (269.6 s, 95.6 BPM), copied from
  `../audio/runA/data/`. Lines carry `section` (intro, verse1, chorus1, verse2, verse3, chorus2,
  verse4, bridge, break, drop, final, outro). `audio.json` names the mp3 in `source` (the
  preview plays it) and the sheet `suno_lyrics_v1_short.txt` in `lyrics_sheet`.
- `data-radio-sim/`: the same timing with the eight lines the radio cut
  (`suno_lyrics_v2_radio.txt`) drops removed (`tools/radio_sim.mjs`). It exists to test absent
  lines: `--data data-radio-sim --check`. It has no audio of its own.
- A new take only needs its `lyrics.json` and `audio.json` in `data/`. Figures, numbers and
  the loop are all derived from the lyric content.

## The page and the permanent elements

| element | file | notes |
|---|---|---|
| paper | `page.js` | `#f4efe3` stock, a 32 px (5 mm) `#e6dfcf` grid, per-pixel grain, faint mottling, darker edges; baked once per output size, blitted every frame |
| ink | `shared/renderer/core/ink.js` | the wobble pen: strokes are filled ribbons that wobble by noise of their arclength, seeded by plate + stroke key; `p` draws an exact prefix, so strokes grow without wiggling |
| HUD | `hud.js`, text from `story.js` | `n = 1`, mono in a hand-ruled box; the bridge shows each myth's n as it is named (`n = 4`, `n = 36 (students)`, `$105,000` for two beats on "Georgia", `n = 0 words`, `n = 6 (1977)`, `n = 106 (2016)`, `n = 567 · 37 labs`, `n = 42 · 1 h / month`), a fast shuffle of them on "So when you ask me", `n = ?` in the break, rolling digits in the drop that settle to `n = 1` on "replication" |
| caption | `caption.js` | the rule, then `Fig. k.` (italic), the title, the citation and note in the light ink: "Fig. 7. Growth saltation in one infant (Lampl, 1992)." Written in left to right at each figure start |
| legend | `legend.js` | the current line in the serif, left-aligned, two rows at most, wrapping at a clause, 40 px (34 min) at 1080p; unsung words light ink, sung words full ink with a wipe through the active word (core `karaoke.js wordProgress`); parentheticals italic and smaller. Hidden in the break |

Fonts: `index.html` declares `Paper Serif` (Iowan Old Style, via `local()`) and `Paper Mono`
(SF Mono if installed system-wide, else Menlo) so the boot can verify they loaded. SF Mono is
only bundled inside Terminal.app on this Mac, so the mono is Menlo, the treatment's fallback.

## The edit (`edit.js`)

A **window** is what the engine draws: one plate per window, windows in song order. A
**figure** is what the caption numbers. They are not the same thing:

| section | windows (plate) | figures |
|---|---|---|
| intro | `title` (from 0 s) | none |
| verse | one per lab, consecutive lines of the same lab (plate = lab key) | one per lab |
| chorus | `chorus` | the bench (lines before the first "Will it replicate?") and the replication |
| bridge | `bridge` | one per myth line (`MYTHS` in labs.js); "Now the part you skip" and "So when you ask me" have none |
| break | `blank` | none |
| drop | `drop` | one (brain growth) |
| final | `final` | the bench again and the grid |
| outro | `outro`, then the loop tail `title` (`loop: true`) | none |

Windows open 0.2 s before their first word (no beat snap: verse motion follows the words).
Figures are numbered in song order over the lines present in the take; a lab whose lines are
all absent has no window and the numbers after it close up. On runA there are 29 figures
(Lampl is Fig. 7, Hamlin Fig. 19, the bridge's helper Fig. 25, the final grid Fig. 29); on the
radio sim 26. Section plates are registered in `SECTION_PLATES` and reported by render.mjs:

```
[render] section plate ok: intro title -> title 0:00.00-0:14.73 (14.7s)
[render] section plate ok: chorus bench -> chorus 0:44.90-1:04.98 (20.1s), chorus 2:17.77-2:28.15 (10.4s)
[render] section plate ok: bridge -> bridge 2:58.12-3:40.06 (41.9s)
[render] section plate ok: break -> blank 3:40.06-3:41.89 (1.8s)
[render] section plate ok: drop -> drop 3:41.89-3:59.07 (17.2s)
[render] section plate ok: final grid -> final 3:59.07-4:20.26 (21.2s)
[render] section plate ok: outro page -> outro 4:20.26-4:28.72 (8.5s)
[render] section plate ok: outro loop -> title 4:28.72-4:29.60 (0.9s)
```

## The loop

The song ends on paper and the HUD (`n = 1`), and the intro begins the same way: the title,
the rule and the legend only arrive at "Fifteen babies". So the loop is a short tail window
after the last outro line has left the legend: its frames are the intro's first frames, frame
for frame (`story.loopTime`). The tail's shift is a whole number of beats (268.716 s on runA),
so the intro's beat-locked heartbeat trace restarts on the tail's beats. `--check` compares
tail frames with their intro frames and fails if they differ or the tail is missing.

## The scene interface (for the second pass)

Each lab is one module, `scenes/labs/<key>.js`, where `<key>` is the lab's key in `labs.js`,
its plate id and its window id. It exports:

```js
export default {
  draw(ctx, C) { /* paint the figure for time C.t inside C.area */ },
};
```

That is all a lab needs. It is registered already: `scenes/labs/index.js` imports every lab
module, `labs.js` holds its rule (the patterns that send lines to it), its caption title,
citation and note, and a one-line brief from the treatment. **To fill a lab, edit only its own
file.** The rest of the spine is frozen for the second pass: `kit.js`, `plate.js`, `edit.js`,
`labs.js`, `story.js`, `song.js`, the overlays and the section plates. A helper only your lab
needs goes in your lab file (or a sibling `scenes/labs/<key>-*.js` you own). If a caption title
or citation must change, say so rather than editing `labs.js`, so two agents never edit one file.

### What C provides

C is rebuilt for every frame by `plate.js` from (song time, window). Nothing in it carries
state between frames. **None of its lookups throw.**

| group | fields |
|---|---|
| time | `t` (song time, loop-mapped), `fps`, `start`, `end`, `dur`, `lt` (= t - start), `p` (lt / dur), `inP` (0..1 over the first 0.6 s, eased: the draw-in), `erasing` (true while your plate is the previous one being erased; t is then held at your window's end) |
| window | `win`, `plate` (= the lab key), `params`, `lab` (the labs.js entry: key, title, cite, note, brief), `seed` |
| figures | `fig` (the figure the caption shows now: `num`, `title`, `cite`, `key`, `start`, `end`, `lines`), `figs` (all), `num` ({ key: number }), `figNum(key)` (number or null) |
| lines | `lines` (this lab's lines **present in the take**, in order; each has `text`, `n` (normalized), `start`, `end`, `words`), `line` (the current one: the last that has started, else the first), `lineIndex`, `lineP` (0..1 through it), `words` (its words: `w`, `start`, `end`, `conf`, `syl?`, `backing`) |
| lookups | `has(text)` → bool; `lineFor(text)` → line or null; `word(text, { line, nth, backing })` → word or null; `wordTime(text, o)` → start or `o.fallback` (default **Infinity**, "never": `t >= C.wordTime('drop')` is simply false when the word is absent); `wordEnd(text, o)`; `since(text, o)` → t - wordTime; `wseg(text, dur, o)` → 0..1 over `dur` seconds from the word (0 when absent); `seg(a, b)`, `eseg(a, b, easing)`. Text matches normalized words, exact first, then contains: "thirty six" matches "thirty-six"; a multi-word text returns the first word of the run. `o.line` is an index into `lines` or a text the line contains |
| beat | `B` (the beat grid: `beatDur`, `snap`, `beatEnv`, ...), `beat` (B.beatPos(t)), `onBeat(time, mode = 'beat')` (snap a time to the beat: stamps hit on the beat) |
| drawing | `area` ({ x: 120, y: 112, w: 1680, h: 712 }: draw only inside it), `page` (1920 x 1080), `ruleY` (864), `P` (palette: `paper`, `grid`, `ink`, `ink2` (light ink), `ink3`, the baby's clay keys), `SERIF`, `MONO`; `pen(label, opts)` → a wobble pen seeded by your plate + label; `text(s, x, y, o)` (o: size, style 'italic', weight, mono, color, align, alpha, track, reveal 0..1); `stamp(label, x, y, { t0, size, rot })`; `baby(o)`; `inkBaby(o)`; `adult(o)`; `axes(pen, x0, y0, w, h, o)`; `placeholder(o)` |
| engine | `env`, `L` (lyrics), `story` |

Coordinates are always the 16:9 page (1920 x 1080 design px), in every layout: the 9:16 layout
scales the whole page into its upper half and draws its own caption and legend. The
compositor owns the paper, the caption, the legend and the HUD; a lab paints its figure only.
It also erases the previous plate (fade and left-to-right wipe over 0.4 s) while yours draws
in, so draw your figure in over `C.inP` and do not clear the page.

**The pen** (`C.pen('axes')`, or `makePen` from `kit.js`): `line(x0, y0, x1, y1, o)`,
`poly(points, o)`, `spline(points, o)`, `curve(p0, c1, c2, p1, o)`, `quad`, `arc(cx, cy, r, a0,
a1, o)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, o)`, `circle`, `rect(x, y, w, h, o)` (four overshooting
strokes), `rrect`, `arrow`, `hatch(region, { angle, gap, p })` (region = [x, y, w, h], a
polygon, or a path function with `o.bbox`), `dotted(points, { gap, r })`, `dashed`. Every call
takes `o.p` (drawn fraction), `o.k` (a stable stroke key: give one to any stroke that appears
partway through a figure, or every later stroke changes its wobble), `color`, `width`,
`wobble`, `alpha`. `pen.with({ ... })` shares the seed and stroke counter with other defaults;
`pen.sub(label)` is an independent pen.

**The baby**: `C.baby({ x, y, r, ... })` draws baby Clawd (`shared/clawd/baby.js`) on the
page: clay colours, no phone (both nubs free: `free` and `other`, modes `down`, `wave`, `up`,
`hold` (to `at`), `point`, `none`), the EEG cap on (`cap: { to: [x, y] }` in r units sets
where the wire ribbon goes; `cap: null` takes it off). (x, y) is the face centre; the body is
2.5r wide; `babyStandY(groundY, r)` from kit.js places it standing. Expressions as in the 2am
video: `eyes` (`open`, `half`, `closed`, `squeeze`, `wide`, `focus`), `open` (lid 0..1; `blink(t,
seed)` from kit.js), `look`, `mouth` (`line`, `o`, `smile`, `grin`, `press`, `none`),
`mouthOpen`, `brows`, `tears` (pale blue, the only other colour allowed in the baby), `blush`,
`tilt`. `C.inkBaby(o)` draws an ink outline copy (the other data points); `ink: 0..1` fades
between. `C.adult(o)` draws adult Clawd as ink line art (`shared/clawd/adult.js`, `style:
'ink'`): `x, y` (the ground point), `u` (unit; the body is 10u x 6u), `pose` ('stand' | 'sit'),
`eyes`, `mouth`, `look`, `lean`, `arms`, `p` (draw-in).

### Rules of the page

- Only the baby is coloured. Adults, apparatus, other data points, stamps: ink.
- Ink lines, hatching for shade (not fills), the light ink for grid lines, error bars and leaders.
- Everything is drawn in time: strokes grow (`p`), numbers roll, curves plot left to right.
  Nothing pops in except rubber stamps, which hit on the beat (`C.stamp(..., { t0: C.onBeat(time) })`).
- In verses the motion follows the words, not the beat. Hero moments land on the sung word.
- Pure: no `Date`, no `Math.random`, no state kept between calls. Use `hash`, `noise1` and
  `rng(seed)` from `core/util.js`, seeded per object.
- Lines that share a lab share one figure that keeps developing; `C.lineIndex` and the word
  lookups say where in it we are.

### Absent lines

The radio cut drops lines (Grant, both Hirsh-Pasek lines, Lampl's "terrible" and "ninety-five
percent" lines, Fenton's event counts, neonatal imitation, thirty million words), and a later
take may drop others. The spine handles this at three levels:

1. A lab with none of its lines in the take has no window, no figure number, and the numbers
   after it close up; cross-references in captions (`{helper}` in a note) follow, and a
   `[bracketed]` part of a note that names an absent figure is dropped.
2. Inside a lab, `C.lines` holds only the lines present. Test for a beat of the figure with
   `C.has('front page')` or `C.lineFor('Grant')`, and key motion to words with `wordTime`,
   whose default "never" (Infinity) quietly skips a moment whose word is absent.
3. A verse line no rule matches continues the previous lab of its section (`[labs] ... continue
   the previous lab` in the render log), or, with none before it, gets the `unmatched`
   placeholder (`[labs] ... 'unmatched' placeholder`).

Test with the radio sim: `--data data-radio-sim --check`, `?data=data-radio-sim/` in the preview.

### Checking a lab

- Preview `?plate=<key>` (the hero frame, 75 percent into the window), then scrub; `[` `]` jump
  between plates.
- `tools/figures.mjs` prints each lab's window time, so `--stills` can hit its words.
- `--check` must still pass (determinism and the loop).

## Files

- `song.js`: the compositor (paper, wash, plates with the erase, caption, legend, HUD; the
  9:16 arrangement).
- `edit.js`: windows, figures, numbering, cross-references, the loop tail, `SECTION_PLATES`.
- `labs.js`: the rule table (labs, bridge myths, section figures).
- `story.js`: song-level curves (loop, figure at t, HUD text, heartbeat time, rule).
- `plate.js`: the C contract. `kit.js`: the plate kit (frozen). `page.js`: paper, palette, fonts.
- `legend.js`, `caption.js`, `hud.js`: the three permanent elements.
- `scenes/`: `title.js` (intro and loop tail), `chorus.js` (chorus and final), `bridge.js`,
  `blank.js`, `drop.js`, `outro.js`, `labs/` (one module per lab, all placeholders for now).
- `tools/`: `figures.mjs`, `radio_sim.mjs`.
- Shared with the 2am video: `shared/clawd/baby.js` (baby Clawd, with the opt-in EEG cap and
  phone-free nubs), `shared/clawd/adult.js` (adult Clawd, with the opt-in ink style),
  `shared/renderer/core/ink.js` (the pen). The 2am video re-exports both characters and its
  frames are pixel-identical to before the move.

## Departures and gaps (spine)

- The heartbeat trace follows the beat grid (one ECG complex per beat): audio.json has no
  heartbeat onsets and the intro's heartbeat is not separable from the narration in the mix.
  An `audio.json` heartbeat list would be a drop-in replacement in `story.js`.
- The title page adds a small "METHODS · A STUDY OF ONE" kicker over the title.
- The break hides the legend, as the treatment lists only paper, rule and HUD.
- The chorus has two figure numbers (bench, replication); that is what puts Lampl at Fig. 7 as
  in the treatment's example, but the final grid is Fig. 29, not 31.
- Bridge covers are simple ink sketches; the treatment's extra beats (the 1998 door, the Georgia
  peach, the receipt printing, the 37 hills multiplying in place) are partly there.
- 9:16 is functional but plain: the 16:9 page scaled into the top half, a taller caption and
  legend below.

BashCount saturated pixels by hue in the extracted frames
input
description Count saturated pixels by hue in the extracted frames
S=/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jalex-projects-clawdpop/ca2089be-6e29-4607-84a4-ba095fc3fca1/scratchpad; /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/.venv/bin/python -I - <<EOF
import numpy as np, glob
from PIL import Image
files = sorted(glob.glob('$S/mp4/f-*.png'))
for f in files:
    a = np.asarray(Image.open(f).convert('RGB')).astype(np.float32) / 255
    mx, mn = a.max(2), a.min(2)
    sat = np.where(mx > 0, (mx - mn) / np.maximum(mx, 1e-6), 0)
    m = (sat > 0.35) & (mx > 0.25)
    n = int(m.sum())
    if n == 0: print(f.split('/')[-1], 'no saturated px'); continue
    r, g, b = a[..., 0][m], a[..., 1][m], a[..., 2][m]
    # hue in degrees
    mxv, mnv = np.maximum(np.maximum(r, g), b), np.minimum(np.minimum(r, g), b)
    d = np.maximum(mxv - mnv, 1e-6)
    h = np.where(mxv == r, ((g - b) / d) % 6, np.where(mxv == g, (b - r) / d + 2, (r - g) / d + 4)) * 60
    warm = ((h < 40) | (h > 330)).sum()
    other = n - warm
    print(f.split('/')[-1].ljust(14), 'saturated', n, 'warm(baby family)', int(warm), 'other', int(other), ('hues:' + str(np.unique((h[~((h < 40) | (h > 330))] // 30 * 30).astype(int)))) if other else '')
EOF

BashLook for heartbeat thumps in the drums, bass and other stems12:30 PM
input
description Look for heartbeat thumps in the drums, bass and other stems
timeout 180000
W=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/work/replicate-n-1-6b228558-dd2de1a324/stems; /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/.venv/bin/python -I - <<EOF
import numpy as np, soundfile as sf
def env(name, a, b):
    x, sr = sf.read('$W/' + name + '.wav', start=int(a * 44100), stop=int(b * 44100), always_2d=True)
    x = x.mean(1)
    # low band energy (heartbeat thumps are 30-120 Hz): crude lowpass by moving average
    k = int(sr / 150)
    lp = np.convolve(x, np.ones(k) / k, 'same')
    hop = int(sr * 0.01)
    e = np.sqrt(np.convolve(lp ** 2, np.ones(hop * 3) / (hop * 3), 'same'))[::hop]
    return 20 * np.log10(e + 1e-9), sr
for name in ['drums', 'bass', 'other']:
    for a, b in [(0, 15.5), (258, 269.6)]:
        db, sr = env(name, a, b)
        pk = [i for i in range(20, len(db) - 20) if db[i] == db[i - 15:i + 16].max() and db[i] - db[i - 25:i].min() > 9 and db[i] > db.max() - 30]
        ts = [round(a + i * 0.01, 2) for i in pk]
        d = np.diff(ts)
        print(f'{name:6s} {a:5.0f}-{b:5.1f}s peak {db.max():6.1f} dB  thumps {len(ts):3d}  median gap {np.median(d) if len(d) else 0:.3f}s  first: {ts[:14]}')
EOF

BashPrint the drums and bass envelopes over the intro12:30 PM
input
description Print the drums and bass envelopes over the intro
timeout 180000
W=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/work/replicate-n-1-6b228558-dd2de1a324/stems; /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/.venv/bin/python -I - <<EOF
import numpy as np, soundfile as sf
for name in ['drums','bass']:
    x, sr = sf.read('$W/' + name + '.wav', start=0, stop=int(16 * 44100), always_2d=True)
    x = x.mean(1)
    hop = int(sr * 0.02)
    e = np.array([np.sqrt(np.mean(x[i:i+hop]**2)) for i in range(0, len(x)-hop, hop)])
    db = 20*np.log10(e+1e-9)
    print(name, 'max', db.max().round(1))
    for s in range(0, 16, 2):
        seg = db[s*50:(s+2)*50]
        print(f'  {s:2d}-{s+2:2d}s', ''.join(' .:-=+*#%@'[min(9, max(0, int((v - (db.max()-45)) / 4.5)))] for v in seg))
    # autocorrelation of the onset strength in 0-14 s
    o = np.maximum(0, np.diff(db[:700]))
    o = o - o.mean()
    ac = np.correlate(o, o, 'full')[len(o)-1:]
    lags = np.arange(len(ac)) * 0.02
    rng = (lags > 0.3) & (lags < 1.5)
    best = lags[rng][np.argsort(ac[rng])[-4:]]
    print('  autocorr best periods (s):', np.sort(best).round(2), ' beat =', round(60/95.566, 3))
EOF

V=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video; sed -i '' 's/^function layoutRuns(ctx, runs, o) {/\/** Caption layout: tokens, widths, rows and the size that fits (exported for layout sweeps). *\/\nexport function layoutRuns(ctx, runs, o) {/' $V/caption.js && grep -n "export function layoutRuns" $V/caption.js; cat > /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jalex-projects-clawdpop/ca2089be-6e29-4607-84a4-ba095fc3fca1/scratchpad/sweep.mjs <<'EOF'
// Lay out every legend line and every caption, for each data dir and layout, and report overflow.
import puppeteer from '/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/node_modules/puppeteer-core/lib/puppeteer/puppeteer-core.js';
import { createServer, listen } from '/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/scripts/serve.mjs';
const srv = createServer('/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop');
const port = await listen(srv, 0);
const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome', headless: true });
let bad = 0;
try {
  for (const data of ['data/', 'data-radio-sim/']) for (const layout of ['16x9', '9x16']) {
    const page = await browser.newPage();
    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/songs/baby-facts/video/index.html?render=1&w=960&h=540&layout=${layout}&data=${data}`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
    await page.waitForFunction('window.__render && (window.__render.ready || window.__render.error)');
    const r = await page.evaluate(async (layout) => {
      const { layoutLegend, LEGEND } = await import('/songs/baby-facts/video/legend.js');
      const { layoutRuns, captionRuns, CAPTION } = await import('/songs/baby-facts/video/caption.js');
      const tall = layout === '9x16';
      const LO = tall ? { ...LEGEND, x: 56, maxW: 968, size: 56, minSize: 44, rows: 6 } : LEGEND;
      const CO = tall ? { ...CAPTION, x: 56, maxW: 968, size: 38, minSize: 30, rows: 2 } : CAPTION;
      const ctx = document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d');
      const env = window.__render.env;
      const out = { legend: [], caption: [], maxRows: 0, minSize: 99, capMin: 99 };
      for (const l of env.L.lines) {
        const L = layoutLegend(ctx, l, LO);
        out.maxRows = Math.max(out.maxRows, L.rows.length); out.minSize = Math.min(out.minSize, L.size);
        const widths = L.rows.map(([i, j]) => L.ws.slice(i, j).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) + L.space * (j - i - 1));
        if (L.rows.length > LO.rows || L.size < LO.minSize - 1e-6 || Math.max(...widths) > LO.maxW + 0.5) out.legend.push(`${l.text.slice(0, 60)} rows ${L.rows.length} size ${L.size} w ${Math.max(...widths).toFixed(0)}`);
      }
      for (const f of env.story.figs) {
        const C = layoutRuns(ctx, captionRuns(f), CO);
        out.capMin = Math.min(out.capMin, C.size);
        if (C.rows.length > CO.rows) out.caption.push(`Fig. ${f.num} ${f.key}: rows ${C.rows.length} at ${C.size}`);
      }
      return out;
    }, layout);
    console.log(`${data.padEnd(16)} ${layout}: legend max rows ${r.maxRows}, min size ${r.minSize}; caption min size ${r.capMin}${r.legend.length ? '\n  LEGEND OVERFLOW ' + r.legend.join('\n  ') : ''}${r.caption.length ? '\n  CAPTION OVERFLOW ' + r.caption.join('\n  ') : ''}`);
    bad += r.legend.length + r.caption.length;
    await page.close();
  }
} finally { await browser.close(); srv.close(); }
process.exit(bad ? 1 : 0);
EOF

// TEMPORARY ink-adult test (reverted after the still)
export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const A = C.area;
    const ground = A.y + A.h - 40;
    const P = C.P;
    // something behind, to see the paper occlude it
    C.pen('bg', { width: 2 }).hatch([A.x + 40, A.y + 260, A.w - 80, 200], { gap: 14 });
    C.adult({ x: 300, y: ground, u: 40, pose: 'stand', eyes: 'open', k: 1 });
    C.adult({ x: 760, y: ground, u: 40, pose: 'sit', eyes: 'closed', mouth: 'o', k: 2 });
    C.adult({ x: 1120, y: ground, u: 14, pose: 'stand', eyes: 'tired', k: 3 });
    C.adult({ x: 1320, y: ground, u: 14, pose: 'sit', eyes: 'open', look: [0.6, 0.4], k: 4 });
    C.adult({ x: 1600, y: ground, u: 26, pose: 'stand', eyes: 'open', p: 0.5, k: 5 });
    C.text('stand u40 open / sit u40 closed o / stand u14 tired / sit u14 look / stand u26 p=0.5', A.x + 20, A.y + 40, { size: 24, mono: true, color: P.ink2 });
  },
};

What's built:

  • Shared characters: baby Clawd and adult Clawd moved to shared/clawd/, with re-exports left in the 2am song. The EEG cap, the phone-free arm and the adult ink style are all opt-in.
  • Ink pen: the hand-wobble pen is in shared/renderer/core/ink.js.
  • Legend: the lyric legend never exceeds two rows; the smallest size it uses is 37 px. Every caption fits on one row at full size. I checked this for every line in both data sets.
  • Colour: in the frames I extracted, the orange baby is the only saturated colour.
  • Figure numbers: runA has 29 figures (Lampl is Fig. 7, Hamlin Fig. 19, the final grid Fig. 29). On the radio simulation, Hirsh-Pasek and two bridge myths drop out, the numbers close up to 26, and Hamlin's "(but see Fig. N)" follows the change.
  • Tools: node songs/baby-facts/video/tools/figures.mjs [--data data-radio-sim] prints the whole figure table.

Scene interface (for briefing the two agents):

  • One file per lab. Each lab is scenes/labs/<key>.js exporting { draw(ctx, C) }. The key is also its plate id, so the preview's ?plate=<key> jumps straight to it. Every file and registry entry already exists, so an agent edits only its own lab files.
  • Frozen files: kit.js, plate.js, labs.js, edit.js, story.js, song.js, the caption, legend and HUD files, the section plates and shared/. Caption titles and citations live in labs.js; changes go through you.
  • What C provides:
    • time: t, start, end, lt, p, and inP for the draw-in
    • the lab's lines that are actually in the take, the current line, its index and its words
    • fig (number, title, citation) and figNum(key)
    • lookups that never throw: has, lineFor, word, wordTime (an absent word means "never"), since, wseg
    • the beat grid and onBeat
    • the drawing area (16:9 page coordinates in every layout), the palette, and helpers for the pen, text, stamps, the baby, ink copies, the ink adult, axes and the placeholder
  • Who draws what: the compositor draws the paper, caption, legend and HUD, and erases the previous figure. A lab draws only inside its figure area.
  • Absent lines: a lab with none of its lines in the take gets no window and no number. Inside a lab, has()/lineFor() gate optional beats like Grant or "front page". A line that matches no rule continues the previous lab, or gets an "unmatched" placeholder, and the render log says so.
  • Proposed split:
    • Agent A: feldman, abraham, tronick, barburoth, lampl, adolph, hirshpasek, saffran.
    • Agent B: jarvis, fenton, blumberg, cowgill, hespos, kanglee, hamlin, warneken.
    • The full-sheet-only labs (spencer, dominici, walle, saxe, carpenter) can stay placeholders.
    • Neither should regenerate sheets/plates.png. Both should stage explicit paths, because another agent is committing to main right now (the songs/continued work).
  • The README has the full contract, the pen and baby APIs, and the rules of the page.

What still looks off:

  • All verse figures are the same placeholder; only the caption, legend and panel letter differ.
  • The 16 ink copies in the 4x4 grid are clean vector outlines, the least hand-drawn ink on the page.
  • Chorus 2 sings none of the station words, so the cart races through every station in about 1.5 s.
  • The drop's brain outline is lumpy, and the synapse lines read as X marks.
  • The bridge covers are simple sketches, and some stamps overlap cover text (Mozart, the 30,000,000 counter).
  • The cap's wire is a single thin ribbon, and the small copies' electrodes look busy.
  • 9:16 works but is plain. The title-page baby there is clipped at a line you can't see.
  • The bench's swab and cap stand are rudimentary.
  • The loop tail is only 0.88 s, so --check can compare just 2 of its frames.

Departures from the treatment:

  • The final chorus is Fig. 29, not Fig. 31.
  • During the break the lyric legend is hidden ("Here's what did." isn't shown), because the treatment lists only paper, rule and HUD for it.
  • The mono font is Menlo: SF Mono only ships inside Terminal.app here, not system-wide.
  • The heartbeat trace follows the beat grid. The take has no heartbeat to lock to: in the separated stems, drums and bass are silent until about 15 s.
  • I added a small "METHODS · A STUDY OF ONE" line above the title.

Everything is committed, in four commits (a7dd9a0, f751c38, a21b2da, 4935760).

The coordinator sent a message while you were working:
Spine polish pass, please, while two other agents fill the lab figures in scenes/labs/ (they own only their lab files; you own everything else in songs/baby-facts/video/ and shared/). Stage explicit paths when committing; a third agent is also committing to main in songs/continued/.

I looked at frames at 8, 55, 100, 185, 230 and 250 s of your draft. The page, type, HUD, cover stamp and the grid payoff all read well. Fix these:

  1. Chorus 2 sings none of the station words, so the cart races the bench in 1.5 s. When fewer than three station words are present in a chorus, pace the cart across the whole chorus window on the beat grid instead (one station per two beats), and only snap to words when they exist.
  2. The 16 ink copies in the 4x4 grid are the cleanest vectors on the page. Draw them with the pen (same wobble as everything else), and let the orange original keep its clean edges so it reads as the real one.
  3. The drop: give the brain a proper profile (frontal, temporal lobe notch, cerebellum, stem), draw it with the pen, and make the synapses small branching strokes that grow and are then erased on "pruned", not X marks. The cell division into the brain should still be visible as the packing.
  4. Bridge covers: no stamp may overlap cover text. Place each stamp in a clear corner of its cover and keep the counter (30,000,000) clear of the n = 42 stamp. Covers can stay simple, but give each one or two more ink details so they read as objects (CD jewel case hinge, DVD case spine, calendar rings).
  5. The break: show "Here's what did." in the legend. It is the hinge line of the song and must be on screen.
  6. The bench props: a recognisable cotton swab (stick with a wad at each end) and a cap on a small stand; one or two strokes more each.
  7. The loop tail is 0.88 s, which is fine for this take, but make the title plate's loop logic robust to takes with a longer or shorter tail (clamp like the 2am video's story.js does).

Keep everything else as it is. V = '/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/'
def edit(path, pairs):
    s = open(path).read()
    for old, new in pairs:
        assert s.count(old) == 1, (path, old[:70], s.count(old))
        s = s.replace(old, new)
    open(path, 'w').write(s)

edit(V + 'edit.js', [("""  // ---- the loop tail: outro frames that are intro frames -------------------------------
  // After the last outro line has left the legend, the song shows only paper and the HUD;
  // so does the intro before its title draws in. The tail repeats the intro's head frame by
  // frame. Its shift is a whole number of beats (the intro's beat-locked heartbeat trace
  // lands on the tail's beats), rounded to frames in story.js.

  let loop = null;

    let m = Math.ceil(B.beatIndex(clear + b0) - 1e-9);
    let shift = B.beatTime(m) - b0;
    if (!(shift < B.duration - 0.2)) shift = clear;           // no room for a beat-aligned tail
    if (shift < B.duration - 0.2) {

  // After the last outro line has left the legend, the song shows only paper and the HUD;
  // so does the intro's head, before its first lyric and its title arrive (introHead). The
  // tail repeats that head frame by frame, so it may be no longer than the head: it starts
  // at the later of "the outro is clear" and "duration - head". Its shift is a whole number
  // of beats when one fits (the intro's beat-locked heartbeat trace then lands on the tail's
  // beats), rounded to frames in story.js, which also clamps the mapped time into the head.
  const outroG = groups.filter((g) => g.kind === 'outro').at(-1);
  const introFirst = title?.lines?.[0]?.words?.[0]?.start ?? title?.lines?.[0]?.start ?? 1.0;
  const introHead = Math.max(0.2, introFirst - LEGEND_LEAD - 0.05);
  let loop = null;
  if (outroG) {
    const D = B.duration, latest = D - 0.1;
    const clear = outroG.lines.at(-1).end + LEGEND_HOLD + LEGEND_FADE + 0.05;
    const need = Math.max(clear, D - introHead);
    const b0 = B.beatTime(0);
    const m = Math.ceil(B.beatIndex(need + b0) - 1e-9);
    let shift = B.beatTime(m) - b0;
    if (!(shift <= latest)) shift = need;                    // no beat fits: the earliest start that works

      shift = latest;

  function loopTime(t) {
    if (!tail || t < tail.start) return t;
    return Math.max(intro.start, (Math.round(t * fps) - shiftFrames) / fps);
  }""", """  // A tail frame k shows intro frame k - shift, clamped (as the 2am video's story.js does) to
  // the intro's start and to the last frame of its HUD-only head, so a take whose tail comes
  // out longer than the head holds the head's last frame instead of showing the title.
  const shiftFrames = tail ? Math.round(tail.start * fps) - Math.round(intro.start * fps) : 0;
  const headLastFrame = Math.max(Math.round(intro.start * fps), Math.floor(plan.introHead * fps));
  function loopTime(t) {
    if (!tail || t < tail.start) return t;
    const k = Math.round(t * fps) - shiftFrames;
    return Math.min(headLastFrame, Math.max(Math.round(intro.start * fps), k)) / fps;
  }"""),

// HUD at n = ? and the legend with the hinge line (the compositor draws those). One beat of nothing.""")])

// ---- timing ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Everything time-like for one chorus window, from its lines (pure; cheap enough per frame). */
function plan(C) {
  const lines = C.lines;
  const ri = lines.findIndex(isReplicateLine);
  const bench = ri < 0 ? lines : lines.slice(0, ri), repl = ri < 0 ? [] : lines.slice(ri);
  const benchStart = C.start, replStart = repl.length ? repl[0].start - 0.2 : Infinity;
  const benchEnd = Math.min(replStart, C.end);
  const inBench = (m) => { for (let k = 0; k < bench.length; k++) { const w = C.word(m, { line: lines.indexOf(bench[k]) }); if (w) return w.start; } return null; };
  // station arrival times: on the word, else interpolated
  const names = ['scanner', 'slope', 'cap', 'swab', 'seven', 'ten'];
  const tw = names.map((n) => inBench(n));
  const first = bench[0] ? bench[0].start : benchStart;
  const lo = Math.min(benchEnd - 0.5, (bench[0] ? first + Math.min(bench[0].end - first, 2.4) * 0.35 : benchStart + 0.6));
  const hi = benchEnd - 0.35;
  const known = tw.map((x, i) => (x != null ? [i, x] : null)).filter(Boolean);
  const times = tw.map((x, i) => {
    if (x != null) return x;
    const a = [...known].reverse().find(([j]) => j < i), b = known.find(([j]) => j > i);
    const ia = a ? a[0] : -1, ta = a ? a[1] : lo, ib = b ? b[0] : names.length, tb = b ? b[1] : hi;
    return lerp(ta, tb, (i - ia) / (ib - ia));
  });
  // labels on the first line
  const l0 = bench[0] ? lines.indexOf(bench[0]) : -1;
  const lab = (m) => (l0 >= 0 ? C.word(m, { line: l0 }) : null);
  const labels = [['subject', lab('subject')], ['sample', lab('sample')], ['n = 1', lab('one')]];
  // divisions
  const reps = [];
  for (const l of repl) for (const w of l.words) if (/^\(?replicate/i.test(w.w)) reps.push(w.start);
  let div;
  if (C.plate === 'final' || reps.length < 4) {
    const t1 = reps.length ? reps[0] : (isFinite(replStart) ? replStart + 0.6 : Infinity);
    div = [0, 1, 2, 3].map((k) => t1 + (k * C.B.beatDur) / 2);
  } else div = reps.slice(0, 4);
  // final: the rows turn orange
  let rows = null;
  if (C.plate === 'final') {
    const did = [];
    // the start of each "(I did.)": its first backing word
    for (const l of repl) { const b = l.words.find((w) => w.backing); if (b && /did/i.test(l.words.filter((w) => w.backing).map((w) => w.w).join(' '))) did.push(b.start); }
    const yearL = repl.find((l) => /^fifteen babies/.test(l.n)), askL = repl.find((l) => /ask me in a year/.test(l.n));
    const pick = (l, m) => (l ? (l.words.find((w) => C.L.norm(w.w) === m)?.start ?? l.start) : null);
    const trig = [did[0] ?? null, did[1] ?? null, pick(yearL, 'year'), pick(askL, 'ask')];
    const a = div[3] + 0.8, b = C.end - 1.2;
    rows = trig.map((x, i) => (x != null ? x : lerp(a, b, i / 3)));
    for (let i = 1; i < 4; i++) rows[i] = Math.max(rows[i], rows[i - 1] + 0.2);
  }
  return { bench, repl, benchStart, benchEnd, replStart, times, labels, div, rows };
}

// cart x at t: arrives at each station on its time, dwells, moves on
function cartX(t, T) {
  const xs = [ST.scanner, ST.slope, ST.cap, ST.swab, ST.ruler, ST.ruler];
  let x = CART.start, prevT = T.times[0] - Math.min(0.9, Math.max(0.3, T.times[0] - T.benchStart - 0.2));
  let prevX = CART.start;
  for (let i = 0; i < xs.length; i++) {
    const ti = T.times[i];
    if (t >= ti) { x = xs[i]; prevX = xs[i]; prevT = ti; continue; }
    const leave = i === 0 ? prevT : Math.min(prevT + 0.22, ti - 0.12);
    return lerp(prevX, xs[i], ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, leave, ti)));
  }
  return x;
}

const rampY = (x) => { const d = Math.abs(x - ST.slope); return d < 92 ? -70 * (1 - d / 92) : 0; };

// Chorus timing: when the cart reaches each bench station, when the replication starts,
// when the cells divide and (final chorus) when each row of the grid turns orange. One pure
// function of the chorus's lines and the beat grid, shared by the edit (the replication
// figure's caption switches when the replication starts) and the chorus plate.
//
// Stations: scanner, slope, cap, swab, ruler at "seven", the ruler again at "ten".
//   - three or more station words sung: the cart arrives on each word; a missing station is
//     interpolated between its neighbours (chorus 1, the final chorus)
//   - fewer than three (chorus 2 sings only "I'm the subject..."): the cart is paced on the
//     beat grid, one station every two beats from the window start ("ten" one beat after
//     "seven"), still snapping to any station word that is sung; the replication waits for
//     the cart, so the division lands later in the window
// Divisions: the four "replicate" words (main and backing) sung after the replication has
// started; the final chorus, or fewer than four, divide on half beats from the first one.

import { lerp } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { norm } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/lyrics.js';
import { isReplicateLine } from './labs.js';

export const STATIONS = ['scanner', 'slope', 'cap', 'swab', 'seven', 'ten'];

function wordIn(lines, m) {
  const mm = norm(m);
  for (const test of [(w) => norm(w.w) === mm, (w) => norm(w.w).includes(mm)]) for (const l of lines) for (const w of l.words) if (test(w)) return w;
  return null;
}

export function chorusTiming(lines, B, { start, end, final = false }) {
  const ri = lines.findIndex(isReplicateLine);
  const bench = ri < 0 ? lines : lines.slice(0, ri), repl = ri < 0 ? [] : lines.slice(ri);
  const benchStart = start;
  const replLine = repl.length ? repl[0].start - 0.2 : Infinity;
  const tw = STATIONS.map((n) => wordIn(bench, n)?.start ?? null);
  const sung = tw.filter((x) => x != null).length;
  let times, paced = false;
  if (sung >= 3) {
    const benchEnd = Math.min(replLine, end);
    const first = bench[0] ? bench[0].start : benchStart;
    const lo = Math.min(benchEnd - 0.5, (bench[0] ? first + Math.min(bench[0].end - first, 2.4) * 0.35 : benchStart + 0.6));
    const hi = benchEnd - 0.35;
    const known = tw.map((x, i) => (x != null ? [i, x] : null)).filter(Boolean);
    times = tw.map((x, i) => {
      if (x != null) return x;
      const a = [...known].reverse().find(([j]) => j < i), b = known.find(([j]) => j > i);
      const ia = a ? a[0] : -1, ta = a ? a[1] : lo, ib = b ? b[0] : STATIONS.length, tb = b ? b[1] : hi;
      return lerp(ta, tb, (i - ia) / (ib - ia));
    });
  } else {
    // on the beat grid: one station per two beats ("ten" one beat after "seven")
    paced = true;
    times = [];
    let prev = start;
    STATIONS.forEach((n, i) => {
      const step = i === 5 ? 1 : 2;
      const onGrid = B.beatTime(Math.ceil(B.beatIndex(prev) - 1e-6) + step);
      const w = tw[i];
      const t = w != null && w > prev + 0.3 ? w : onGrid;
      times.push(t);
      prev = t;
    });
  }
  const benchEnd = paced ? Math.min(end, Math.max(replLine, times[5] + B.beatDur * 0.5)) : Math.min(replLine, end);
  const replStart = repl.length ? Math.max(replLine, paced ? times[5] + B.beatDur * 0.5 : -Infinity) : Infinity;
  // labels on the first bench line
  const l0 = bench[0] ? [bench[0]] : [];
  const labels = [['subject', wordIn(l0, 'subject')], ['sample', wordIn(l0, 'sample')], ['n = 1', wordIn(l0, 'one')]];
  // divisions
  const reps = [];
  for (const l of repl) for (const w of l.words) if (/^\(?replicate/i.test(w.w) && w.start >= replStart) reps.push(w.start);
  let div;
  if (final || reps.length < 4) {
    const t1 = reps.length ? reps[0] : (isFinite(replStart) ? replStart + 0.6 : Infinity);
    div = [0, 1, 2, 3].map((k) => t1 + (k * B.beatDur) / 2);
  } else div = reps.slice(0, 4);
  // final: the rows turn orange on each "(I did.)", "One year" and "Ask me in a year"
  let rows = null;
  if (final) {
    const did = [];
    for (const l of repl) { const b = l.words.find((w) => w.backing); if (b && /did/i.test(l.words.filter((w) => w.backing).map((w) => w.w).join(' '))) did.push(b.start); }
    const yearL = repl.find((l) => /^fifteen babies/.test(l.n)), askL = repl.find((l) => /ask me in a year/.test(l.n));
    const pick = (l, m) => (l ? (l.words.find((w) => norm(w.w) === m)?.start ?? l.start) : null);
    const trig = [did[0] ?? null, did[1] ?? null, pick(yearL, 'year'), pick(askL, 'ask')];
    const a = div[3] + 0.8, b = end - 1.2;
    rows = trig.map((x, i) => (x != null ? x : lerp(a, b, i / 3)));
    for (let i = 1; i < 4; i++) rows[i] = Math.max(rows[i], rows[i - 1] + 0.2);
  }
  return { bench, repl, benchStart, benchEnd, replStart, times, paced, sung, labels, div, rows };
}

cat >> /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/clawd/baby.js <<'EOF'

// ---- baby Clawd as ink line art (opt-in; the baby-facts video's ink copies) ------------------
//
// drawInkBaby(ctx, o): the same silhouette drawn with a wobble pen on paper: the body, legs,
// nubs, curl and EEG cap outlined by o.pen (a makePen() pen, required: give each copy its own
// pen.sub(key) so copies do not wobble alike), filled with o.paper so it hides what is
// behind, a hatched band low on the body where the clay is darker, ink eyes with a paper
// glint, a pen mouth. Options: x, y, r, tilt, eyes ('open' | 'closed' | 'half'), open, look,
// mouth ('line' | 'smile' | 'o' | 'none'), free / other ({ mode: 'down' | 'up' | 'wave',
// angle }), cap (null for none; { wires: false } for no ribbon, { to } for its end), ink,
// paper, alpha.

import { makePen } from '../renderer/core/ink.js';

function quadPts(p0, c, p1, n = 8) {
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 1; i <= n; i++) { const t = i / n, u = 1 - t; out.push([u * u * p0[0] + 2 * u * t * c[0] + t * t * p1[0], u * u * p0[1] + 2 * u * t * c[1] + t * t * p1[1]]); }
  return out;
}
function bodyPoly(r) {
  const { l, r: rt, t, b, rad, bulge } = G0.body;
  const L = l * r, R = rt * r, T = t * r, B = b * r, k = rad * r, q = bulge * r;
  let cur = [L + k, T];
  const pts = [cur];
  const q2 = (c, p) => { pts.push(...quadPts(cur, c, p)); cur = p; };
  q2([0, T - q], [R - k, T]); q2([R, T], [R, T + k]); q2([R + q, (T + B) / 2], [R, B - k]); q2([R, B], [R - k, B]);
  q2([0, B + q], [L + k, B]); q2([L, B], [L, B - k]); q2([L - q, (T + B) / 2], [L, T + k]); q2([L, T], [L + k, T]);
  return pts;
}
/** The part of a closed polygon below the horizontal line y = y0 (one half-plane clip). */
function below(poly, y0) {
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < poly.length; i++) {
    const a = poly[i], b = poly[(i + 1) % poly.length];
    const ina = a[1] >= y0, inb = b[1] >= y0;
    if (ina) out.push(a);
    if (ina !== inb) { const f = (y0 - a[1]) / (b[1] - a[1]); out.push([a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * f, y0]); }
  }
  return out;
}
const fillPoly = (ctx, pts, color) => { ctx.beginPath(); pts.forEach((p, i) => (i ? ctx.lineTo(p[0], p[1]) : ctx.moveTo(p[0], p[1]))); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.fill(); };

/** A nub outline from root a to tip b (width t0 at the root, t1 at the tip), open at the root. */
function nubPts(a, b, t0, t1) {
  const dx = b[0] - a[0], dy = b[1] - a[1], d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
  const nx = -dy / d, ny = dx / d, ux = dx / d, uy = dy / d;
  const h0 = t0 / 2, h1 = t1 / 2;
  const pts = [[a[0] + nx * h0, a[1] + ny * h0], [b[0] + nx * h1 - ux * h1, b[1] + ny * h1 - uy * h1]];
  for (let i = 1; i < 8; i++) { const an = Math.PI / 2 - (i / 8) * Math.PI; pts.push([b[0] - ux * h1 + (ux * Math.cos(an) + nx * Math.sin(an)) * h1, b[1] - uy * h1 + (uy * Math.cos(an) + ny * Math.sin(an)) * h1]); }
  pts.push([b[0] - nx * h1 - ux * h1, b[1] - ny * h1 - uy * h1], [a[0] - nx * h0, a[1] - ny * h0]);
  return pts;
}

export function drawInkBaby(ctx, o) {
  const r = o.r, ink = o.ink || '#1d1a17', paper = o.paper || '#f4efe3';
  const pen = (o.pen || makePen(ctx, 'ink-baby', { color: ink })).with({ color: ink, width: clamp(r * 0.042, 1.3, 2.6), wobble: clamp(r * 0.045, 0.7, 2.4), wave: Math.max(40, r * 1.2) });
  const thin = pen.with({ width: clamp(r * 0.026, 0.9, 1.6), wobble: clamp(r * 0.02, 0.4, 1) });
  const fs = o.phoneArm === 'right' ? -1 : 1;
  const arm = G0.arm;
  const root = (k) => [k * arm.x * r, arm.y * r];
  const nubTip = (f, side) => {
    const m = f?.mode || 'down';
    if (m === 'none') return null;
    if (m === 'wave' || m === 'up') {
      const a = f.angle ?? 0, lift = m === 'wave' ? 0.95 + 0.4 * Math.sin(a) : 1.2 + 0.25 * Math.sin(a), len = 0.62 * r;
      return [side * (arm.x * r + Math.cos(lift) * len), arm.y * r - Math.sin(lift) * len];
    }
    if (m === 'hold') return [(f.at?.[0] ?? side * 0.6) * r, (f.at?.[1] ?? 1.2) * r];
    return [side * 1.62 * r, 0.82 * r];
  };
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= o.alpha ?? 1;
  ctx.translate(o.x, o.y);
  ctx.rotate(o.tilt || 0);
  // behind the body: the curl and the legs
  pen.curve([-0.04 * r, -0.92 * r], [-0.08 * r, -1.22 * r], [0.12 * r, -1.42 * r], [0.27 * r, -1.3 * r], { k: 'curl' });
  for (const lx of G0.legs) {
    const x0 = (lx - G0.legW / 2) * r, y0 = (G0.body.b - 0.12) * r, w = G0.legW * r, h = (G0.legH + 0.12) * r;
    ctx.fillStyle = paper; ctx.fillRect(x0, y0, w, h);
    thin.hatch([x0, y0, w, h], { gap: Math.max(3, r * 0.09), k: `legh${lx}` });
    pen.rect(x0, y0, w, h, { over: Math.min(2, r * 0.03), k: `leg${lx}` });
  }
  // the body, its darker band hatched
  const body = bodyPoly(r);
  fillPoly(ctx, body, paper);
  thin.hatch(below(body, 1.08 * r), { gap: Math.max(3.5, r * 0.1), angle: -0.9, k: 'band' });
  pen.stroke([...body, body[1]], { k: 'body' });
  // the cap
  if (o.cap) {
    const cap = o.cap === true ? {} : o.cap;
    const cp = [[-1.31, -0.4], [-1.32, -0.6]].map(([x, y]) => [x * r, y * r]);
    let cur = cp[1];
    const q = (c, p) => { cp.push(...quadPts(cur, [c[0] * r, c[1] * r], [p[0] * r, p[1] * r])); cur = [p[0] * r, p[1] * r]; };
    q([-1.33, -1.06], [-0.78, -1.08]); q([0, -1.22], [0.78, -1.08]); q([1.33, -1.06], [1.32, -0.6]);
    cp.push([1.31 * r, -0.4 * r]); cur = cp.at(-1); q([0, -0.52], [-1.31, -0.4]);
    fillPoly(ctx, cp, paper);
    pen.stroke(cp, { k: 'cap' });
    thin.quad([-1.3 * r, -0.53 * r], [0, -0.66 * r], [1.3 * r, -0.53 * r], { k: 'seam' });
    const E = [[-0.98, -0.7], [-0.5, -0.86], [0, -0.92], [0.5, -0.86], [0.98, -0.7], [-0.28, -1.05], [0.28, -1.05]];
    if (cap.wires !== false) {
      const hub = [0.42 * r, -1.38 * r], to = cap.to || [1.7, -4.6];
      for (const [ex, ey] of E.slice(0, 5)) thin.quad([ex * r, ey * r], [ex * r * 0.75 + hub[0] * 0.25, Math.min(ey * r, hub[1]) - 0.12 * r], hub, { k: `lead${ex}` });
      thin.curve(hub, [hub[0] + (to[0] * r - hub[0]) * 0.3, hub[1] + (to[1] * r - hub[1]) * 0.3 + 0.35 * r], [hub[0] + (to[0] * r - hub[0]) * 0.7, hub[1] + (to[1] * r - hub[1]) * 0.7 + 0.3 * r], [to[0] * r, to[1] * r], { k: 'ribbon' });
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = ink;
    for (const [ex, ey] of E) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex * r, ey * r, 0.065 * r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
  }
  // the nubs
  for (const [f, side, key] of [[o.free, -fs, 'free'], [o.other, fs, 'other']]) {
    const tip = nubTip(f, side);
    if (!tip) continue;
    const pts = nubPts(root(side), tip, arm.t * r, arm.t * r * 1.05);
    fillPoly(ctx, pts, paper);
    pen.stroke(pts, { k: `nub-${key}` });
  }
  // the face: ink eyes with a paper glint (or a closed arc), the mouth in pen
  const E = G0.eye;
  const lx = (o.look?.[0] ?? 0) * 0.07 * r, ly = (o.look?.[1] ?? 0) * 0.06 * r;
  const open = clamp(o.open ?? 1) * (o.eyes === 'half' ? 0.5 : 1);
  for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
    const ex = k * E.dx * r + lx, ey = E.dy * r + ly;
    if (o.eyes === 'closed' || open < 0.18) { pen.quad([ex - 0.15 * r, ey + 0.02 * r], [ex, ey + 0.14 * r], [ex + 0.15 * r, ey + 0.02 * r], { k: `eye${k}` }); continue; }
    const w = E.w * r, h = E.h * r * open;
    rr(ctx, ex - w / 2, ey + E.h * r / 2 - h, w, h, Math.min(E.rad * r, h / 2)); ctx.fillStyle = ink; ctx.fill();
    if (open > 0.4) { ctx.fillStyle = paper; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex - w * 0.17, ey + E.h * r / 2 - h * 0.73, w * 0.22, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
  }
  const my = G0.mouthY * r;
  if (o.mouth === 'smile') pen.quad([-0.12 * r, my - 0.02 * r], [0, my + 0.1 * r], [0.12 * r, my - 0.02 * r], { k: 'mouth' });
  else if (o.mouth === 'o') pen.ellipse(0, my + 0.02 * r, 0.07 * r, 0.08 * r, { k: 'mouth' });
  else if (o.mouth !== 'none') pen.line(-0.08 * r, my, 0.08 * r, my, { k: 'mouth' });
  ctx.restore();
}

def edit(path, pairs):
    s = open(path).read()
    for old, new in pairs:
        assert s.count(old) == 1, (path, old[:70], s.count(old))
        s = s.replace(old, new)
    open(path, 'w').write(s)

export function drawPaperBaby(ctx, o) {

  const capDef = { color: P.cap, shade: P.capShade, electrode: P.ink };

const BC = { x: 830, y: 462, s: 760 };
const OUTLINE = [[-0.5, 0.02], [-0.47, -0.14], [-0.38, -0.27], [-0.24, -0.35], [-0.06, -0.38], [0.12, -0.36], [0.28, -0.29], [0.4, -0.18], [0.48, -0.04],
  [0.49, 0.08], [0.45, 0.16], [0.38, 0.2], [0.43, 0.25], [0.39, 0.31], [0.28, 0.33], [0.19, 0.29], [0.13, 0.31], [0.11, 0.41], [0.05, 0.42], [0.05, 0.3],
  [-0.08, 0.27], [-0.26, 0.24], [-0.38, 0.2], [-0.46, 0.12], [-0.5, 0.02]].map(([x, y]) => [BC.x + x * BC.s, BC.y + y * BC.s]);
const R = [118, 92, 72, 56, 44, 33];

function inside(pt, poly) {
  let c = false;
  for (let i = 0, j = poly.length - 1; i < poly.length; j = i++) {
    const [xi, yi] = poly[i], [xj, yj] = poly[j];
    if ((yi > pt[1]) !== (yj > pt[1]) && pt[0] < ((xj - xi) * (pt[1] - yi)) / (yj - yi) + xi) c = !c;
  }
  return c;
}

/** 32 leaf cells inside the cerebrum, and the division tree above them (centroids). */
const TREE = (() => {
  const cand = [];
  for (let gy = -0.34; gy <= 0.24; gy += 0.075) for (let gx = -0.46; gx <= 0.46; gx += 0.08) {
    const p = [BC.x + (gx + (hash(cand.length, 5) - 0.5) * 0.02) * BC.s, BC.y + gy * BC.s];
    const m = R[5] * 1.05;
    if ([[0, 0], [m, 0], [-m, 0], [0, m], [0, -m]].every(([dx, dy]) => inside([p[0] + dx, p[1] + dy], OUTLINE)) && !(gx > 0.0 && gy > 0.18)) cand.push(p);
  }
  // keep 32, evenly through the candidates
  const leaves = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) leaves.push(cand[Math.floor((i * cand.length) / 32)]);
  const levels = [];
  const build = (pts, depth, idx) => {
    const cx = pts.reduce((s, p) => s + p[0], 0) / pts.length, cy = pts.reduce((s, p) => s + p[1], 0) / pts.length;
    (levels[depth] ||= [])[idx] = [cx, cy];
    if (pts.length === 1) return;
    const xs = pts.map((p) => p[0]), ys = pts.map((p) => p[1]);
    const byX = Math.max(...xs) - Math.min(...xs) >= Math.max(...ys) - Math.min(...ys);
    const s = pts.slice().sort((a, b) => (byX ? a[0] - b[0] : a[1] - b[1]));
    build(s.slice(0, s.length / 2), depth + 1, idx * 2);
    build(s.slice(s.length / 2), depth + 1, idx * 2 + 1);
  };
  build(leaves, 0, 0);
  // synapses: neighbouring leaves
  const links = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < 32; j++) {
    const d = Math.hypot(leaves[i][0] - leaves[j][0], leaves[i][1] - leaves[j][1]);
    if (d < 92) links.push([i, j]);
  }
  return { levels, leaves, links };
})();

export default {

cat > /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/scenes/bridge.js <<'EOF'
// The bridge ("the part you skip"): the page dims (an ink wash, drawn by the compositor), a
// heading on "Now the part you skip", then one journal cover or product per myth, drawn as
// ink line art and stamped on the beat with its sample size (the HUD takes the n as each
// lands, story.js). The baby watches, unimpressed. On "So when you ask me... will it
// replicate?" everything fades to the lighter ink except the HUD, which shuffles the n's.
//
// Each cover has fixed stamp slots in clear corners, away from its type (STAMPS below; the
// stamp texts and their words are MYTHS in labs.js). With globalThis.__bridgeDebug set to an
// array, every frame pushes { key, text: [boxes], stamps: [boxes] } so a check can prove no
// stamp overlaps cover type (it has no effect on the pixels).

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease, hash, TAU } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { P, text, drawPaperBaby, babyStandY, blink, stampSize } from '../kit.js';
import { MYTH } from '../labs.js';

const CX = 880, CY = 470;
/** Stamp slots per cover: [x, y, size] in order of MYTHS[key].stamps. */
const STAMPS = {
  wonderweeks: [[978, 662, 42], [772, 662, 40]],
  mozart: [[880, 632, 32]],
  einstein: [[958, 642, 38]],
  imitation: [[702, 288, 36], [880, 694, 30]],
  helper: [[1052, 732, 36], [1112, 238, 42]],
  thirtymillion: [[880, 652, 34]],
};

/** Type on a cover: drawn like kit.text, its box recorded for the overlap check. */
function T(ctx, boxes, s, x, y, o) {
  const w = text(ctx, s, x, y, o);
  const size = o.size ?? 32;
  const x0 = o.align === 'center' ? x - w / 2 : o.align === 'right' ? x - w : x;
  boxes.push({ s, x: x0, y: y - size * 0.78, w, h: size * 0.98 });
  return w;
}

function cover(ctx, C, key, p, t, f, boxes) {
  const pen = C.pen(`cover-${key}`, { width: 2.6 });
  const thin = pen.with({ width: 1.5 });
  const lineIdx = C.lines.indexOf(f.lines[0]);
  const wt = (m) => { const w = C.word(m, { line: lineIdx }); return w ? w.start : Infinity; };
  const paper = (x, y, w, h) => { ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(p * 1.6); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h); ctx.restore(); };
  if (key === 'wonderweeks') {
    // a wall calendar book: rings along the top of the grid, ten leaps circled
    const w = 400, h = 500, x = CX - w / 2, y = CY - h / 2;
    paper(x, y, w, h);
    pen.rect(x, y, w, h, { p, k: 'book' });
    thin.line(x + 22, y, x + 22, y + h, { p, k: 'spine' });
    T(ctx, boxes, 'THE WONDER', CX + 10, y + 70, { size: 40, align: 'center', track: 0.06, reveal: seg(p, 0.3, 0.8) });
    T(ctx, boxes, 'WEEKS', CX + 10, y + 116, { size: 40, align: 'center', track: 0.06, reveal: seg(p, 0.35, 0.85) });
    const gx = x + 62, gy = y + 170, cw = 42, ch = 44;
    thin.line(gx - 6, gy - 16, gx + 7 * cw + 6, gy - 16, { p, k: 'bar' });
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) pen.with({ width: 1.8 }).ellipse(gx + 18 + i * 52, gy - 16, 6, 12, { p: seg(p, 0.2, 0.6), k: `ring${i}` });
    for (let r = 0; r < 5; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 7; c++) thin.rect(gx + c * cw, gy + r * ch, cw, ch, { p: seg(p, 0.3 + 0.01 * (r * 7 + c), 0.6 + 0.01 * (r * 7 + c)), over: 0, k: `g${r}${c}`, wobble: 0.6 });
    const leaps = [3, 6, 9, 12, 16, 19, 23, 26, 30, 33];
    const shown = Math.floor(10 * seg(t, wt('ten') - 0.1, wt('ten') + 1.2));
    leaps.slice(0, shown).forEach((d, i) => pen.with({ width: 2 }).ellipse(gx + (d % 7) * cw + cw / 2, gy + Math.floor(d / 7) * ch + ch / 2, 19, 17, { k: `leap${i}` }));
  } else if (key === 'mozart') {
    // a CD jewel case with its hinge, the disc sliding out
    const s = 420, x = CX - s / 2, y = CY - s / 2;
    const slide = 120 * ease.outCubic(seg(t, f.start + 0.4, f.start + 1.2));
    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(p * 1.6); ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(CX + slide, CY, 175, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
    pen.circle(CX + slide, CY, 175, { p, k: 'disc' });
    thin.circle(CX + slide, CY, 60, { p, k: 'label' });
    thin.circle(CX + slide, CY, 16, { p, k: 'hole' });
    paper(x, y, s, s);
    pen.rect(x, y, s, s, { p, k: 'case' });
    // the hinge: a strip down the left edge with its two pins
    thin.line(x + 26, y, x + 26, y + s, { p, k: 'hinge' });
    for (const hy of [y + 26, y + s - 56]) thin.rect(x + 6, hy, 14, 30, { p, k: `pin${hy}` });
    thin.rect(x + 40, y + 18, s - 58, s - 36, { p, k: 'inner' });
    T(ctx, boxes, 'MOZART', CX + 12, y + 150, { size: 54, align: 'center', track: 0.08, reveal: seg(p, 0.3, 0.8) });
    T(ctx, boxes, 'for babies', CX + 12, y + 200, { size: 34, style: 'italic', align: 'center', reveal: seg(p, 0.4, 0.9) });
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) thin.spline([[x + 110 + i * 90, y + 300], [x + 130 + i * 90, y + 270], [x + 150 + i * 90, y + 300]], { p: seg(p, 0.5, 1), k: `note${i}` });
  } else if (key === 'einstein') {
    // a DVD case: the spine down the left, a clear sleeve, the ball; the refund receipt prints out beside it
    const w = 320, h = 450, x = CX - w / 2, y = CY - h / 2;
    paper(x, y, w, h);
    pen.rrect(x, y, w, h, 18, { p, k: 'case' });
    thin.line(x + 34, y + 4, x + 34, y + h - 4, { p, k: 'spine' });
    thin.rect(x + 46, y + 18, w - 64, h - 36, { p, k: 'sleeve' });
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(x + 17, y + h / 2); ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 2);
    text(ctx, 'DVD', 0, 8, { size: 20, mono: true, align: 'center', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(p, 0.5, 1) });
    ctx.restore();
    T(ctx, boxes, 'BABY', CX + 16, y + 92, { size: 46, align: 'center', track: 0.1, reveal: seg(p, 0.3, 0.8) });
    T(ctx, boxes, 'EINSTEIN', CX + 16, y + 142, { size: 46, align: 'center', track: 0.1, reveal: seg(p, 0.35, 0.85) });
    pen.circle(CX + 16, CY + 30, 70, { p: seg(p, 0.4, 1), k: 'ball' });
    thin.arc(CX + 16, CY + 30, 70, -0.6, 2.2, { p: seg(p, 0.6, 1), k: 'ballline' });
    const rc = MYTH.einstein.receipt;
    const tr = rc ? wt(rc.word) : Infinity;
    if (t >= tr - 0.05) {
      const len = 230 * ease.outCubic(seg(t, tr - 0.05, tr + 0.9));
      const rx = x - 190, rw = 150, ry = y + 210;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(rx - 4, ry, rw + 8, len + 14); ctx.clip();
      ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.fillRect(rx, ry, rw, 230);
      thin.line(rx, ry, rx, ry + 230, { k: 'rcL' });
      thin.line(rx + rw, ry, rx + rw, ry + 230, { k: 'rcR' });
      const zz = []; for (let i = 0; i <= 10; i++) zz.push([rx + (rw * i) / 10, ry + 230 + (i % 2 ? 7 : 0)]);
      thin.poly(zz, { k: 'rcZ' });
      rc.lines.forEach((s, i) => T(ctx, boxes, s, rx + rw / 2, ry + 44 + i * 44, { size: i === 2 ? 30 : 22, mono: true, align: 'center', weight: i === 2 ? 700 : 400 }));
      ctx.restore();
      thin.line(rx - 14, ry, rx + rw + 14, ry, { k: 'slot' });
    }
  } else if (key === 'imitation') {
    // a mounted photo (corner mounts, a strip of tape) with an ink sketch of a newborn, tongue out
    const w = 440, h = 380, x = CX - w / 2, y = CY - h / 2;
    paper(x, y, w, h);
    pen.rect(x, y, w, h, { p, k: 'photo' });
    thin.rect(x + 20, y + 20, w - 40, h - 80, { p, k: 'print' });
    for (const [cx, cy, sx, sy] of [[x + 20, y + 20, 1, 1], [x + w - 20, y + 20, -1, 1], [x + 20, y + h - 60, 1, -1], [x + w - 20, y + h - 60, -1, -1]]) thin.poly([[cx, cy + sy * 26], [cx, cy], [cx + sx * 26, cy]], { p, close: true, k: `mount${cx}${cy}` });
    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= 0.6 * p; ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.translate(CX + 120, y - 2); ctx.rotate(0.12); ctx.fillRect(-50, -14, 100, 28); ctx.restore();
    pen.with({ width: 1.2 }).rect(CX + 70, y - 16, 100, 28, { p, k: 'tape' });
    pen.ellipse(CX, CY - 20, 110, 120, { p: seg(p, 0.2, 0.8), k: 'face' });
    pen.line(CX - 50, CY - 50, CX - 22, CY - 50, { p: seg(p, 0.5, 0.9), k: 'eyeL' });
    pen.line(CX + 22, CY - 50, CX + 50, CY - 50, { p: seg(p, 0.5, 0.9), k: 'eyeR' });
    pen.ellipse(CX, CY + 18, 34, 14, { p: seg(p, 0.55, 0.95), k: 'mouth' });
    pen.with({ width: 2.2 }).spline([[CX - 18, CY + 24], [CX, CY + 66], [CX + 18, CY + 24]], { p: seg(p, 0.6, 1), k: 'tongue' });
    T(ctx, boxes, '1977', x + 30, y + h - 22, { size: 26, mono: true, color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(p, 0.6, 1) });
  } else if (key === 'helper') {
    // a journal: bound spine, masthead, the hill (later 37 hills), a barcode; the coin flips beside it
    const w = 400, h = 520, x = CX - w / 2, y = CY - h / 2;
    paper(x, y, w, h);
    pen.rect(x, y, w, h, { p, k: 'journal' });
    thin.line(x + 14, y, x + 14, y + h, { p, k: 'bind' });
    T(ctx, boxes, 'Nature', CX + 6, y + 100, { size: 82, weight: 700, style: 'italic', align: 'center', reveal: seg(p, 0.3, 0.8) });
    thin.line(x + 26, y + 128, x + w - 26, y + 128, { p, k: 'mast' });
    // a barcode, bottom left
    thin.rect(x + 30, y + h - 66, 92, 44, { p, k: 'bar' });
    for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) if (hash(i, 9) > 0.35) thin.line(x + 38 + i * 5.6, y + h - 60, x + 38 + i * 5.6, y + h - 30, { p, k: `bc${i}`, wobble: 0.2 });
    const many = seg(t, wt('manybabies'), wt('manybabies') + 1.6);
    if (many <= 0) {
      const hx = CX, hy = y + 400;
      pen.spline([[x + 40, hy], [hx - 40, hy - 150], [hx + 60, hy - 170], [x + w - 40, hy]], { p: seg(p, 0.4, 0.9), k: 'hill' });
      pen.circle(hx - 30, hy - 190, 20, { p: seg(p, 0.6, 1), k: 'round' });
      pen.rect(hx + 50, hy - 210, 32, 32, { p: seg(p, 0.6, 1), k: 'square' });
      pen.poly([[hx - 110, hy - 70], [hx - 88, hy - 110], [hx - 66, hy - 70]], { p: seg(p, 0.6, 1), close: true, k: 'tri' });
    } else {
      // thirty-seven tiny hills (ManyBabies 4, 37 labs), in rows of eight
      const n = Math.floor(37 * many);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const c = i % 8, r = Math.floor(i / 8);
        const sx = x + 52 + c * 42, sy = y + 196 + r * 58;
        thin.spline([[sx - 16, sy], [sx - 4, sy - 20], [sx + 7, sy - 22], [sx + 18, sy]], { k: `h${i}` });
      }
    }
    const tc = wt('coin');
    if (t >= tc - 0.1) {
      const k = t - tc;
      const fy = CY - 130 - 80 * Math.sin(Math.min(Math.PI, k * 3)), sq = Math.abs(Math.cos(k * 11));
      ctx.fillStyle = P.paper; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(CX - 330, fy, 46, 46 * sq + 3, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      pen.ellipse(CX - 330, fy, 46, 46 * sq + 3, { k: 'coin' });
    }
  } else if (key === 'thirtymillion') {
    // a word counter: a display window with its digits, four screws, a dial
    const w = 640, h = 220, x = CX - w / 2, y = CY - h / 2;
    paper(x, y, w, h);
    pen.rrect(x, y, w, h, 14, { p, k: 'counter' });
    thin.rect(x + 40, y + 40, w - 80, 104, { p, k: 'window' });
    for (const [sx, sy] of [[x + 16, y + 16], [x + w - 16, y + 16], [x + 16, y + h - 16], [x + w - 16, y + h - 16]]) {
      thin.circle(sx, sy, 6, { p, k: `screw${sx}${sy}` });
      thin.line(sx - 4, sy + 3, sx + 4, sy - 3, { p, k: `slot${sx}${sy}` });
    }
    pen.circle(x + w - 30, y + h - 52, 14, { p, k: 'dial' });
    const v = Math.round(30000000 * ease.outCubic(seg(t, f.start + 0.2, f.start + 1.6)));
    T(ctx, boxes, v.toLocaleString('en-US'), CX, y + 124, { size: 80, mono: true, align: 'center', alpha: seg(p, 0.3, 0.8) });
    T(ctx, boxes, 'words', CX, y + h - 26, { size: 30, style: 'italic', align: 'center', color: P.ink2, alpha: seg(p, 0.5, 1) });
  }
}

function stamps(ctx, C, key, f, t, boxes) {
  const m = MYTH[key];
  const lineIdx = C.lines.indexOf(f.lines[0]);
  (m.stamps || []).forEach((s, i) => {
    const w = C.word(s.word, { line: lineIdx });
    const slot = STAMPS[key]?.[i];
    if (!w || !slot) return;
    const [x, y, size] = slot;
    const rot = (hash(i, key.length) - 0.5) * 0.24;
    C.stamp(s.text, x, y, { t0: C.onBeat(w.start + 0.05), size, rot });
    if (boxes) {
      const d = stampSize(s.text, size), c = Math.abs(Math.cos(rot)), sn = Math.abs(Math.sin(rot));
      const bw = d.w * c + d.h * sn, bh = d.w * sn + d.h * c;
      boxes.push({ s: s.text, x: x - bw / 2, y: y - bh / 2, w: bw, h: bh });
    }
  });
}

const hit = (a, b) => a.x < b.x + b.w && b.x < a.x + a.w && a.y < b.y + b.h && b.y < a.y + a.h;

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const t = C.t, st = C.story;
    const ask = st.askLine;
    const dim = ask ? seg(t, ask.start, ask.start + 0.9) : 0;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= 1 - 0.62 * dim;
    // the heading
    const head = C.lines.find((l) => /now the part you skip/.test(l.n));
    const myths = C.figs.filter((f) => f.kind === 'myth' && f.start >= C.start - 1e-6 && f.start < C.end);
    const firstMyth = myths[0]?.start ?? Infinity;
    if (head) {
      const k = ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, firstMyth - 0.1, firstMyth + 0.5));
      const a = ease.outCubic(seg(t, head.start - 0.1, head.start + 0.5));
      const hx = lerp(960, C.area.x + 30, k), hy = lerp(420, C.area.y + 64, k), sz = lerp(70, 38, k);
      text(ctx, 'SUPPLEMENTARY MATERIAL', hx, hy - sz * 1.2, { size: lerp(26, 22, k), mono: true, track: 0.24, align: k > 0.5 ? 'left' : 'center', color: P.ink2, alpha: a });
      text(ctx, 'The part you skip', hx, hy, { size: sz, style: 'italic', align: k > 0.5 ? 'left' : 'center', reveal: a });
    }
    // the current myth's cover, the previous one erasing
    let cur = null;
    for (const f of myths) if (f.start <= t) cur = f;
    if (cur) {
      const i = myths.indexOf(cur);
      const prev = myths[i - 1];
      if (prev && t < cur.start + 0.35) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.globalAlpha *= 1 - seg(t, cur.start, cur.start + 0.35);
        cover(ctx, C, prev.key, 1, cur.start, prev, []);
        stamps(ctx, C, prev.key, prev, cur.start, null);
        ctx.restore();
      }
      const p = ease.outCubic(seg(t, cur.start + (prev ? 0.2 : 0), cur.start + (prev ? 0.2 : 0) + 0.9));
      const tb = [], sb = [];
      cover(ctx, C, cur.key, p, t, cur, tb);
      stamps(ctx, C, cur.key, cur, t, sb);
      const dbg = globalThis.__bridgeDebug;
      if (Array.isArray(dbg)) dbg.push({ key: cur.key, t, text: tb, stamps: sb, overlaps: sb.flatMap((s) => tb.filter((b) => hit(s, b)).map((b) => `${s.s} x ${b.s}`)) });
    }
    ctx.restore();
    // the baby, unimpressed (Reviewer 2)
    const r = 62;
    const enter = ease.outCubic(seg(t, C.start + 0.3, C.start + 1.2));
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= enter * (1 - 0.5 * dim);
    drawPaperBaby(ctx, {
      x: 1600, y: babyStandY(C.area.y + C.area.h - 30, r), r, tilt: 0.04,
      eyes: 'half', open: 0.5 * blink(t, 31) + 0.001, look: [-0.8, 0.1], mouth: 'line', brows: null,
      free: { mode: 'down' }, other: { mode: 'down' }, cap: { to: [3.2, -5], sway: t * 0.3 },
    });
    ctx.restore();
  },
};
EOF

import json, os, copy
V = '/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/'
L = json.load(open(V + 'data/lyrics.json')); A = json.load(open(V + 'data/audio.json'))
def shift_outro(lines, dt):
    out = copy.deepcopy(lines)
    for l in out:
        if l['section'] == 'outro':
            l['start'] += dt; l['end'] += dt
            for w in l['words']:
                w['start'] += dt; w['end'] += dt
                if 'syl' in w: w['syl'] = [[a + dt, b + dt] for a, b in w['syl']]
    return out
for name, dt in [('data-looptest-long', -4.0), ('data-looptest-short', 1.6)]:
    os.makedirs(V + name, exist_ok=True)
    json.dump({**L, 'lines': shift_outro(L['lines'], dt)}, open(V + name + '/lyrics.json', 'w'))
    json.dump(A, open(V + name + '/audio.json', 'w'))
print('ok')

## The loop

The song ends on paper and the HUD (`n = 1`), and the intro begins the same way: the title,
the rule and the legend only arrive at "Fifteen babies". So the loop is a short tail window
after the last outro line has left the legend: its frames are the intro's first frames, frame
for frame (`story.loopTime`). The tail's shift is a whole number of beats (268.716 s on runA),
so the intro's beat-locked heartbeat trace restarts on the tail's beats. `--check` compares
tail frames with their intro frames and fails if they differ or the tail is missing.

## The scene interface (for the second pass)

Each lab is one module, `scenes/labs/<key>.js`, where `<key>` is the lab's key in `labs.js`,
its plate id and its window id. It exports:

```js
export default {
  draw(ctx, C) { /* paint the figure for time C.t inside C.area */ },
};
```

That is all a lab needs. It is registered already: `scenes/labs/index.js` imports every lab
module, `labs.js` holds its rule (the patterns that send lines to it), its caption title,
citation and note, and a one-line brief from the treatment. **To fill a lab, edit only its own
file.** The rest of the spine is frozen for the second pass: `kit.js`, `plate.js`, `edit.js`,
`labs.js`, `story.js`, `song.js`, the overlays and the section plates. A helper only your lab
needs goes in your lab file (or a sibling `scenes/labs/<key>-*.js` you own). If a caption title
or citation must change, say so rather than editing `labs.js`, so two agents never edit one file.

### What C provides

2.5r wide; `babyStandY(groundY, r)` from kit.js places it standing. Expressions as in the 2am
video: `eyes` (`open`, `half`, `closed`, `squeeze`, `wide`, `focus`), `open` (lid 0..1; `blink(t,
seed)` from kit.js), `look`, `mouth` (`line`, `o`, `smile`, `grin`, `press`, `none`),
`mouthOpen`, `brows`, `tears` (pale blue, the only other colour allowed in the baby), `blush`,
`tilt`. `C.inkBaby(o)` draws an ink outline copy (the other data points); `ink: 0..1` fades
between. `C.adult(o)` draws adult Clawd as ink line art (`shared/clawd/adult.js`, `style:
'ink'`): `x, y` (the ground point), `u` (unit; the body is 10u x 6u), `pose` ('stand' | 'sit'),
`eyes`, `mouth`, `look`, `lean`, `arms`, `p` (draw-in).

### Rules of the page


- `song.js`: the compositor (paper, wash, plates with the erase, caption, legend, HUD; the
  9:16 arrangement).
- `edit.js`: windows, figures, numbering, cross-references, the loop tail, `SECTION_PLATES`.
- `labs.js`: the rule table (labs, bridge myths, section figures).
- `story.js`: song-level curves (loop, figure at t, HUD text, heartbeat time, rule).
- `plate.js`: the C contract. `kit.js`: the plate kit (frozen). `page.js`: paper, palette, fonts.
- `legend.js`, `caption.js`, `hud.js`: the three permanent elements.
- `scenes/`: `title.js` (intro and loop tail), `chorus.js` (chorus and final), `bridge.js`,
  `blank.js`, `drop.js`, `outro.js`, `labs/` (one module per lab, all placeholders for now).
- `tools/`: `figures.mjs`, `radio_sim.mjs`.
- Shared with the 2am video: `shared/clawd/baby.js` (baby Clawd, with the opt-in EEG cap and
  phone-free nubs), `shared/clawd/adult.js` (adult Clawd, with the opt-in ink style),
  `shared/renderer/core/ink.js` (the pen). The 2am video re-exports both characters and its
  frames are pixel-identical to before the move.

## Departures and gaps (spine)

- The heartbeat trace follows the beat grid (one ECG complex per beat). audio.json has no
  heartbeat onsets, and this take has no heartbeat to lock to: in the cached htdemucs_ft stems
  (`shared/align/work/replicate-n-1-6b228558-*/stems/`) drums and bass are silent until the beat
  enters at about 15 s, and no stem has a periodic thump at a heart rate over 0-15 s or
  258-269.6 s (the 0.32 s pulses at 265-267 s are a half-beat drum fill). A take with an
  audible heartbeat would want its onsets in audio.json and a drop-in change in `story.js`.
- The title page adds a small "METHODS · A STUDY OF ONE" kicker over the title.
- The break hides the legend, as the treatment lists only paper, rule and HUD.
- The chorus has two figure numbers (bench, replication); that is what puts Lampl at Fig. 7 as
  in the treatment's example, but the final grid is Fig. 29, not 31.
- Bridge covers are simple ink sketches; the treatment's extra beats (the 1998 door, the Georgia
  peach, the receipt printing, the 37 hills multiplying in place) are partly there.
- 9:16 is functional but plain: the 16:9 page scaled into the top half, a taller caption and
  legend below.

All 8 section plates report ok, none MISSING: intro title, chorus bench (two windows), bridge, break, drop, final grid, outro page, outro loop.

What changed:

  1. Chorus pacing. The timing now lives in one shared module, chorus-time.js. A chorus that sings three or more station words still lands each station on its word, so chorus 1 and the final chorus are unchanged. Chorus 2 sings none, so the cart now rides one station every two beats (139.6 to 145.2 s), snapping to a station word if one is sung. The division then lands on "Fifteen babies", and the caption switches to the replication figure when the division starts.
  2. Ink copies. The 16 grid copies are now drawn with the wobble pen: outlined, paper inside, hatched band and legs, each copy keeping its own wobble. The orange original keeps its clean edges.
  3. Drop. The brain now has a real profile: frontal lobe, the notch under the temporal pole, cerebellum with folia, a short stem, then faint sulci, all drawn with the pen. Synapses are small forking strokes that grow on "synapses" and "then more", and about half retract on "pruned". The cells still divide and pack into the shape.
  4. Bridge covers. Each cover has fixed stamp slots in clear corners. A check script confirms no stamp overlaps cover type on either data set, and the n = 42 stamp now sits below the 30,000,000 counter. Each cover got one or two extra details:
    • calendar rings on the Wonder Weeks
    • a hinge with pins on the CD jewel case
    • a spine marked "DVD" on Baby Einstein, plus a printed refund receipt
    • mounts and tape on the photo
    • a binding and barcode on the journal

    • screws and a dial on the counter

      The Mozart stamp now reads "n = 36 (students)" so it fits inside the case.

  5. Break. "Here's what did." is now shown in the legend.
  6. Bench props. The cap sits on a small head-form stand and hops onto the baby's head on "cap". The swab is double-ended, held in a clip on a post, and taps the cheek.
  7. Loop tail. The tail can no longer run past the intro's HUD-only opening. The mapped time is also clamped at both ends, as in the 2am video. I tested two made-up takes: with the outro 4 s earlier the tail stays at 0.88 s, and with it running past the end the tail cuts in 0.1 s before the end with a warning. Both pass --check.

Heads-up for the lab agents: C.baby({ ink: k }) and C.inkBaby now draw the pen copy. Tronick's committed panels a and b already show it, and it looks consistent with the grid. They should pass a stable k per copy so each keeps its own wobble. The ink copy only supports eyes open, half or closed and mouths line, smile or o, so tears, grin and brows are dropped on copies.

Frozen-file list (one new file since last time: chorus-time.js). Lab agents own only scenes/labs/<their keys>.js, plus any scenes/labs/<key>-*.js helpers they create. Everything else is frozen:

  • songs/baby-facts/video/: README.md, index.html, song.js, edit.js, chorus-time.js, labs.js, story.js, plate.js, kit.js, page.js, legend.js, caption.js, hud.js
  • scenes/: title.js, chorus.js, bridge.js, blank.js, drop.js, outro.js, and scenes/labs/index.js
  • data/, data-radio-sim/, tools/, sheets/
  • all of shared/
